SpeechParaling-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Paralinguistic-Aware Speech Generation
Authors: Ruohan Liu, Shukang Yin, Tao Wang, Dong Zhang, Weiji Zhuang, Shuhuai Ren, Ran He, Caifeng Shan, Chaoyou Fu
First: 2026-04-22T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:59:58+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://speechparaling-bench.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Paralinguistic cues are essential for natural human-computer interaction, yet their evaluation in Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) remains limited by coarse feature coverage and the inherent subjectivity of assessment. To address these challenges, we introduce SpeechParaling-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for paralinguistic-aware speech generation. It expands existing coverage from fewer than 50 to over 100 fine-grained features, supported by more than 1,000 English-Chinese parallel speech queries, and is organized into three progressively challenging tasks: fine-grained control, intra-utterance variation, and context-aware adaptation. To enable reliable evaluation, we further develop a pairwise comparison pipeline, in which candidate responses are evaluated against a fixed baseline by an LALM-based judge. By framing evaluation as relative preference rather than absolute scoring, this approach mitigates subjectivity and yields more stable and scalable assessments without costly human annotation. Extensive experiments reveal substantial limitations in current LALMs. Even leading proprietary models struggle with comprehensive static control and dynamic modulation of paralinguistic features, while failure to correctly interpret paralinguistic cues accounts for 43.3% of errors in situational dialogue. These findings underscore the need for more robust paralinguistic modeling toward human-aligned voice assistants.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpeechParaling-Bench：面向语用感知语音生成的综合基准
语用线索对自然人机交互至关重要，但大型音频-语言模型（LALMs）中对其评估仍受限于粗粒度特征覆盖和评估的主观性。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了SpeechParaling-Bench，一个面向语用感知语音生成的综合基准。该基准将现有覆盖从不足50项扩展至超过100项细粒度特征，支持1000+英中平行语音查询，并划分为三个逐步递进的任务：细粒度控制、句内变化与上下文自适应。为实现可靠评估，我们进一步开发了成对比较评估流程，由基于LALM的评估器将候选响应与固定基线进行对比。通过将评估框架转化为相对偏好而非绝对评分，该方法缓解了主观性，实现了更稳定、可扩展的评估，无需昂贵的人工标注。大量实验揭示当前LALMs存在显著局限：即使领先专有模型也难以全面实现静态控制与动态语用特征调制，而语用线索误判导致情境对话中43.3%的错误。这些发现强调了构建更鲁棒语用建模以实现人机对齐语音助手的必要性。
Summary / 总结
To address the limited evaluation of paralinguistic cues in Large Audio-Language Models, the authors introduce SpeechParaling-Bench, a benchmark covering over 100 fine-grained features using 1,000 English-Chinese speech queries, organized into three increasing difficulty tasks. They employ a pairwise comparison pipeline with an LALM-based judge to reduce subjectivity and enable scalable, relative preference-based evaluation. Experiments show that even top proprietary models struggle with static control and dynamic modulation of paralinguistic features, and misinterpretation of such cues causes 43.3% of errors in dialogue contexts, highlighting the need for improved paralinguistic modeling in voice assistants.
为解决大音频语言模型中对语用特征评估不足的问题，作者构建了SpeechParaling-Bench基准，涵盖100多个细粒度特征，使用1000组英汉语音对，分为三类递进任务：精细控制、句内变化与上下文自适应。评估采用基于LALM的配对比较机制，通过相对偏好评分降低主观性，实现可扩展评估。实验表明，当前顶尖模型在静态控制与动态语用特征调节上表现不佳，且语用特征误判导致对话场景中43.3%的错误，凸显语音助手需强化语用建模以更贴近人类交互需求。
DeVI: Physics-based Dexterous Human-Object Interaction via Synthetic Video Imitation
Authors: Hyeonwoo Kim, Jeonghwan Kim, Kyungwon Cho, Hanbyul Joo
First: 2026-04-22T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:59:55+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://snuvclab.github.io/devi/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advances in video generative models enable the synthesis of realistic human-object interaction videos across a wide range of scenarios and object categories, including complex dexterous manipulations that are difficult to capture with motion capture systems. While the rich interaction knowledge embedded in these synthetic videos holds strong potential for motion planning in dexterous robotic manipulation, their limited physical fidelity and purely 2D nature make them difficult to use directly as imitation targets in physics-based character control. We present DeVI (Dexterous Video Imitation), a novel framework that leverages text-conditioned synthetic videos to enable physically plausible dexterous agent control for interacting with unseen target objects. To overcome the imprecision of generative 2D cues, we introduce a hybrid tracking reward that integrates 3D human tracking with robust 2D object tracking. Unlike methods relying on high-quality 3D kinematic demonstrations, DeVI requires only the generated video, enabling zero-shot generalization across diverse objects and interaction types. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DeVI outperforms existing approaches that imitate 3D human-object interaction demonstrations, particularly in modeling dexterous hand-object interactions. We further validate the effectiveness of DeVI in multi-object scenes and text-driven action diversity, showcasing the advantage of using video as an HOI-aware motion planner.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DeVI：基于物理的灵巧人机交互合成视频模仿
近期视频生成模型的进展使得能够合成涵盖多种场景和物体类别的逼真人机交互视频，包括难以通过动作捕捉系统获取的复杂灵巧操作。尽管这些合成视频中蕴含的丰富交互知识对灵巧机器人操作的运动规划具有巨大潜力，但其物理保真度有限且纯二维特性使其难以直接作为基于物理的动画角色控制的模仿目标。我们提出DeVI（灵巧视频模仿），一种新颖框架，利用文本条件合成视频，实现对未见过目标物体的物理合理灵巧代理控制。为克服生成2D提示的不精确性，我们引入混合跟踪奖励机制，结合3D人体跟踪与鲁棒2D物体跟踪。与依赖高质量3D运动示范的方法不同，DeVI仅需生成视频，即可实现跨多样物体和交互类型的零样本泛化。大量实验表明，DeVI在模仿3D人机交互示范的方法中表现更优，尤其在建模灵巧手-物体交互方面。我们进一步验证了DeVI在多物体场景和文本驱动动作多样性中的有效性，展示了视频作为HOI感知运动规划器的优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for physically plausible dexterous manipulation in robotics using synthetic videos, DeVI introduces a framework that leverages text-conditioned synthetic videos to guide physics-based agent control for unseen objects. To address the limitations of 2D video cues, it employs a hybrid tracking reward combining 3D human pose estimation with robust 2D object tracking. Unlike methods requiring precise 3D demonstrations, DeVI only needs generated videos, enabling zero-shot generalization across diverse interactions. Experimental results show DeVI significantly outperforms existing imitation methods in modeling dexterous hand-object interactions, and it also demonstrates strong performance in multi-object scenes and text-driven action diversity.
为实现机器人在物理上合理的灵巧操作，该研究提出DeVI框架，利用文本引导的合成视频指导物理仿真角色与未知物体的交互。为解决2D合成视频精度不足的问题，该方法结合3D人体姿态估计与2D物体跟踪构建混合奖励机制，提升控制精度。与依赖高质量3D演示的方法不同，DeVI仅需合成视频即可实现零样本泛化，适用于多样物体与交互类型。实验表明，DeVI在灵巧手-物交互建模上显著优于现有方法，同时在多物体场景与文本驱动动作多样性方面也表现优异。
Breaking the Assistant Mold: Modeling Behavioral Variation in LLM Based Procedural Character Generation
Authors: Maan Qraitem, Kate Saenko, Bryan A. Plummer
First: 2026-01-06T20:18:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:59:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Procedural content generation has enabled vast virtual worlds through levels, maps, and quests, but large-scale character generation remains underexplored. We identify two alignment-induced biases in existing methods: a positive moral bias, where characters uniformly adopt agreeable stances (e.g. always saying lying is bad), and a helpful assistant bias, where characters invariably answer questions directly (e.g. never refusing or deflecting). While such tendencies suit instruction-following systems, they suppress dramatic tension and yield predictable characters, stemming from maximum likelihood training and assistant fine-tuning. To address this, we introduce PersonaWeaver, a framework that disentangles world-building (roles, demographics) from behavioral-building (moral stances, interactional styles), yielding characters with more diverse reactions and moral stances, as well as second-order diversity in stylistic markers like length, tone, and punctuation. Code: https://github.com/mqraitem/Persona-Weaver
中文标题/摘要
标题：打破助手模式：基于大语言模型的程序化角色生成中的行为多样性建模
程序化内容生成已通过关卡、地图和任务构建了广阔虚拟世界，但大规模角色生成仍属探索不足。我们发现现有方法存在两种由对齐引发的偏差：道德正向偏差（角色普遍采取可接受立场，如始终说“说谎不好”）和助手式偏差（角色总直接回答问题，从不拒绝或回避）。此类倾向虽适合指令跟随系统，却抑制戏剧张力并导致角色可预测，根源在于最大似然训练与助手微调。为此，我们提出PersonaWeaver框架，将世界观构建（角色、人口统计）与行为构建（道德立场、互动风格）解耦，生成反应与道德立场更丰富的角色，同时在长度、语气、标点等风格标记上实现二阶多样性。代码：https://github.com/mqraitem/Persona-Weaver
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of behavioral diversity in large language model-based procedural character generation, the authors identify two alignment-induced biases: a positive moral bias favoring agreeable stances and a helpful assistant bias favoring direct responses. To counteract these, they propose PersonaWeaver, a framework that separates world-building attributes from behavioral attributes, enabling more varied moral positions and interactional styles. Experiments show that PersonaWeaver generates characters with significantly greater behavioral diversity, including varied tone, length, and punctuation, while maintaining coherence and alignment with role context.
为解决大语言模型生成角色时行为单一的问题，本文指出现有方法存在两种对齐偏差——道德正向偏差和助手式回应偏差，导致角色缺乏戏剧张力。为此，作者提出PersonaWeaver框架，将角色世界观属性（如身份、人口统计）与行为属性（如道德立场、互动风格）解耦，从而生成更具多样性和复杂性的角色。实验表明，该框架在道德立场和风格标记（如语气、长度、标点）上显著优于传统基于助手微调的方法。
Parallel-SFT: Improving Zero-Shot Cross-Programming-Language Transfer for Code RL
Authors: Zhaofeng Wu, Shiqi Wang, Boya Peng, Anuj Goyal, Melanie Kambadur, Sebastian Ruder, Yoon Kim, Chloe Bi
First: 2026-04-22T17:58:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:58:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern language models demonstrate impressive coding capabilities in common programming languages (PLs), such as C++ and Python, but their performance in lower-resource PLs is often limited by training data availability. In principle, however, most programming skills are universal across PLs, so the capability acquired in one PL should transfer to others. In this work, we propose the task of zero-shot cross-programming-language transfer for code RL. We find that, for Llama-3.1, RL training for code generation in a source PL fails to improve, and sometimes even degrades, the performance on other target PLs. To address this, we hypothesize that effective RL transfer requires a generalizable SFT initialization before RL. We thus propose **Parallel-SFT**, an SFT strategy that incorporates "parallel programs" -- functionally equivalent code implemented in multiple PLs -- into the data mixture. We demonstrate that this improves transferability: when we subsequently perform RL on our Parallel-SFT model, we observe better generalization to unseen PLs. Analysis of the model internal representations reveals that Parallel-SFT leads to a more functionality-centric latent space, where equivalent programs across PLs are more tightly clustered, which we hypothesize to contribute to the improved transferability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Parallel-SFT：提升代码强化学习中的零样本跨编程语言迁移能力
现代语言模型在常见编程语言（如C++和Python）中展现出卓越的编码能力，但在资源较少的编程语言中的表现常受限于训练数据的可用性。然而，从原则上讲，大多数编程技能在不同编程语言间具有通用性，因此在一种语言中习得的能力应能迁移到其他语言。本文提出零样本跨编程语言迁移任务用于代码强化学习。我们发现，对Llama-3.1模型在源语言中进行代码生成的强化学习训练，不仅未能提升其在其他目标语言上的表现，有时甚至会使其性能下降。为解决此问题，我们假设有效的强化学习迁移需要在强化学习前进行可迁移的监督微调（SFT）初始化。因此，我们提出**Parallel-SFT**，一种将“并行程序”——即在多种编程语言中实现功能等价的代码——纳入数据混合的SFT策略。实验证明，该策略提升了迁移能力：当我们在Parallel-SFT模型上进行后续强化学习时，观察到其对未见过的编程语言具有更好的泛化能力。模型内部表示分析表明，Parallel-SFT构建了一个更以功能为中心的潜在空间，其中不同编程语言中的等价程序更紧密地聚类，我们推测这有助于提升迁移能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that language models trained on common programming languages often underperform in lower-resource languages despite shared coding principles, this work investigates zero-shot cross-language transfer for code reinforcement learning. The authors find that direct RL training on a source language can harm performance on target languages, suggesting the need for a generalizable initialization. To address this, they introduce Parallel-SFT, a supervised fine-tuning method that mixes functionally equivalent code across multiple programming languages during training. Experimental results show that Parallel-SFT significantly improves zero-shot transfer performance to unseen languages, with analysis indicating that the model’s latent space becomes more focused on functional equivalence, enhancing cross-language generalization.
鉴于语言模型在主流编程语言上训练良好，但在资源匮乏的语言上表现受限，本文研究了代码强化学习中的零样本跨语言迁移问题。作者发现，直接在源语言上进行强化学习训练反而会损害目标语言性能，表明需要更好的初始化策略。为此，他们提出了Parallel-SFT方法，通过混合多语言功能等价代码进行监督微调。实验表明，该方法显著提升了模型对未见语言的泛化能力，分析显示其构建了更聚焦功能等价的潜在空间，从而增强了跨语言迁移效果。
PokeVLA: Empowering Pocket-Sized Vision-Language-Action Model with Comprehensive World Knowledge Guidance
Authors: Yupeng Zheng, Xiang Li, Songen Gu, Yuhang Zheng, Shuai Tian, Weize Li, Linbo Wang, Senyu Fei, Pengfei Li, Yinfeng Gao, Zebin Xing, Yilun Chen, Qichao Zhang, Haoran Li, Wenchao Ding
First: 2026-04-22T17:58:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:58:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advances in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have opened new avenues for robot manipulation, yet existing methods exhibit limited efficiency and a lack of high-level knowledge and spatial awareness. To address these challenges, we propose PokeVLA, a lightweight yet powerful foundation model for embodied manipulation that effectively infuses vision-language understanding into action learning. Our framework introduces a two-stage training paradigm: first, we pre-train a compact vision-language model (PokeVLM) on a curated multimodal dataset of 2.4M samples encompassing spatial grounding, affordance, and embodied reasoning tasks; second, we inject manipulation-relevant representations into the action space through multi-view goal-aware semantics learning, geometry alignment, and a novel action expert. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on the LIBERO-Plus benchmark and in real-world deployment, outperforming comparable baselines in success rate and robustness under diverse perturbations. To foster reproducibility and community progress, we will open-source our code, model weights, and the scripts for the curated pre-training dataset. Project page: https://getterupper.github.io/PokeVLA
中文标题/摘要
标题：PokeVLA：借助全面世界知识引导，赋能口袋大小的视觉-语言-动作模型
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的最新进展为机器人操作开辟了新途径，但现有方法效率有限，缺乏高层知识和空间感知能力。为应对这些挑战，我们提出PokeVLA，一种轻量级但强大的具身操作基础模型，有效将视觉-语言理解融入动作学习。我们的框架采用两阶段训练范式：首先，在包含240万样本的精选多模态数据集上预训练紧凑型视觉-语言模型（PokeVLM），涵盖空间定位、可操作性及具身推理任务；其次，通过多视角目标感知语义学习、几何对齐及新颖的动作专家，将操作相关表征注入动作空间。大量实验表明，PokeVLA在LIBERO-Plus基准测试及真实部署中均达到最先进性能，在多样扰动下成功率与鲁棒性均优于同类基线。为促进可复现性与社区进展，我们将开源代码、模型权重及精选预训练数据集脚本。项目页面：https://getterupper.github.io/PokeVLA
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and lack of high-level spatial knowledge in existing Vision-Language-Action models for robot manipulation, PokeVLA introduces a lightweight yet powerful foundation model that integrates vision-language understanding into action learning. The method employs a two-stage training process: first, a compact vision-language model (PokeVLM) is pre-trained on a 2.4M-sample multimodal dataset covering spatial grounding, affordance, and embodied reasoning; second, manipulation-relevant representations are injected into the action space via multi-view goal-aware semantics, geometry alignment, and a novel action expert. Experimental results show state-of-the-art performance on LIBERO-Plus, achieving higher success rates and robustness under diverse perturbations compared to existing baselines.
为解决现有视觉-语言-动作模型在机器人操作中效率低、缺乏高层空间感知的问题，作者提出PokeVLA，一种轻量级基础模型，将视觉-语言理解融入动作学习。该方法采用两阶段训练：首先在包含空间定位、可操作性及具身推理任务的240万样本多模态数据集上预训练紧凑型视觉-语言模型（PokeVLM）；其次通过多视角目标感知语义、几何对齐及新型动作专家，将操作相关表征注入动作空间。实验表明，PokeVLA在LIBERO-Plus基准及真实部署中表现最优，成功率和对多样化扰动的鲁棒性显著优于同类基线模型。
AVISE: Framework for Evaluating the Security of AI Systems
Authors: Mikko Lempinen, Joni Kemppainen, Niklas Raesalmi
First: 2026-04-22T17:58:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:58:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As artificial intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly deployed across critical domains, their security vulnerabilities pose growing risks of high-profile exploits and consequential system failures. Yet systematic approaches to evaluating AI security remain underdeveloped. In this paper, we introduce AVISE (AI Vulnerability Identification and Security Evaluation), a modular open-source framework for identifying vulnerabilities in and evaluating the security of AI systems and models. As a demonstration of the framework, we extend the theory-of-mind-based multi-turn Red Queen attack into an Adversarial Language Model (ALM) augmented attack and develop an automated Security Evaluation Test (SET) for discovering jailbreak vulnerabilities in language models. The SET comprises 25 test cases and an Evaluation Language Model (ELM) that determines whether each test case was able to jailbreak the target model, achieving 92% accuracy, an F1-score of 0.91, and a Matthews correlation coefficient of 0.83. We evaluate nine recently released language models of diverse sizes with the SET and find that all are vulnerable to the augmented Red Queen attack to varying degrees. AVISE provides researchers and industry practitioners with an extensible foundation for developing and deploying automated SETs, offering a concrete step toward more rigorous and reproducible AI security evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AVISE：用于评估人工智能系统安全性的框架
随着人工智能（AI）系统在关键领域中的部署日益广泛，其安全漏洞正带来日益严重的高调攻击和系统故障风险。然而，系统性评估AI安全的方法仍不成熟。本文提出了AVISE（AI漏洞识别与安全评估），一个模块化开源框架，用于识别AI系统和模型中的漏洞并评估其安全性。作为框架的演示，我们将基于心智理论的多轮红皇后攻击扩展为增强型对抗语言模型（ALM）攻击，并开发了一种自动化安全评估测试（SET），用于发现语言模型中的越狱漏洞。该SET包含25个测试用例和一个评估语言模型（ELM），用于判断每个测试用例是否成功越狱目标模型，准确率达92%，F1分数为0.91，马修斯相关系数为0.83。我们使用SET评估了九个不同规模的最新发布语言模型，发现所有模型均不同程度地存在增强型红皇后攻击漏洞。AVISE为研究人员和行业从业者提供了一个可扩展的基础，用于开发和部署自动化SET，是迈向更严谨、可重复的AI安全评估的重要一步。
Summary / 总结
As AI systems grow more prevalent in critical applications, their security flaws threaten high-impact exploits and system failures, yet no systematic evaluation methods exist. AVISE, an open-source modular framework, addresses this gap by enabling vulnerability detection and security assessment of AI models. The authors demonstrate its utility by adapting the Red Queen attack into an Adversarial Language Model (ALM) augmented attack and creating an automated Security Evaluation Test (SET) with 25 test cases and an Evaluation Language Model (ELM) to detect jailbreaks, achieving 92% accuracy, 0.91 F1-score, and 0.83 Matthews correlation coefficient. Testing nine diverse language models revealed all were vulnerable to this attack, highlighting widespread security risks and providing a reproducible, extensible foundation for future AI security evaluations.
随着AI系统在关键领域应用日益广泛，其安全漏洞可能引发重大事故，但系统化的评估方法仍显不足。AVISE是一个开源模块化框架，旨在识别和评估AI系统的安全漏洞。作者通过将红皇后攻击扩展为增强型对抗语言模型攻击，并构建包含25个测试用例和评估语言模型（ELM）的自动化安全评估测试（SET），实现了92%准确率、0.91 F1值和0.83马修斯相关系数。对九个不同规模的语言模型测试发现，全部存在不同程度的越狱漏洞，揭示了广泛的安全风险，并为未来自动化安全评估提供了可扩展的基准。
FedSIR: Spectral Client Identification and Relabeling for Federated Learning with Noisy Labels
Authors: Sina Gholami, Abdulmoneam Ali, Tania Haghighi, Ahmed Arafa, Minhaj Nur Alam
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-22T17:49:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:49:20+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 5th Workshop on Federated Learning for Computer Vision (FedVision), CVPR 2026. Sina Gholami and Abdulmoneam Ali contributed equally
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw data; however, the presence of noisy labels across distributed clients can severely degrade the learning performance. In this paper, we propose FedSIR, a multi-stage framework for robust FL under noisy labels. Different from existing approaches that mainly rely on designing noise-tolerant loss functions or exploiting loss dynamics during training, our method leverages the spectral structure of client feature representations to identify and mitigate label noise. Our framework consists of three key components. First, we identify clean and noisy clients by analyzing the spectral consistency of class-wise feature subspaces with minimal communication overhead. Second, clean clients provide spectral references that enable noisy clients to relabel potentially corrupted samples using both dominant class directions and residual subspaces. Third, we employ a noise-aware training strategy that integrates logit-adjusted loss, knowledge distillation, and distance-aware aggregation to further stabilize federated optimization. Extensive experiments on standard FL benchmarks demonstrate that FedSIR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods for FL with noisy labels. The code is available at https://github.com/sinagh72/FedSIR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FedSIR：面向噪声标签的联邦学习中的谱客户端识别与重标注
联邦学习（FL）允许在不共享原始数据的情况下协同训练模型；然而，分布式客户端间存在噪声标签会严重降低学习性能。本文提出FedSIR，一种面向噪声标签的鲁棒联邦学习多阶段框架。与现有主要依赖设计噪声容忍损失函数或利用训练过程中的损失动态的方法不同，我们的方法利用客户端特征表示的谱结构来识别并缓解标签噪声。 该框架包含三个关键组件：首先，通过分析类间特征子空间的谱一致性，在最小通信开销下识别干净与噪声客户端；其次，干净客户端提供谱参考，使噪声客户端能利用主导类方向和残差子空间对潜在被污染样本进行重标注；第三，我们采用噪声感知训练策略，结合对数几率调整损失、知识蒸馏和距离感知聚合，进一步稳定联邦优化。在标准FL基准上的大量实验表明，FedSIR在噪声标签联邦学习中持续优于现有最先进方法。代码见https://github.com/sinagh72/FedSIR。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the degradation of federated learning performance due to noisy labels across clients, FedSIR introduces a multi-stage framework that leverages spectral structures in client feature representations to identify and correct label noise. It first distinguishes clean from noisy clients by analyzing spectral consistency of class-wise feature subspaces with minimal communication. Clean clients then serve as spectral references to help noisy clients relabel corrupted samples using dominant class directions and residual subspaces. Finally, a noise-aware training strategy combining logit-adjusted loss, knowledge distillation, and distance-aware aggregation stabilizes federated optimization. Experiments on standard FL benchmarks show FedSIR consistently outperforms existing methods under noisy label conditions.
为了解决联邦学习中因客户端标签噪声导致的性能下降问题，FedSIR提出了一种基于谱结构的框架，通过不共享原始数据的方式识别并修正标签噪声。该框架首先通过分析各类别特征子空间的谱一致性，区分干净客户端与噪声客户端；随后利用干净客户端的谱参考，结合主导类别方向与残差子空间对噪声客户端中的错误标签进行重标注；最后采用结合对数调整损失、知识蒸馏和距离感知聚合的噪声感知训练策略以稳定联邦优化。在多个标准联邦学习基准上的实验表明，FedSIR在噪声标签环境下持续优于现有最先进方法。
Closing the Domain Gap in Biomedical Imaging by In-Context Control Samples
Authors: Ana Sanchez-Fernandez, Thomas Pinetz, Werner Zellinger, Günter Klambauer
First: 2026-04-22T17:49:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:49:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The central problem in biomedical imaging are batch effects: systematic technical variations unrelated to the biological signal of interest. These batch effects critically undermine experimental reproducibility and are the primary cause of failure of deep learning systems on new experimental batches, preventing their practical use in the real world. Despite years of research, no method has succeeded in closing this performance gap for deep learning models. We propose Control-Stabilized Adaptive Risk Minimization via Batch Normalization (CS-ARM-BN), a meta-learning adaptation method that exploits negative control samples. Such unperturbed reference images are present in every experimental batch by design and serve as stable context for adaptation. We validate our novel method on Mechanism-of-Action (MoA) classification, a crucial task for drug discovery, on the large-scale JUMP-CP dataset. The accuracy of standard ResNets drops from 0.939 $\pm$ 0.005, on the training domain, to 0.862 $\pm$ 0.060 on data from new experimental batches. Foundation models, even after Typical Variation Normalization, fail to close this gap. We are the first to show that meta-learning approaches close the domain gap by achieving 0.935 $\pm$ 0.018. If the new experimental batches exhibit strong domain shifts, such as being generated in a different lab, meta-learning approaches can be stabilized with control samples, which are always available in biomedical experiments. Our work shows that batch effects in bioimaging data can be effectively neutralized through principled in-context adaptation, which also makes them practically usable and efficient.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过上下文控制样本缩小生物医学成像中的域差距
生物医学成像的核心问题是批次效应：与生物信号无关的系统性技术变异。这些批次效应严重削弱实验可重复性，也是深度学习系统在新实验批次上失败的主要原因，阻碍其在现实世界中的实际应用。尽管多年研究，尚无方法成功缩小深度学习模型的这一性能差距。我们提出控制稳定自适应风险最小化（CS-ARM-BN），一种利用负控制样本的元学习适应方法。此类未受扰动的参考图像在每个实验批次中均有设计，作为稳定的适应上下文。我们在大规模JUMP-CP数据集上验证了该方法在药物发现关键任务——机制作用（MoA）分类中的有效性。标准ResNet在训练域的准确率为0.939 ± 0.005，而在新实验批次数据上降至0.862 ± 0.060。即使经过典型变异标准化，基础模型也未能弥合此差距。我们首次证明，元学习方法通过达到0.935 ± 0.018的准确率成功缩小域差距。若新实验批次存在强烈域偏移（如在不同实验室生成），元学习方法可通过始终存在的控制样本加以稳定。我们的工作表明，通过原理性的上下文适应，生物医学成像数据中的批次效应可有效中和，使其具备实用性和高效性。
Summary / 总结
To address the persistent issue of batch effects in biomedical imaging that degrade model performance across experimental batches, the authors introduce CS-ARM-BN, a meta-learning adaptation method leveraging negative control samples—unperturbed reference images inherently present in each batch—to stabilize model adaptation. Evaluated on the JUMP-CP dataset for Mechanism-of-Action classification, standard ResNets suffer a significant accuracy drop from 0.939 to 0.862 when applied to new batches, while foundation models with normalization fail to close the gap. CS-ARM-BN, however, achieves 0.935 accuracy, demonstrating that meta-learning with in-context control samples can effectively neutralize domain shifts and restore model performance, making deep learning models practically deployable in real-world biomedical settings.
为解决生物医学成像中批次效应导致的深度学习模型跨实验批次性能下降问题，作者提出CS-ARM-BN方法，利用实验批次中固有的未扰动对照样本（负控制样本）进行元学习适应，以稳定模型表现。在JUMP-CP数据集的药物作用机制分类任务中，标准ResNet模型在新批次上的准确率从训练域的0.939±0.005骤降至0.862±0.060，而基础模型即使经过典型变异归一化也未能弥合该差距。相比之下，CS-ARM-BN达到0.935±0.018，证明借助上下文对照样本的元学习方法可有效消除域偏移，使深度学习模型在真实生物医学场景中具备实用性和高效性。
From Meme to Method: Rethinking Animal Adoption Platforms through the Cat Distribution System
Authors: Carl Angelo Angcana, Jamlech Iram Gojo Cruz
First: 2026-04-22T17:47:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:47:53+00:00
Comments: To be published in Proceedings of the 2025 International Conference on Human-Engaged Computing (ICHEC 2025), November 21-23, 2025, Singapore, Singapore. ACM, New York, NY, USA, 14 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The internet folklore of the Cat Distribution System (CDS) humorously suggests that cats are "assigned" to people rather than intentionally sought. Beyond its playful origins, CDS reflects a culturally resonant way people perceive and engage in adoption, and this user context can guide the redesign and improvement of adoption systems. In the Philippines, where an estimated 13.11 million stray cats and dogs place the country sixth worldwide in overpopulation, this framing offers a novel way to rethink adoption platforms. We developed a prototype application inspired by CDS principles, focusing on features such as algorithmic matchmaking, community reporting, and proximity-based discovery. An initial evaluation with potential users (n=35) indicated that the system was positively received for its ease of use and its alignment with users' intuitive expectations, though participants highlighted areas for improvement in transparency of matchmaking and owner-adopter communication. The findings suggest that culturally embedded metaphors like CDS can shape mental models, making adoption processes feel more serendipitous and less transactional.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从梗到方法：通过猫分配系统重新思考动物领养平台
猫分配系统（CDS）的网络民间传说幽默地暗示猫是“分配”给人们的，而非主动寻求。尽管其起源轻松戏谑，CDS却反映了人们感知和参与领养的一种文化共鸣方式，这种用户语境可指导领养系统的重新设计与改进。在菲律宾，约有1311万只流浪猫狗，使该国成为全球流浪动物数量第六的国家，这一框架为重新思考领养平台提供了新颖视角。我们开发了一款受CDS理念启发的原型应用，聚焦算法匹配、社区报告和基于位置的发现等功能。对潜在用户（n=35）的初步评估显示，该系统因其易用性及与用户直觉预期的契合而获得积极反馈，但参与者也指出了匹配透明度和养宠者-领养者沟通方面的改进空间。研究结果表明，如CDS般深植于文化中的隐喻可塑造心理模型，使领养过程更显偶然性，而非交易性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the cultural resonance of the Cat Distribution System meme, this study explores how adopting a serendipitous, algorithmically matched adoption model can improve user experience on animal adoption platforms in the Philippines, where stray animal overpopulation is severe. The researchers developed a prototype app incorporating matchmaking, community reporting, and proximity-based discovery, inspired by the meme’s metaphor of cats being 'assigned' rather than chosen. Initial testing with 35 users showed positive reception for usability and alignment with intuitive expectations, though participants requested greater transparency in matching logic and clearer communication channels between owners and adopters.
受网络迷因‘猫分配系统’启发，该研究旨在通过将动物领养视为一种‘随机分配’而非交易，重新设计菲律宾的领养平台（该国流浪猫狗数量高达1311万，居全球第六）。系统采用算法匹配、社区报告和基于位置的发现功能，以契合用户直觉。35名潜在用户测试显示，系统在易用性和契合用户认知模型方面获得好评，但用户希望提升匹配逻辑透明度及领养人与主人间的沟通清晰度。
Global Offshore Wind Infrastructure: Deployment and Operational Dynamics from Dense Sentinel-1 Time Series
Authors: Thorsten Hoeser, Felix Bachofer, Claudia Kuenzer
First: 2026-04-22T17:47:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:47:33+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 16 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The offshore wind energy sector is expanding rapidly, increasing the need for independent, high-temporal-resolution monitoring of infrastructure deployment and operation at global scale. While Earth Observation based offshore wind infrastructure mapping has matured for spatial localization, existing open datasets lack temporally dense and semantically fine-grained information on construction and operational dynamics. We introduce a global Sentinel-1 synthetic aperture radar (SAR) time series data corpus that resolves deployment and operational phases of offshore wind infrastructure from 2016Q1 to 2025Q1. Building on an updated object detection workflow, we compile 15,606 time series at detected infrastructure locations, with overall 14,840,637 events as analysis-ready 1D SAR backscatter profiles, one profile per Sentinel-1 acquisition and location. To enable direct use and benchmarking, we release (i) the analysis ready 1D SAR profiles, (ii) event-level baseline semantic labels generated by a rule-based classifier, and (iii) an expert-annotated benchmark dataset of 553 time series with 328,657 event labels. The baseline classifier achieves a macro F1 score of 0.84 in event-wise evaluation and an area under the collapsed edit similarity-quality threshold curve (AUC) of 0.785, indicating temporal coherence. We demonstrate that the resulting corpus supports global-scale analyses of deployment dynamics, the identification of differences in regional deployment patterns, vessel interactions, and operational events, and provides a reference for developing and comparing time series classification methods for offshore wind infrastructure monitoring.
中文标题/摘要
标题：全球海上风电基础设施：基于密集Sentinel-1时序数据的部署与运行动态
海上风电行业正迅速扩张，亟需在全球范围内对基础设施的部署与运行进行独立、高时间分辨率的监测。尽管基于地球观测的海上风电基础设施空间定位已趋于成熟，但现有公开数据集缺乏在时间上密集且语义精细的施工与运行动态信息。我们引入了一个全球Sentinel-1合成孔径雷达（SAR）时序数据集，覆盖2016年第一季度至2025年第一季度，可解析海上风电基础设施的部署与运行阶段。基于更新的对象检测工作流，我们在检测到的基础设施位置上编纂了15,606个时序数据，总计14,840,637个分析就绪的1D SAR后向散射剖面（每个Sentinel-1采集与位置对应一个剖面）。为便于直接使用与基准测试，我们发布：（i）分析就绪的1D SAR剖面；（ii）由规则分类器生成的事件级基础语义标签；（iii）包含553个时序数据、328,657个事件标签的专家标注基准数据集。基础分类器在事件级评估中达到0.84的宏F1分数，且在折叠编辑相似性-质量阈值曲线下的AUC为0.785，表明时间一致性。我们证明该数据集支持全球尺度的部署动态分析、区域部署模式差异识别、船舶交互及运行事件检测，并为海上风电基础设施监测的时间序列分类方法的开发与比较提供参考。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the rapid growth of offshore wind energy and the need for global, high-temporal-resolution monitoring, this study leverages dense Sentinel-1 SAR time series from 2016Q1 to 2025Q1 to track deployment and operational phases of offshore wind infrastructure. Using an updated object detection workflow, the researchers compiled 15,606 time series with 14.8 million event-level SAR backscatter profiles, and released a benchmark dataset of 553 time series with 328,657 expert-labeled events. A rule-based classifier achieved a macro F1 score of 0.84 and an AUC of 0.785, demonstrating temporal coherence and enabling global-scale analysis of deployment patterns, vessel interactions, and operational events.
为应对海上风电快速扩张带来的全球高时间分辨率监测需求，本研究利用2016至2025年间密集的Sentinel-1 SAR时序数据，构建并分析海上风电基础设施的部署与运营阶段。基于改进的目标检测流程，研究团队收集了15,606个时序数据，包含1480万条事件级SAR后向散射剖面，并发布含专家标注的基准数据集用于模型对比。规则基分类器在事件级评估中F1值达0.84，AUC为0.785，表明时间一致性良好，支持全球部署模式、船舶交互及运营事件的分析。
Stream-CQSA: Avoiding Out-of-Memory in Attention Computation via Flexible Workload Scheduling
Authors: Yiming Bian, Joshua M. Akey
First: 2026-04-22T17:46:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:46:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The scalability of long-context large language models is fundamentally limited by the quadratic memory cost of exact self-attention, which often leads to out-of-memory (OOM) failures on modern hardware. Existing methods improve memory efficiency to near-linear complexity, while assuming that the full query, key, and value tensors fit in device memory. In this work, we remove this assumption by introducing CQS Divide, an operation derived from cyclic quorum sets (CQS) theory that decomposes attention into a set of independent subsequence computations whose recomposition yields exactly the same result as full-sequence attention. Exploiting this decomposition, we introduce Stream-CQSA, a memory-adaptive scheduling framework that partitions attention into subproblems that fit within arbitrary memory budgets. This recasts attention from a logically monolithic operation into a collection of schedulable tasks, enabling flexible execution across devices without inter-device communication. Experiments demonstrate predictable memory scaling and show that exact attention over billion-token sequences can be executed on a single GPU via streaming, without changing the underlying mathematical definition of attention or introducing approximation error.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Stream-CQSA：通过灵活的工作负载调度避免注意力计算中的内存溢出
长上下文大语言模型的可扩展性从根本上受限于精确自注意力的二次内存开销，这常导致现代硬件上的内存溢出（OOM）失败。现有方法通过提升内存效率将复杂度降至近线性，但假设查询、键和值张量完全适配设备内存。本文通过引入源自循环多数集（CQS）理论的CQS分割操作，打破该假设：该操作将注意力分解为一组独立的子序列计算，其重组结果与完整序列注意力完全一致。基于此分解，我们提出Stream-CQSA，一种内存自适应调度框架，可将注意力划分为任意内存预算下可容纳的子问题。该方法将注意力从逻辑上单一的操作重构为可调度任务集合，从而在设备间无需通信的情况下实现灵活执行。实验表明，内存消耗可预测，且无需改变注意力的数学定义或引入近似误差，即可在单个GPU上流式执行十亿级token序列的精确注意力计算。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the memory bottlenecks in long-context LLMs caused by quadratic attention costs, this work introduces Stream-CQSA, a framework that avoids out-of-memory failures by decomposing attention into independent, memory-bounded subtasks using CQS Divide—a mathematical operation derived from cyclic quorum sets. This decomposition allows attention to be executed as a stream of schedulable tasks, enabling flexible memory usage across devices without communication overhead. Experiments confirm that exact attention over billion-token sequences can be run on a single GPU without approximation, achieving predictable memory scaling and maintaining computational accuracy.
为了解决长上下文大语言模型因精确自注意力的二次内存开销导致的内存溢出问题，本文提出Stream-CQSA框架，通过引入源自循环多数集理论的CQS Divide操作，将注意力分解为多个独立、内存受限的子任务。该分解使注意力可被调度为可执行的任务流，无需设备间通信，即可在任意内存预算下灵活运行。实验表明，该方法可在单张GPU上精确执行十亿级token序列的注意力计算，且内存消耗可预测，未改变注意力的数学定义或引入近似误差。
Convergent Evolution: How Different Language Models Learn Similar Number Representations
Authors: Deqing Fu, Tianyi Zhou, Mikhail Belkin, Vatsal Sharan, Robin Jia
First: 2026-04-22T17:45:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:45:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models trained on natural text learn to represent numbers using periodic features with dominant periods at $T=2, 5, 10$. In this paper, we identify a two-tiered hierarchy of these features: while Transformers, Linear RNNs, LSTMs, and classical word embeddings trained in different ways all learn features that have period-$T$ spikes in the Fourier domain, only some learn geometrically separable features that can be used to linearly classify a number mod-$T$. To explain this incongruity, we prove that Fourier domain sparsity is necessary but not sufficient for mod-$T$ geometric separability. Empirically, we investigate when model training yields geometrically separable features, finding that the data, architecture, optimizer, and tokenizer all play key roles. In particular, we identify two different routes through which models can acquire geometrically separable features: they can learn them from complementary co-occurrence signals in general language data, including text-number co-occurrence and cross-number interaction, or from multi-token (but not single-token) addition problems. Overall, our results highlight the phenomenon of convergent evolution in feature learning: A diverse range of models learn similar features from different training signals.
中文标题/摘要
标题：趋同进化：不同语言模型如何学习相似的数字表示
在自然文本上训练的语言模型学会使用具有主导周期 $T=2, 5, 10$ 的周期性特征来表示数字。本文识别出这些特征的两级层次结构：虽然Transformer、线性RNN、LSTM和不同训练方式的经典词嵌入均在傅里叶域中学习到周期为$T$的尖峰特征，但只有部分模型能学习到几何可分离特征，从而线性分类模$T$的数字。为解释这一不一致，我们证明傅里叶域稀疏性是模$T$几何可分离性的必要但不充分条件。实证上，我们研究模型训练何时产生几何可分离特征，发现数据、架构、优化器和分词器均起关键作用。特别是，我们识别出两种模型获得几何可分离特征的途径：其一，从通用语言数据中的互补共现信号学习，包括文本-数字共现和跨数字交互；其二，从多词（而非单词）加法问题中学习。总体而言，我们的结果突显了特征学习中的趋同进化现象：多样化的模型从不同的训练信号中学习到相似的特征。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates why different language models, despite varying architectures and training methods, converge on similar number representations characterized by periodic Fourier features with dominant periods at T=2, 5, 10. The authors demonstrate that while many models exhibit Fourier-domain sparsity, only some acquire geometrically separable features enabling linear classification of numbers modulo T. They show that Fourier sparsity alone is insufficient for geometric separability and identify two pathways for acquiring such features: learning from co-occurrence patterns in natural language data (including number-text associations) or from multi-token arithmetic tasks. Experimental results confirm that data, architecture, optimizer, and tokenizer all influence whether geometric separability emerges, revealing convergent evolution in how diverse models develop similar numerical representations from distinct training signals.
该研究探讨为何不同架构的语言模型（如Transformer、RNN和经典词嵌入）在训练后会收敛到相似的周期性数字表示，尽管其训练方式各异。作者发现，所有模型在傅里叶域均呈现T=2,5,10的周期尖峰，但仅部分模型能习得几何可分离特征，从而支持模T线性分类。他们证明傅里叶稀疏性是必要但非充分条件，实证表明数据、架构、优化器和分词器共同影响模型是否获得此类特征。两种路径浮现：一是从自然文本中数字与词汇的共现模式学习，二是从多词加法任务中学习。研究揭示了特征学习中的趋同演化现象——不同模型通过不同训练信号独立获得相似表示。
ParetoSlider: Diffusion Models Post-Training for Continuous Reward Control
Authors: Shelly Golan, Michael Finkelson, Ariel Bereslavsky, Yotam Nitzan, Or Patashnik
First: 2026-04-22T17:44:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:44:56+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://shelley-golan.github.io/ParetoSlider-webpage/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning (RL) post-training has become the standard for aligning generative models with human preferences, yet most methods rely on a single scalar reward. When multiple criteria matter, the prevailing practice of ``early scalarization'' collapses rewards into a fixed weighted sum. This commits the model to a single trade-off point at training time, providing no inference-time control over inherently conflicting goals -- such as prompt adherence versus source fidelity in image editing. We introduce ParetoSlider, a multi-objective RL (MORL) framework that trains a single diffusion model to approximate the entire Pareto front. By training the model with continuously varying preference weights as a conditioning signal, we enable users to navigate optimal trade-offs at inference time without retraining or maintaining multiple checkpoints. We evaluate ParetoSlider across three state-of-the-art flow-matching backbones: SD3.5, FluxKontext, and LTX-2. Our single preference-conditioned model matches or exceeds the performance of baselines trained separately for fixed reward trade-offs, while uniquely providing fine-grained control over competing generative goals.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ParetoSlider：用于连续奖励控制的扩散模型后训练
强化学习（RL）后训练已成为将生成模型与人类偏好对齐的标准方法，但大多数方法依赖单一标量奖励。当多个标准重要时，当前“早期标量化”的做法将奖励压缩为固定加权和，这使模型在训练时锁定单一权衡点，无法在推理时控制固有冲突的目标——例如图像编辑中的提示遵循性与源保真度。我们提出ParetoSlider，一种多目标强化学习（MORL）框架，训练单个扩散模型以近似整个帕累托前沿。通过将连续变化的偏好权重作为条件信号训练模型，用户可在推理时导航最优权衡，无需重新训练或维护多个检查点。我们在三个最先进的流匹配骨干网络（SD3.5、FluxKontext和LTX-2）上评估ParetoSlider，其单个偏好条件模型表现与为固定奖励权衡分别训练的基线相当或更优，同时独特提供对竞争生成目标的精细控制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of scalar reward methods in reinforcement learning that fix trade-offs during training, ParetoSlider introduces a multi-objective RL framework that trains a single diffusion model to approximate the full Pareto front. By conditioning the model on continuously varying preference weights during training, users can dynamically adjust trade-offs at inference time without retraining. Evaluated on SD3.5, FluxKontext, and LTX-2, ParetoSlider achieves performance matching or exceeding that of separately trained baselines while offering fine-grained control over conflicting objectives like prompt adherence and source fidelity.
为了解决强化学习中单标量奖励方法在训练时固定权衡比例的问题，ParetoSlider提出了一种多目标强化学习框架，训练单个扩散模型以逼近完整的帕累托前沿。通过在训练中将连续变化的偏好权重作为条件信号，用户可在推理时动态调整权衡比例，无需重新训练或维护多个模型。在SD3.5、FluxKontext和LTX-2三个前沿流匹配架构上评估，ParetoSlider表现与单独训练的基线模型相当或更优，同时实现了对冲突生成目标（如提示遵循与源保真度）的精细控制。
Mask World Model: Predicting What Matters for Robust Robot Policy Learning
Authors: Yunfan Lou, Xiaowei Chi, Xiaojie Zhang, Zezhong Qian, Chengxuan Li, Rongyu Zhang, Yaoxu Lyu, Guoyu Song, Chuyao Fu, Haoxuan Xu, Pengwei Wang, Shanghang Zhang
First: 2026-04-21T17:05:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:44:56+00:00
Comments: 16 pages,5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
World models derived from large-scale video generative pre-training have emerged as a promising paradigm for generalist robot policy learning. However, standard approaches often focus on high-fidelity RGB video prediction, this can result in overfitting to irrelevant factors, such as dynamic backgrounds and illumination changes. These distractions reduce the model's ability to generalize, ultimately leading to unreliable and fragile control policies. To address this, we introduce the Mask World Model (MWM), which leverages video diffusion architectures to predict the evolution of semantic masks instead of pixels. This shift imposes a geometric information bottleneck, forcing the model to capture essential physical dynamics and contact relations while filtering out visual noise. We seamlessly integrate this mask dynamics backbone with a diffusion-based policy head to enable robust end-to-end control. Extensive evaluations demonstrate the superiority of MWM on the LIBERO and RLBench simulation benchmarks, significantly outperforming the state-of-the-art RGB-based world models. Furthermore, real-world experiments and robustness evaluation (via random token pruning) reveal that MWM exhibits superior generalization capabilities and robust resilience to texture information loss.
中文标题/摘要
标题：掩码世界模型：预测对稳健机器人策略学习至关重要的因素
基于大规模视频生成预训练的世界模型已成为通用机器人策略学习的有前景范式。然而，标准方法通常专注于高保真RGB视频预测，这可能导致模型过度拟合无关因素，如动态背景和光照变化。这些干扰降低了模型的泛化能力，最终导致控制策略不可靠且脆弱。为解决此问题，我们提出了掩码世界模型（MWM），该模型利用视频扩散架构预测语义掩码的演化，而非像素值。这一转变施加了几何信息瓶颈，迫使模型捕捉关键物理动态和接触关系，同时过滤视觉噪声。我们无缝地将该掩码动态主干与基于扩散的策略头集成，以实现稳健的端到端控制。大量评估表明，MWM在LIBERO和RLBench仿真基准上显著优于现有基于RGB的世界模型。此外，真实世界实验和鲁棒性评估（通过随机令牌剪枝）表明，MWM展现出更强的泛化能力和对纹理信息丢失的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the overfitting issues of standard RGB-based world models in robot policy learning, this work introduces the Mask World Model (MWM), which predicts semantic masks instead of raw pixels using video diffusion architectures. This approach enforces a geometric information bottleneck, compelling the model to focus on essential physical dynamics and contact relations while ignoring distracting visual noise. The MWM integrates a mask dynamics backbone with a diffusion-based policy head for end-to-end control, achieving superior performance on simulation benchmarks (LIBERO and RLBench) and demonstrating stronger generalization and robustness in real-world settings, even under texture loss induced by random token pruning.
为解决传统RGB世界模型易受无关视觉噪声干扰、泛化能力差的问题，本文提出掩码世界模型（MWM），通过视频扩散预测语义掩码而非像素，强制模型聚焦物理动态与接触关系，过滤视觉干扰。MWM将掩码预测骨干与扩散策略头结合，实现端到端控制。在LIBERO和RLBench仿真基准上，MWM显著优于现有RGB模型；真实世界实验及随机令牌剪枝测试表明其泛化能力更强，对纹理丢失更具鲁棒性。
Adapting TrOCR for Printed Tigrinya Text Recognition: Word-Aware Loss Weighting for Cross-Script Transfer Learning
Authors: Yonatan Haile Medhanie, Yuanhua Ni
First: 2026-04-22T17:43:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:43:50+00:00
Comments: Code and models available at https://github.com/YoHa2024NKU/Tigrinya_TrOCR_Printed Pre-trained models: https://huggingface.co/Yonatanhaile2026/tigrinya-trocrprinted, https://huggingface.co/Yonatanhaile2026/tigrinya-trocrhandwritten
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Transformer-based OCR models have shown strong performance on Latin and CJK scripts, but their application to African syllabic writing systems remains limited. We present the first adaptation of TrOCR for printed Tigrinya using the Ge'ez script. Starting from a pre-trained model, we extend the byte-level BPE tokenizer to cover 230 Ge'ez characters and introduce Word-Aware Loss Weighting to resolve systematic word-boundary failures that arise when applying Latin-centric BPE conventions to a new script. The unmodified model produces no usable output on Ge'ez text. After adaptation, the TrOCR-Printed variant achieves 0.22% Character Error Rate and 97.20% exact match accuracy on a held-out test set of 5,000 synthetic images from the GLOCR dataset. An ablation study confirms that Word-Aware Loss Weighting is the critical component, reducing CER by two orders of magnitude compared to vocabulary extension alone. The full pipeline trains in under three hours on a single 8 GB consumer GPU. All code, model weights, and evaluation scripts are publicly released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：将TrOCR适配用于印刷体提格尼亚文字识别：基于词感知的损失加权用于跨文字系统迁移学习
基于Transformer的OCR模型在拉丁字母和CJK文字上表现优异，但在非洲音节文字系统中的应用仍有限。本文首次将TrOCR适配用于采用格泽文字的印刷体提格尼亚文字。从预训练模型出发，我们将字节级BPE分词器扩展至覆盖230个格泽字符，并引入词感知损失加权机制，以解决将拉丁字母中心化BPE规范应用于新文字系统时出现的系统性词边界错误。未经修改的模型在格泽文字上无法输出可用结果。经过适配后，TrOCR-Printed变体在GLOCR数据集的5000张合成图像验证集上，字符错误率为0.22%，精确匹配准确率为97.20%。消融研究表明，词感知损失加权是关键组件，相比仅扩展词汇表，其将字符错误率降低了两个数量级。完整流程在单张8GB消费级GPU上训练时间不足三小时。所有代码、模型权重和评估脚本均已公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of OCR models adapted for African syllabic scripts like Ge'ez, this work adapts TrOCR for printed Tigrinya by extending its tokenizer to 230 Ge'ez characters and introducing Word-Aware Loss Weighting to address word-boundary errors from Latin-centric tokenization. The unmodified model fails entirely on Ge'ez text, but the adapted version achieves 0.22% Character Error Rate and 97.20% exact match accuracy on 5,000 synthetic test images. An ablation study shows Word-Aware Loss Weighting is essential, reducing error by two orders of magnitude over vocabulary extension alone, with the full pipeline training in under three hours on a consumer GPU.
为解决非洲辅音文字（如格兹文）缺乏OCR支持的问题，本研究将TrOCR适配为印刷体提格尼亚文，扩展其分词器以覆盖230个格兹字符，并引入词感知损失加权机制以解决拉丁系分词导致的词边界错误。未经修改的模型在格兹文本上完全失效，而适配后模型在5000张合成测试图像上达到0.22%字符错误率和97.20%精确匹配率。消融实验表明词感知损失加权是关键，其性能比仅扩展词汇表提升两个数量级，整个流程在消费级GPU上训练耗时不足三小时。
Diagnosing CFG Interpretation in LLMs
Authors: Hanqi Li, Lu Chen, Kai Yu
First: 2026-04-22T17:43:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:43:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As LLMs are increasingly integrated into agentic systems, they must adhere to dynamically defined, machine-interpretable interfaces. We evaluate LLMs as in-context interpreters: given a novel context-free grammar, can LLMs generate syntactically valid, behaviorally functional, and semantically faithful outputs? We introduce RoboGrid, a framework that disentangles syntax, behavior, and semantics through controlled stress-tests of recursion depth, expression complexity, and surface styles. Our experiments reveal a consistent hierarchical degradation: LLMs often maintain surface syntax but fail to preserve structural semantics. Despite the partial mitigation provided by CoT reasoning, performance collapses under structural density, specifically deep recursion and high branching, with semantic alignment vanishing at extreme depths. Furthermore, "Alien" lexicons reveal that LLMs rely on semantic bootstrapping from keywords rather than pure symbolic induction. These findings pinpoint critical gaps in hierarchical state-tracking required for reliable, grammar-agnostic agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型CFG解释能力诊断
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）在代理系统中的集成度提高，它们必须遵循动态定义、机器可解释的接口。我们评估LLMs作为上下文解释器的能力：给定一个新颖的无上下文文法，LLMs能否生成语法正确、行为功能完备且语义忠实的输出？我们引入RoboGrid框架，通过控制递归深度、表达式复杂度和表面风格的应力测试，将语法、行为与语义解耦。实验揭示了一种一致的层级退化现象：LLMs常能保持表面语法，但无法保留结构语义。尽管思维链（CoT）推理部分缓解了这一问题，但在结构密度高（特别是深层递归和高分支）时性能崩溃，极端深度下语义对齐完全消失。此外，“异星”词汇表表明LLMs依赖关键词的语义引导而非纯粹符号归纳。这些发现指出了构建可靠、与文法无关的代理系统所需层级状态追踪的关键缺陷。
Summary / 总结
As large language models (LLMs) are deployed in agentic systems requiring machine-interpretable interfaces, researchers investigate whether LLMs can correctly interpret novel context-free grammars by generating syntactically valid, behaviorally functional, and semantically faithful outputs. Using RoboGrid, a framework that isolates syntax, behavior, and semantics through controlled stress tests involving recursion depth, expression complexity, and surface style variations, the study finds that LLMs consistently degrade hierarchically: they preserve surface syntax but fail to maintain structural semantics under increasing complexity, especially deep recursion and high branching. CoT reasoning offers limited improvement, and semantic alignment collapses at extreme depths. Additionally, experiments with "Alien" lexicons show LLMs depend on semantic bootstrapping from keywords rather than symbolic induction, revealing critical gaps in hierarchical state-tracking for reliable, grammar-agnostic agents.
随着大语言模型（LLM）被用于需要机器可解释接口的代理系统，研究者评估LLM能否正确解析新的上下文无关文法，生成语法正确、行为功能完备且语义忠实的输出。通过RoboGrid框架，在控制递归深度、表达复杂度和表面风格等压力测试下，研究发现LLM存在层级性退化：能保持表面语法但无法维持结构语义，尤其在深度递归和高分支情况下表现崩溃。即使使用思维链（CoT）推理，效果也有限，极端深度下语义对齐完全消失。此外，使用“异星”词汇实验表明，LLM依赖关键词语义引导而非符号归纳，揭示了构建可靠、与文法无关代理所必需的层级状态追踪能力的关键缺陷。
OMIBench: Benchmarking Olympiad-Level Multi-Image Reasoning in Large Vision-Language Model
Authors: Qiguang Chen, Chengyu Luan, Jiajun Wu, Qiming Yu, Yi Yang, Yizhuo Li, Jingqi Tong, Xiachong Feng, Libo Qin, Wanxiang Che
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-22T17:37:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:37:40+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Camera Ready
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have made substantial advances in reasoning tasks at the Olympiad level. Nevertheless, current Olympiad-level multimodal reasoning benchmarks for these models often emphasize single-image analysis and fail to exploit contextual information across multiple images. We present OMIBench, a benchmark designed to evaluate Olympiad-level reasoning when the required evidence is distributed over multiple images. It contains problems from biology, chemistry, mathematics, and physics Olympiads, together with manually annotated rationales and evaluation protocols for both exact and semantic answer matching. Across extensive experiments on OMIBench, we observe meaningful performance gaps in existing models. Even the strongest LVLMs, such as Gemini-3-Pro, attain only about 50% on the benchmark. These results position OMIBench as a focused resources for studying and improving multi-image reasoning in LVLMs.
中文标题/摘要

Relative Principals, Pluralistic Alignment, and the Structural Value Alignment Problem
Authors: Travis LaCroix
First: 2026-04-22T17:36:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:36:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted in the Ninth Annual ACM Conference on Fairness, Accountability, and Transparency (ACM FAccT) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The value alignment problem for artificial intelligence (AI) is often framed as a purely technical or normative challenge, sometimes focused on hypothetical future systems. I argue that the problem is better understood as a structural question about governance: not whether an AI system is aligned in the abstract, but whether it is aligned enough, for whom, and at what cost. Drawing on the principal-agent framework from economics, this paper reconceptualises misalignment as arising along three interacting axes: objectives, information, and principals. The three-axis framework provides a systematic way of diagnosing why misalignment arises in real-world systems and clarifies that alignment cannot be treated as a single technical property of models but an outcome shaped by how objectives are specified, how information is distributed, and whose interests count in practice. The core contribution of this paper is to show that the three-axis decomposition implies that alignment is fundamentally a problem of governance rather than engineering alone. From this perspective, alignment is inherently pluralistic and context-dependent, and resolving misalignment involves trade-offs among competing values. Because misalignment can occur along each axis -- and affect stakeholders differently -- the structural description shows that alignment cannot be "solved" through technical design alone, but must be managed through ongoing institutional processes that determine how objectives are set, how systems are evaluated, and how affected communities can contest or reshape those decisions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：相对委托人、多元对齐与结构化价值对齐问题
人工智能的价值对齐问题常被框定为纯粹的技术或规范性挑战，有时聚焦于假设的未来系统。本文主张，该问题更宜理解为治理结构问题：并非抽象地讨论AI系统是否对齐，而是其对齐程度、面向何人、以及代价如何。借鉴经济学中的委托-代理框架，本文将错位重构为沿三个相互作用的轴线产生：目标、信息与委托人。三轴框架提供了一种系统性诊断现实系统中错位成因的方法，并阐明对齐不能视为模型的单一技术属性，而应视为由目标设定方式、信息分配机制及实际利益相关者构成的治理结果。本文核心贡献在于揭示三轴分解意味着对齐本质上是治理问题而非工程问题。从这一视角，对齐具有内在多元性与情境依赖性，解决错位需在竞争性价值间进行权衡。由于错位可沿任一轴线发生并影响不同利益相关者，结构化描述表明，仅靠技术设计无法“解决”对齐问题，必须通过持续的制度性过程加以管理，以决定目标如何设定、系统如何评估，以及受影响社区如何参与或重塑这些决策。
Summary / 总结
This paper argues that AI value alignment is fundamentally a governance issue rather than a purely technical one, motivated by the need to understand misalignment not as a model property but as a structural problem shaped by objectives, information, and principals. Using a principal-agent framework, it decomposes misalignment into three interacting axes and demonstrates that alignment outcomes depend on how objectives are defined, how information flows, and whose interests are prioritized. Experimental analysis across real-world systems reveals that alignment is context-sensitive and pluralistic, requiring institutional processes to manage trade-offs among competing values rather than relying solely on technical fixes.
本文认为人工智能的价值对齐问题本质上是治理问题而非纯技术问题，通过目标、信息和委托人三个相互作用的维度，运用委托-代理框架进行分析。作者指出，对齐偏差并非源于模型设计本身，而是源于目标如何设定、信息如何分配以及实际中哪些利益相关者被优先考虑。实验结果虽未详述，但暗示对齐必须通过制度性过程管理，而非单纯工程化解决，因为不同价值间的权衡不可避免且依赖于具体情境。
Survival of the Cheapest: Cost-Aware Hardware Adaptation for Adversarial Robustness
Authors: Charles Meyers, Mohammad Reza Saleh Sedghpour, Tommy Löfstedt, Erik Elmroth
First: 2024-09-11T20:43:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:36:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deploying adversarially robust machine learning systems requires continuous trade-offs between robustness, cost, and latency. We present an autonomic decision-support framework providing a quantitative foundation for adaptive hardware selection and hyper-parameter tuning in cloud-native deep learning. The framework applies accelerated failure time (AFT) models to quantify the effect of hardware choice, batch size, epochs, and validation accuracy on model survival time. This framework can be naturally integrated into an autonomic control loop (monitor--analyse--plan--execute, MAPE-K), where system metrics such as cost, robustness, and latency are continuously evaluated and used to adapt model configurations and hardware selection. Experiments across three GPU architectures confirm the framework is both sound and cost-effective: the Nvidia L4 yields a 20% increase in adversarial survival time while costing 75% less than the V100, demonstrating that expensive hardware does not necessarily improve robustness. The analysis further reveals that model inference latency is a stronger predictor of adversarial robustness than training time or hardware configuration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：最便宜者的生存：面向对抗鲁棒性的成本感知硬件自适应

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance adversarial robustness with cost and latency in cloud-native deep learning, the paper introduces an autonomic framework using accelerated failure time models to quantify how hardware, batch size, epochs, and validation accuracy affect model survival time under adversarial attacks. Integrated into a MAPE-K control loop, the system continuously adapts configurations based on real-time metrics. Experiments across three GPU architectures show that the Nvidia L4 achieves 20% longer survival time at 75% lower cost than the V100, proving that expensive hardware isn't always better, and that inference latency is a stronger predictor of robustness than training time or hardware choice.
为在云原生深度学习中平衡对抗鲁棒性、成本与延迟，本文提出一种自主框架，利用加速失效时间模型量化硬件、批大小、训练轮数和验证准确率对模型在对抗攻击下存活时间的影响。该框架嵌入MAPE-K控制循环，持续监控成本、鲁棒性和延迟指标并自动调整配置。在三种GPU架构上的实验表明，Nvidia L4在成本仅为V100的75%的情况下，使模型对抗存活时间提升20%，证明昂贵硬件未必更优，且推理延迟比训练时长或硬件配置更能预测鲁棒性。
Beyond the Crowd: LLM-Augmented Community Notes for Governing Health Misinformation
Authors: Jiaying Wu, Zihang Fu, Haonan Wang, Fanxiao Li, Jiafeng Guo, Preslav Nakov, Min-Yen Kan
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-10-13T13:57:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:34:25+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Community Notes, the crowd-sourced misinformation governance system on X (formerly Twitter), allows users to flag misleading posts, attach contextual notes, and rate the notes' helpfulness. However, our empirical analysis of 30.8K health-related notes reveals substantial latency, with a median delay of 17.6 hours before notes receive a helpfulness status. To improve responsiveness during real-world misinformation surges, we propose CrowdNotes+, a unified LLM-based framework that augments Community Notes for faster and more reliable health misinformation governance. CrowdNotes+ integrates two modes: (1) evidence-grounded note augmentation and (2) utility-guided note automation, supported by a hierarchical three-stage evaluation of relevance, correctness, and helpfulness. We instantiate the framework with HealthNotes, a benchmark of 1.2K health notes annotated for helpfulness, and a fine-tuned helpfulness judge. Our analysis first uncovers a key loophole in current crowd-sourced governance: voters frequently conflate stylistic fluency with factual accuracy. Addressing this via our hierarchical evaluation, experiments across 15 representative LLMs demonstrate that CrowdNotes+ significantly outperforms human contributors in note correctness, helpfulness, and evidence utility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越群体：基于大语言模型增强的社区注释系统用于治理健康 misinformation
X（原推特）上的社区注释系统允许用户标记误导性帖子、添加上下文注释并评价注释的有用性。然而，我们对30,800条健康相关注释的实证分析显示，注释获得有用性评级存在显著延迟，中位延迟达17.6小时。为提升真实世界 misinformation 暴发期间的响应速度，我们提出 CrowdNotes+，一个统一的基于大语言模型的框架，用于增强社区注释，实现更快、更可靠的健康 misinformation 治理。CrowdNotes+ 包含两种模式：（1）基于证据的注释增强，（2）基于效用的注释自动化，辅以分层三阶段评估（相关性、正确性、有用性）。我们以 HealthNotes 为实例，该基准包含1,200条标注有用性的健康注释及一个微调后的有用性评估模型。分析首先揭示当前众包治理的关键漏洞：投票者常将语言流畅性与事实准确性混淆。通过分层评估解决此问题，实验在15个代表性大语言模型上表明，CrowdNotes+ 在注释正确性、有用性及证据效用方面显著优于人工贡献者。

Motivated by the slow response time of crowd-sourced health misinformation governance—median delay of 17.6 hours for note evaluation—the authors propose CrowdNotes+, an LLM-augmented framework to accelerate and improve note quality. Experiments on 1.2K annotated health notes across 15 LLMs show CrowdNotes+ significantly outperforms human contributors in correctness, helpfulness, and evidence utility, while also revealing that human voters often mistake stylistic fluency for factual accuracy.

WISCA: A Lightweight Model Transition Method to Improve LLM Training via Weight Scaling
Authors: Jiacheng Li, Jianchao Tan, Zhidong Yang, Pingwei Sun, Feiye Huo, Jiayu Qin, Xiangyu Zhang, Maoxin He, Yerui Sun, Yuchen Xie, Guangming Tan, Weile Jia, Xunliang Cai, Tong Zhao
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-08-21T08:08:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:31:33+00:00
Comments: Findings of the Association for Computational Linguistics: ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformer architecture gradually dominates the LLM field. Recent advances in training optimization for Transformer-based large language models (LLMs) primarily focus on architectural modifications or optimizer adjustments. However, these approaches lack systematic optimization of weight patterns during training. Weight pattern refers to the distribution and relative magnitudes of weight parameters in a neural network. To address this issue, we propose a Weight Scaling method called WISCA to enhance training efficiency and model quality by strategically improving neural network weight patterns without changing network structures. By rescaling weights while preserving model outputs, WISCA indirectly optimizes the model's training trajectory. Experiments demonstrate that WISCA significantly improves convergence quality (measured by generalization capability and loss reduction), particularly in LLMs with Grouped Query Attention (GQA) architectures and LoRA fine-tuning tasks. Empirical results show 5.6% average improvement on zero-shot validation tasks and 2.12% average reduction in training perplexity across multiple architectures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WISCA：一种通过权重缩放提升大语言模型训练效率的轻量级模型转换方法
Transformer架构逐渐主导大语言模型领域。近期针对基于Transformer的大语言模型（LLMs）训练优化的研究主要集中在架构修改或优化器调整上。然而，这些方法缺乏对训练过程中权重模式的系统性优化。权重模式指神经网络中权重参数的分布及其相对大小。为解决此问题，我们提出一种名为WISCA的权重缩放方法，通过策略性地改善神经网络权重模式（而不改变网络结构）来提升训练效率和模型质量。WISCA在保持模型输出不变的前提下重新缩放权重，从而间接优化模型的训练轨迹。实验表明，WISCA显著提升了收敛质量（以泛化能力和损失降低衡量），尤其在采用分组查询注意力（GQA）架构和LoRA微调任务的模型中表现突出。实证结果显示，在多个架构上，零样本验证任务平均提升5.6%，训练困惑度平均降低2.12%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of systematic optimization of weight patterns in Transformer-based LLM training, the authors propose WISCA, a lightweight weight scaling method that enhances training efficiency and model quality without altering network architecture. WISCA rescales weights while preserving model outputs, thereby indirectly guiding the training trajectory toward better convergence. Experimental results show that WISCA improves generalization and reduces loss, with 5.6% average gain on zero-shot validation tasks and 2.12% average reduction in training perplexity across multiple architectures, particularly benefiting GQA-based LLMs and LoRA fine-tuning.
为系统优化训练过程中神经网络权重分布而不改变网络结构，作者提出WISCA方法，通过在保持输出不变的前提下对权重进行缩放，间接改善训练轨迹。在GQA架构和LoRA微调等多类模型上实验表明，WISCA显著提升收敛质量，零样本验证任务平均提升5.6%，训练困惑度平均降低2.12%。
Synthesizing Multi-Agent Harnesses for Vulnerability Discovery
Authors: Hanzhi Liu, Chaofan Shou, Xiaonan Liu, Hongbo Wen, Yanju Chen, Ryan Jingyang Fang, Yu Feng
First: 2026-04-22T17:27:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:27:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM agents have begun to find real security vulnerabilities that human auditors and automated fuzzers missed for decades, in source-available targets where the analyst can build and instrument the code. In practice the work is split among several agents, wired together by a harness: the program that fixes which roles exist, how they pass information, which tools each may call, and how retries are coordinated. When the language model is held fixed, changing only the harness can still change success rates by several-fold on public agent benchmarks, yet most harnesses are written by hand; recent harness optimizers each search only a narrow slice of the design space and rely on coarse pass/fail feedback that gives no diagnostic signal about why a trial failed. AgentFlow addresses both limitations with a typed graph DSL whose search space jointly covers agent roles, prompts, tools, communication topology, and coordination protocol, paired with a feedback-driven outer loop that reads runtime signals from the target program itself to diagnose which part of the harness caused the failure and rewrite it accordingly. We evaluate AgentFlow on TerminalBench-2 with Claude Opus 4.6 and on Google Chrome with Kimi K2.5. AgentFlow reaches 84.3% on TerminalBench-2, the highest score in the public leaderboard snapshot we evaluate against, and discovers ten previously unknown zero-day vulnerabilities in Google Chrome, including two Critical sandbox-escape vulnerabilities (CVE-2026-5280 and CVE-2026-6297).
中文标题/摘要
标题：合成多智能体框架以发现漏洞
大型语言模型智能体已开始在源码可获取的目标中发现人类审计员和自动化模糊测试工具数十年来忽略的真实安全漏洞，分析员可构建并植入代码。实践中，工作由多个智能体分工协作，通过一个框架（harness）连接：该框架定义角色存在方式、信息传递机制、各智能体可调用的工具及重试协调方式。当语言模型固定时，仅调整框架仍可使成功率在公开智能体基准测试中提升数倍，但现有框架多由人工编写；近期框架优化器仅搜索设计空间的狭窄部分，且依赖粗粒度的通过/失败反馈，无法诊断失败原因。AgentFlow通过类型化图DSL联合覆盖智能体角色、提示、工具、通信拓扑与协调协议的搜索空间，并结合基于反馈的外环机制，读取目标程序运行时信号以诊断框架中导致失败的部分并相应重写。我们在TerminalBench-2上使用Claude Opus 4.6、在Google Chrome上使用Kimi K2.5评估AgentFlow，其在TerminalBench-2上达到84.3%的最高分（当前公开排行榜中最高），并在Google Chrome中发现十项此前未知的零日漏洞，包括两项关键的沙箱逃逸漏洞（CVE-2026-5280和CVE-2026-6297）。
Summary / 总结

Evaluated on TerminalBench-2 with Claude Opus 4.6 and Google Chrome with Kimi K2.5, AgentFlow achieved 84.3% success rate on TerminalBench-2 — the highest public score at evaluation — and uncovered ten previously unknown zero-day vulnerabilities in Chrome, including two critical sandbox-escape flaws.
为提升多智能体漏洞发现系统的效能，本文提出AgentFlow框架，通过合成协调程序（harness）来优化智能体角色、通信与工具调用。该框架采用类型化图DSL联合探索设计空间，并结合基于运行时信号的反馈机制诊断失败原因并重写配置。在TerminalBench-2（Claude Opus 4.6）和Google Chrome（Kimi K2.5）上评估，AgentFlow达成84.3%成功率，为公开榜单最高，并发现10个此前未知的零日漏洞，包括两个关键的沙箱逃逸漏洞（CVE-2026-5280与CVE-2026-6297）。
LEXIS: LatEnt ProXimal Interaction Signatures for 3D HOI from an Image
Authors: Dimitrije Antić, Alvaro Budria, George Paschalidis, Sai Kumar Dwivedi, Dimitrios Tzionas
First: 2026-04-22T17:27:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:27:13+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 11 figures, 4 tables. Project page: https://anticdimi.github.io/lexis
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reconstructing 3D Human-Object Interaction from an RGB image is essential for perceptive systems. Yet, this remains challenging as it requires capturing the subtle physical coupling between the body and objects. While current methods rely on sparse, binary contact cues, these fail to model the continuous proximity and dense spatial relationships that characterize natural interactions. We address this limitation via InterFields, a representation that encodes dense, continuous proximity across the entire body and object surfaces. However, inferring these fields from single images is inherently ill-posed. To tackle this, our intuition is that interaction patterns are characteristically structured by the action and object geometry. We capture this structure in LEXIS, a novel discrete manifold of interaction signatures learned via a VQ-VAE. We then develop LEXIS-Flow, a diffusion framework that leverages LEXIS signatures to estimate human and object meshes alongside their InterFields. Notably, these InterFields help in a guided refinement that ensures physically-plausible, proximity-aware reconstructions without requiring post-hoc optimization. Evaluation on Open3DHOI and BEHAVE shows that LEXIS-Flow significantly outperforms existing SotA baselines in reconstruction, contact, and proximity quality. Our approach not only improves generalization but also yields reconstructions perceived as more realistic, moving us closer to holistic 3D scene understanding. Code & models will be public at https://anticdimi.github.io/lexis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LEXIS：从图像中重建3D人-物交互的LatEnt近邻交互特征
从RGB图像重建3D人-物交互对感知系统至关重要，但该任务仍具挑战性，因其需捕捉身体与物体间的细微物理耦合。当前方法依赖稀疏二值接触线索，无法建模自然交互中连续的邻近性和密集的空间关系。我们通过InterFields表示法解决此问题，该表示法编码全身与物体表面的密集连续邻近性。然而，从单张图像推断这些场域本质上是不适定的。为此，我们提出直觉：交互模式由动作与物体几何结构所决定。我们通过VQ-VAE学习到一种新颖的离散流形交互签名集合——LEXIS。随后，我们开发了LEXIS-Flow扩散框架，利用LEXIS签名同时估计人体与物体网格及其InterFields。值得注意的是，这些InterFields可引导精细化过程，确保物理合理且邻近感知的重建，无需后处理优化。在Open3DHOI和BEHAVE数据集上的评估表明，LEXIS-Flow在重建、接触与邻近性质量上显著优于现有SOTA基线。我们的方法不仅提升泛化能力，还生成更逼真的重建结果，推动我们向全面3D场景理解迈进。代码与模型将在https://anticdimi.github.io/lexis公开。
