A Hough transform approach to safety-aware scalar field mapping using Gaussian Processes
Authors: Muzaffar Qureshi, Trivikram Satharasi, Tochukwu E. Ogri, Kyle Volle, Rushikesh Kamalapurkar
First: 2026-04-22T17:27:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:27:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a framework for mapping unknown scalar fields using a sensor-equipped autonomous robot operating in unsafe environments. The unsafe regions are defined as regions of high-intensity, where the field value exceeds a predefined safety threshold. For safe and efficient mapping of the scalar field, the sensor-equipped robot must avoid high-intensity regions during the measurement process. In this paper, the scalar field is modeled as a sample from a Gaussian process (GP), which enables Bayesian inference and provides closed-form expressions for both the predictive mean and the uncertainty. Concurrently, the spatial structure of the high-intensity regions is estimated in real-time using the Hough transform (HT), leveraging the evolving GP posterior. A safe sampling strategy is then employed to guide the robot towards safe measurement locations, using probabilistic safety guarantees on the evolving GP posterior. The estimated high-intensity regions also facilitate the design of safe motion plans for the robot. The effectiveness of the approach is verified through two numerical simulation studies and an indoor experiment for mapping a light-intensity field using a wheeled mobile robot.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于高斯过程的安全感知标量场映射的霍夫变换方法
本文提出了一种利用配备传感器的自主机器人在不安全环境中对未知标量场进行映射的框架。不安全区域被定义为场值超过预设安全阈值的高强度区域。为安全高效地映射标量场，该机器人必须在测量过程中避开高强度区域。本文将标量场建模为高斯过程（GP）的样本，从而支持贝叶斯推断，并提供预测均值和不确定性的闭式表达。同时，利用高斯过程后验的实时演化，通过霍夫变换（HT）估计高强度区域的空间结构。随后采用安全采样策略引导机器人前往安全测量位置，该策略基于高斯过程后验的演化所提供的概率安全保证。所估计的高强度区域还支持为机器人设计安全运动规划。该方法的有效性通过两个数值仿真研究和一个使用轮式移动机器人进行室内光照强度场映射的实验得到验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to safely map unknown scalar fields in hazardous environments, this paper proposes a framework where a sensor-equipped robot avoids high-intensity regions while building a probabilistic map. The scalar field is modeled as a Gaussian process to enable Bayesian inference and uncertainty quantification, while the Hough transform is used in real-time to estimate the spatial structure of unsafe zones based on the evolving GP posterior. A safety-aware sampling strategy then guides the robot to measure in low-risk areas using probabilistic guarantees from the GP, and the identified unsafe regions inform safe motion planning. Experimental results from simulations and an indoor light-field mapping experiment confirm the method’s effectiveness in safely and efficiently constructing accurate scalar field maps.
为在需避开高强度区域的危险环境中安全高效地构建未知标量场地图，本文提出结合高斯过程建模与霍夫变换实时检测高危区域的方法。机器人利用不断更新的高斯过程后验分布，以概率安全保证引导采样路径，并通过霍夫变换估计高危区域的空间结构。仿真与室内光照场实验结果表明，该方法能有效识别并规避高危区域，同时实现准确的场重建与安全导航。
Gauge-Equivariant Graph Neural Networks for Lattice Gauge Theories
Authors: Ali Rayat, Yaohang Li, Gia-Wei Chern
First: 2026-04-22T17:21:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:21:20+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Local gauge symmetry underlies fundamental interactions and strongly correlated quantum matter, yet existing machine-learning approaches lack a general, principled framework for learning under site-dependent symmetries, particularly for intrinsically nonlocal observables. Here we introduce a gauge-equivariant graph neural network that embeds non-Abelian symmetry directly into message passing via matrix-valued, gauge-covariant features and symmetry-compatible updates, extending equivariant learning from global to fully local symmetries. In this formulation, message passing implements gauge-covariant transport across the lattice, allowing nonlocal correlations and loop-like structures to emerge naturally from local operations. We validate the approach across pure gauge, gauge-matter, and dynamical regimes, establishing gauge-equivariant message passing as a general paradigm for learning in systems governed by local symmetry.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于格点规范理论的规范协变图神经网络
局部规范对称性是基本相互作用和强关联量子物质的基础，但现有机器学习方法缺乏在位置依赖对称性下学习的通用、原理性框架，特别是针对本征非局域可观测量。本文引入一种规范协变图神经网络，通过矩阵值、规范协变特征和兼容对称性的更新，将非阿贝尔对称性直接嵌入消息传递过程，将协变学习从全局对称性扩展到完全局域对称性。在此框架下，消息传递实现格点上的规范协变传输，使非局域关联和环状结构自然从局域操作中涌现。我们在纯规范、规范-物质和动力学体系中验证了该方法，确立了规范协变消息传递作为在局部对称性系统中学习的通用范式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model systems with local gauge symmetry—such as lattice gauge theories—this work proposes a gauge-equivariant graph neural network that embeds non-Abelian symmetries directly into message passing using matrix-valued, gauge-covariant features and symmetry-compatible updates. The method extends equivariant learning to fully local symmetries by enabling gauge-covariant transport across the lattice, allowing nonlocal correlations and loop structures to emerge from local operations. Experimental results across pure gauge, gauge-matter, and dynamical regimes demonstrate that this approach effectively captures gauge-invariant physics and establishes gauge-equivariant message passing as a general framework for learning in locally symmetric systems.
为建模具有局部规范对称性的系统（如格点规范理论），本文提出一种规范协变图神经网络，通过矩阵值、规范协变的特征与兼容更新机制，将非阿贝尔规范对称性嵌入消息传递过程，使网络能尊重局部对称性。该方法将协变学习从全局对称性扩展至完全局部对称性，使规范协变的传输在格点上自然实现，并从局部操作中自发生成非局域关联与环状结构。在纯规范、规范-物质及动力学体系中的实验验证表明，该框架有效，确立了规范协变消息传递作为处理局部对称性系统学习的通用范式。
LLaDA2.0-Uni: Unifying Multimodal Understanding and Generation with Diffusion Large Language Model
Authors: Inclusion AI, Tiwei Bie, Haoxing Chen, Tieyuan Chen, Zhenglin Cheng, Long Cui, Kai Gan, Zhicheng Huang, Zhenzhong Lan, Haoquan Li, Jianguo Li, Tao Lin, Qi Qin, Hongjun Wang, Xiaomei Wang, Haoyuan Wu, Yi Xin, Junbo Zhao
First: 2026-04-22T17:20:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:20:42+00:00
Comments: LLaDA2.0-Uni Technical Report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present LLaDA2.0-Uni, a unified discrete diffusion large language model (dLLM) that supports multimodal understanding and generation within a natively integrated framework. Its architecture combines a fully semantic discrete tokenizer, a MoE-based dLLM backbone, and a diffusion decoder. By discretizing continuous visual inputs via SigLIP-VQ, the model enables block-level masked diffusion for both text and vision inputs within the backbone, while the decoder reconstructs visual tokens into high-fidelity images. Inference efficiency is enhanced beyond parallel decoding through prefix-aware optimizations in the backbone and few-step distillation in the decoder. Supported by carefully curated large-scale data and a tailored multi-stage training pipeline, LLaDA2.0-Uni matches specialized VLMs in multimodal understanding while delivering strong performance in image generation and editing. Its native support for interleaved generation and reasoning establishes a promising and scalable paradigm for next-generation unified foundation models. Codes and models are available at https://github.com/inclusionAI/LLaDA2.0-Uni.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LLaDA2.0-Uni：基于扩散大语言模型的多模态理解与生成统一框架
我们提出了LLaDA2.0-Uni，一种支持多模态理解与生成的统一离散扩散大语言模型（dLLM），其架构融合了完全语义离散分词器、基于MoE的dLLM主干网络和扩散解码器。通过SigLIP-VQ将连续视觉输入离散化，模型在主干网络内支持文本与视觉输入的块级掩码扩散，解码器则将视觉标记重建为高保真图像。通过主干网络的前缀感知优化和解码器的少量步数蒸馏，推理效率超越并行解码。依托精心构建的大规模数据集和定制化的多阶段训练流程，LLaDA2.0-Uni在多模态理解方面媲美专用视觉语言模型，同时在图像生成与编辑方面表现优异。其原生支持交错生成与推理，为下一代统一基础模型提供了有前景且可扩展的范式。代码与模型见https://github.com/inclusionAI/LLaDA2.0-Uni。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for unified multimodal understanding and generation, LLaDA2.0-Uni introduces a discrete diffusion large language model that integrates text and vision processing within a single framework. It employs a semantic discrete tokenizer, a MoE-based dLLM backbone, and a diffusion decoder, with visual inputs discretized via SigLIP-VQ to enable block-level masked diffusion. The model achieves high-fidelity image reconstruction and efficient inference through prefix-aware backbone optimizations and few-step decoder distillation. Experimental results show it matches specialized vision-language models in understanding tasks while excelling in image generation and editing, and supports interleaved reasoning and generation, establishing a scalable paradigm for unified foundation models.
为实现图文理解与生成的统一，LLaDA2.0-Uni提出一种离散扩散大语言模型，将文本与视觉处理集成于单一框架。该模型采用语义离散词元、MoE结构的dLLM主干与扩散解码器，通过SigLIP-VQ将视觉输入离散化，支持块级掩码扩散。解码器重建高保真图像，主干通过前缀感知优化与解码器少量蒸馏提升推理效率。实验表明，该模型在图文理解上媲美专用视觉语言模型，图像生成与编辑表现优异，且支持图文交替生成与推理，为下一代统一基础模型提供可扩展范式。
Automatic Ontology Construction Using LLMs as an External Layer of Memory, Verification, and Planning for Hybrid Intelligent Systems
Authors: Pavel Salovskii, Iuliia Gorshkova
First: 2026-04-22T17:19:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:19:43+00:00
Comments: Artificial Intelligence; Knowledge Representation and Reasoning; Information Retrieval; Machine Learning
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a hybrid architecture for intelligent systems in which large language models (LLMs) are extended with an external ontological memory layer. Instead of relying solely on parametric knowledge and vector-based retrieval (RAG), the proposed approach constructs and maintains a structured knowledge graph using RDF/OWL representations, enabling persistent, verifiable, and semantically grounded reasoning. The core contribution is an automated pipeline for ontology construction from heterogeneous data sources, including documents, APIs, and dialogue logs. The system performs entity recognition, relation extraction, normalization, and triple generation, followed by validation using SHACL and OWL constraints, and continuous graph updates. During inference, LLMs operate over a combined context that integrates vector-based retrieval with graph-based reasoning and external tool interaction. Experimental observations on planning tasks, including the Tower of Hanoi benchmark, indicate that ontology augmentation improves performance in multi-step reasoning scenarios compared to baseline LLM systems. In addition, the ontology layer enables formal validation of generated outputs, transforming the system into a generation-verification-correction pipeline. The proposed architecture addresses key limitations of current LLM-based systems, including lack of long-term memory, weak structural understanding, and limited reasoning capabilities. It provides a foundation for building agent-based systems, robotics applications, and enterprise AI solutions that require persistent knowledge, explainability, and reliable decision-making.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用大语言模型作为混合智能系统记忆、验证与规划的外部层进行本体自动构建
本文提出一种混合智能系统架构，其中大语言模型（LLMs）扩展了外部本体记忆层。该方法不依赖参数化知识和基于向量的检索（RAG），而是通过RDF/OWL表示构建并维护结构化知识图谱，实现持久、可验证且语义基础的推理。 核心贡献是一种从异构数据源（包括文档、API和对话日志）自动构建本体的流水线。系统执行实体识别、关系抽取、标准化和三元组生成，随后通过SHACL和OWL约束进行验证，并持续更新图谱。推理阶段，LLMs在结合向量检索、图谱推理和外部工具交互的综合上下文中运行。 在规划任务（包括汉诺塔基准）的实验中，本体增强显著提升了多步推理场景下的性能，优于基线LLM系统。此外，本体层支持生成结果的正式验证，将系统转变为生成-验证-修正流水线。 该架构解决了当前LLM系统的关键局限，包括缺乏长期记忆、结构理解薄弱和推理能力有限的问题，为需要持久知识、可解释性和可靠决策的代理系统、机器人应用和企业AI解决方案提供了基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of LLMs in long-term memory, structural reasoning, and explainability, this paper proposes a hybrid architecture that augments LLMs with an external ontological memory layer built using RDF/OWL. The method automates ontology construction from diverse data sources via entity recognition, relation extraction, normalization, and triple generation, followed by validation using SHACL and OWL constraints and continuous graph updates. Experimental results on planning tasks like the Tower of Hanoi show improved multi-step reasoning performance and formal output validation compared to baseline LLM systems, demonstrating the architecture’s value for agent-based and enterprise AI applications requiring persistent, verifiable knowledge.
为解决大语言模型在长期记忆、结构理解与推理能力方面的不足，本文提出一种混合架构，通过构建基于RDF/OWL的知识图谱作为外部本体记忆层来增强LLM。该方法从异构数据源自动构建本体，包括实体识别、关系抽取、标准化及三元组生成，并通过SHACL与OWL约束进行验证与持续更新。实验表明，在如汉诺塔等规划任务中，引入本体层显著提升多步推理性能，并支持生成结果的正式验证，使系统形成生成-验证-修正的闭环流程。
Can "AI" Be a Doctor? A Study of Empathy, Readability, and Alignment in Clinical LLMs
Authors: Mariano Barone, Francesco Di Serio, Roberto Moio, Marco Postiglione, Giuseppe Riccio, Antonio Romano, Vincenzo Moscato
First: 2026-04-22T17:17:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:17:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in healthcare, yet their communicative alignment with clinical standards remains insufficiently quantified. We conduct a multidimensional evaluation of general-purpose and domain-specialized LLMs across structured medical explanations and real-world physician-patient interactions, analyzing semantic fidelity, readability, and affective resonance. Baseline models amplify affective polarity relative to physicians (Very Negative: 43.14-45.10% vs. 37.25%) and, in larger architectures such as GPT-5 and Claude, produce substantially higher linguistic complexity (FKGL up to 16.91-17.60 vs. 11.47-12.50 in physician-authored responses). Empathy-oriented prompting reduces extreme negativity and lowers grade-level complexity (up to -6.87 FKGL points for GPT-5) but does not significantly increase semantic fidelity. Collaborative rewriting yields the strongest overall alignment. Rephrase configurations achieve the highest semantic similarity to physician answers (up to mean = 0.93) while consistently improving readability and reducing affective extremity. Dual stakeholder evaluation shows that no model surpasses physicians on epistemic criteria, whereas patients consistently prefer rewritten variants for clarity and emotional tone. These findings suggest that LLMs function most effectively as collaborative communication enhancers rather than replacements for clinical expertise.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能能当医生吗？临床大语言模型的共情、可读性与对齐度研究
大语言模型（LLMs）在医疗领域应用日益广泛，但其与临床标准的沟通对齐程度尚未充分量化。我们对通用型和领域专用型LLMs在结构化医学解释与真实医患互动中的多维度表现进行评估，分析语义保真度、可读性与情感共鸣。基线模型相较医生（非常负面：43.14%-45.10% vs. 37.25%）显著放大情感极性；在GPT-5和Claude等大型架构中，语言复杂度显著更高（FKGL最高达16.91-17.60 vs. 医生撰写的11.47-12.50）。以共情为导向的提示可降低极端负面情绪并降低年级水平复杂度（GPT-5最高降低6.87 FKGL点），但未显著提升语义保真度。协作重写实现整体对齐度最佳。重写配置在语义相似度上表现最优（平均达0.93），同时持续提升可读性并降低情感极端性。双主体评估显示，无模型在知识性标准上超越医生，而患者始终偏好重写版本以获得清晰度与情感语调。研究结果表明，LLMs最有效的作用是作为协作沟通增强者，而非临床专业能力的替代者。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) can function effectively as clinical communicators by evaluating their alignment with physician standards across empathy, readability, and semantic fidelity. The researchers compare general-purpose and domain-specialized LLMs against real physician-patient interactions, finding that baseline models tend to generate more negatively charged and linguistically complex responses than physicians. Empathy-focused prompting reduces negativity and complexity but does not improve semantic accuracy. The most effective approach is collaborative rewriting, which yields the highest semantic similarity to physician responses (up to 0.93), improves readability, and reduces emotional extremity. Patient evaluations favor rewritten outputs for clarity and tone, while no model outperforms physicians on epistemic accuracy, suggesting LLMs are best used as collaborative tools rather than clinical substitutes.
本研究评估大语言模型（LLM）在临床沟通中的表现，重点考察其在共情、可读性与语义准确性方面的与医生标准的契合度。研究人员对比通用型与医疗领域专用型LLM与真实医生-患者对话，测量语言复杂度、情感倾向和语义一致性。结果显示，基线模型输出的情感倾向更负面、语言复杂度更高，尤其大型模型如GPT-5和Claude复杂度显著高于医生。共情引导提示可降低负面情绪和复杂度，但不提升语义准确性。而协作重写策略在语义相似度（最高达0.93）、可读性与情感温和性方面表现最优。患者更偏好重写版本，因其更清晰、语气更温和，但无模型在知识准确性上超越医生，表明LLM更适合作为医生的协作沟通辅助工具，而非临床替代者。
Working Memory Constraints Scaffold Learning in Transformers under Data Scarcity
Authors: Pranava Madhyastha, Dagmar Adamcova
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-22T17:14:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:14:52+00:00
Comments: Published in ACL 2026 Findings track
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We investigate the integration of human-like working memory constraints into the Transformer architecture and implement several cognitively inspired attention variants, including fixed-width windows based and temporal decay based attention mechanisms. Our modified GPT-2 models are trained from scratch on developmentally plausible datasets (10M and 100M words). Performance is evaluated on grammatical judgment tasks (BLiMP) and alignment with human reading time data. Our results indicate that these cognitively-inspired constraints, particularly fixed-width attention, can significantly improve grammatical accuracy especially when training data is scarce. These constrained models also tend to show a stronger alignment with human processing metrics. The findings suggest that such constraints may serve as a beneficial inductive bias, guiding models towards more robust linguistic representations, especially in data-limited settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：工作记忆约束在数据稀缺条件下为Transformer学习提供支撑
我们研究了将类人工作记忆约束整合到Transformer架构中，并实现了几种受认知启发的注意力变体，包括基于固定宽度窗口和基于时间衰减的注意力机制。我们的修改版GPT-2模型从发育合理数据集（1000万和1亿词）从零开始训练。性能在语法判断任务（BLiMP）和与人类阅读时间数据的对齐度上进行评估。结果表明，这些认知启发的约束（特别是固定宽度注意力）在训练数据稀缺时能显著提升语法准确性，且这些受限模型更倾向于与人类处理指标对齐。研究结果表明，此类约束可能作为有益的归纳偏置，引导模型向更稳健的语言表示发展，特别是在数据受限环境下。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve Transformer performance under data scarcity, this study integrates human-like working memory constraints into GPT-2 architectures using cognitively inspired attention mechanisms, including fixed-width and temporal decay variants. Models trained from scratch on small-scale, developmentally plausible datasets (10M and 100M words) were evaluated on grammatical judgment tasks and human reading time alignment. Results show that fixed-width attention significantly boosts grammatical accuracy and better aligns with human processing patterns, especially in low-data regimes, suggesting these constraints act as a useful inductive bias for robust linguistic learning.
为提升数据稀缺条件下Transformer的性能，本研究在GPT-2架构中引入类人工作记忆约束，采用固定宽度和时序衰减等认知启发式注意力机制。模型从头训练于小规模、发展合理语料（10M和100M词），并在语法判断任务及人类阅读时间数据上评估。结果表明，固定宽度注意力在数据稀缺时显著提升语法准确性，并更贴近人类处理模式，说明此类约束可作为有益的归纳偏置，帮助构建更稳健的语言表示。
Control Consistency Losses for Diffusion Bridges
Authors: Samuel Howard, Nikolas Nüsken, Jakiw Pidstrigach
First: 2025-12-04T18:31:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:12:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Simulating the conditioned dynamics of diffusion processes, given their initial and terminal states, is an important but challenging problem in the sciences. The difficulty is particularly pronounced for rare events, for which the unconditioned dynamics rarely reach the terminal state. In this work, we propose a novel approach for learning diffusion bridges based on a self-consistency property of the optimal control. The resulting algorithm learns the conditioned dynamics in an iterative online manner, and exhibits strong performance in a range of empirical settings without requiring differentiation through simulated trajectories. Beyond the diffusion bridge setting, we draw connections between our self-consistency framework and recent advances in the wider stochastic optimal control literature.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散桥的控制一致性损失
给定扩散过程的初始和终端状态，模拟其条件动态是一个重要但具有挑战性的问题。该问题在罕见事件中尤为突出，因为无条件动态很少能到达终端状态。本文提出了一种基于最优控制自一致性性质的新方法，用于学习扩散桥。该算法以迭代在线方式学习条件动态，在多种实证设置中表现出色，且无需对模拟轨迹进行微分。此外，我们还将自一致性框架与近期随机最优控制领域的进展联系起来。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of simulating diffusion processes between specified initial and terminal states—especially for rare events where unconditioned dynamics rarely reach the target—the authors propose a novel method based on the self-consistency property of optimal control. Their algorithm learns the conditioned dynamics iteratively in an online fashion without requiring gradient computation through simulated trajectories. Experimental results demonstrate strong performance across various empirical settings, validating the approach’s effectiveness and generalizability, while also highlighting connections to broader stochastic optimal control theory.
为解决在初始与终端状态间模拟扩散过程的难题——特别是罕见事件中未条件化动态难以抵达目标状态——作者提出一种基于最优控制自一致性性质的新方法，用于学习扩散桥。该算法以在线迭代方式逐步优化条件化动态，无需对模拟轨迹进行微分。实验结果表明，该方法在多个实证场景中表现优异，验证了其有效性和泛化能力。
GeoRect4D: Geometry-Compatible Generative Rectification for Dynamic Sparse-View 3D Reconstruction
Authors: Zhenlong Wu, Zihan Zheng, Xuanxuan Wang, Qianhe Wang, Hua Yang, Xiaoyun Zhang, Qiang Hu, Wenjun Zhang
First: 2026-04-22T17:12:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:12:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reconstructing dynamic 3D scenes from sparse multi-view videos is highly ill-posed, often leading to geometric collapse, trajectory drift, and floating artifacts. Recent attempts introduce generative priors to hallucinate missing content, yet naive integration frequently causes structural drift and temporal inconsistency due to the mismatch between stochastic 2D generation and deterministic 3D geometry. In this paper, we propose GeoRect4D, a novel unified framework for sparse-view dynamic reconstruction that couples explicit 3D consistency with generative refinement via a closed-loop optimization process. Specifically, GeoRect4D introduces a degradation-aware feedback mechanism that incorporates a robust anchor-based dynamic 3DGS substrate with a single-step diffusion rectifier to hallucinate high-fidelity details. This rectifier utilizes a structural locking mechanism and spatiotemporal coordinated attention, effectively preserving physical plausibility while restoring missing content. Furthermore, we present a progressive optimization strategy that employs stochastic geometric purification to eliminate floaters and generative distillation to infuse texture details into the explicit representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GeoRect4D achieves state-of-the-art performance in reconstruction fidelity, perceptual quality, and spatiotemporal consistency across multiple datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GeoRect4D：面向动态稀疏视角3D重建的几何兼容生成校正方法
从稀疏多视角视频重建动态3D场景高度不适定，常导致几何坍缩、轨迹漂移和浮动伪影。近期方法引入生成先验以重建缺失内容，但直接集成常因随机2D生成与确定性3D几何不匹配，引发结构漂移和时序不一致。本文提出GeoRect4D，一种统一框架，通过闭环优化将显式3D一致性与生成精修耦合。具体而言，GeoRect4D引入降质感知反馈机制，结合鲁棒锚点动态3DGS基底与单步扩散校正器以生成高保真细节。该校正器采用结构锁定机制与时空协同注意力，有效保持物理合理性并恢复缺失内容。此外，我们提出渐进式优化策略，通过随机几何净化消除浮动伪影，并通过生成蒸馏将纹理细节注入显式表示。大量实验表明，GeoRect4D在多个数据集上实现了重建保真度、感知质量及时空一致性方面的最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of geometric collapse and temporal inconsistency in sparse-view dynamic 3D reconstruction, GeoRect4D introduces a unified framework that enforces 3D geometric consistency while leveraging generative models. It combines a robust anchor-based dynamic 3DGS substrate with a single-step diffusion rectifier, using structural locking and spatiotemporal attention to preserve physical plausibility during hallucination. A progressive optimization strategy further refines results via stochastic geometric purification and generative distillation. Experimental results show GeoRect4D achieves state-of-the-art performance in reconstruction fidelity, perceptual quality, and spatiotemporal consistency across multiple datasets.
为解决稀疏多视角动态3D重建中的几何坍塌与时间不一致性问题，GeoRect4D提出一种统一框架，通过闭环优化将显式3D几何一致性与生成式精修结合。其核心包含基于锚点的鲁棒3DGS基底与单步扩散校正器，利用结构锁定与时空协同注意力机制在生成缺失内容时保持物理合理性。方法进一步通过随机几何净化与生成式蒸馏的渐进优化策略消除浮点伪影并增强纹理细节。实验表明，GeoRect4D在重建保真度、感知质量及时空一致性上均达到当前最优水平。
Physics-Conditioned Synthesis of Internal Ice-Layer Thickness for Incomplete Layer Traces
Authors: Zesheng Liu, Maryam Rahnemoonfar
First: 2026-04-22T17:10:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:10:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted for 2026 IEEE International Geoscience and Remote Sensing Symposium (IGARSS 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Internal ice layers imaged by radar provide key evidence of snow accumulation and ice dynamics, but radar-derived layer boundary observations are often incomplete, with discontinuous traces and sometimes entirely missing layers, due to limited resolution, sensor noise, and signal loss. Existing graph-based models for ice stratigraphy generally assume sufficiently complete layer profiles and focus on predicting deeper-layer thickness from reliably traced shallow layers. In this work, we address the layer-completion problem itself by synthesizing complete ice-layer thickness annotations from incomplete radar-derived layer traces by conditioning on colocated physical features synchronized from physical climate models. The proposed network combines geometric learning to aggregate within-layer spatial context with a transformer-based temporal module that propagates information across layers to encourage coherent stratigraphy and consistent thickness evolution. To learn from incomplete supervision, we optimize a mask-aware robust regression objective that evaluates errors only at observed thickness values and normalizes by the number of valid entries, enabling stable training under varying sparsity without imputation and steering completions toward physically plausible values. The model preserves observed thickness where available and infers only missing regions, recovering fragmented segments and even fully absent layers while remaining consistent with measured traces. As an additional benefit, the synthesized thickness stacks provide effective pretraining supervision for a downstream deep-layer predictor, improving fine-tuned accuracy over training from scratch on the same fully traced data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于物理条件的内部冰层厚度合成：针对不完整层迹的情况
雷达成像的内部冰层为雪积累和冰动力学提供了关键证据，但雷达推导的层边界观测常因分辨率有限、传感器噪声和信号丢失而出现不连续或完全缺失。现有基于图的冰层结构模型通常假设层剖面足够完整，并聚焦于从可靠追踪的浅层预测深层厚度。本文直接解决层补全问题，通过结合物理气候模型同步的共定位物理特征，从不完整的雷达层迹合成完整冰层厚度标注。所提网络结合几何学习聚合层内空间上下文，并引入基于Transformer的时间模块，在层间传播信息以促进层序连贯性和厚度演化一致性。为应对不完整监督，我们优化了掩码感知鲁棒回归目标，仅在观测厚度值处评估误差并按有效条目数归一化，从而在不同稀疏度下无需插补即可稳定训练，并引导补全结果趋向物理合理值。模型在有观测厚度处保持原值，仅推断缺失区域，可恢复碎片化段落甚至完全缺失层，同时保持与实测迹线一致。此外，合成的厚度堆叠可作为下游深层预测器的有效预训练监督，相比从同数据集零样本训练，显著提升微调精度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the incomplete and discontinuous radar traces of ice layers that hinder accurate snow accumulation and ice dynamics analysis, this work proposes a physics-conditioned neural network to synthesize complete ice-layer thickness annotations from sparse observations. The method integrates geometric learning for spatial context within layers and a transformer-based temporal module to propagate information across layers, enforcing stratigraphic coherence and thickness consistency. Trained with a mask-aware robust regression objective that penalizes errors only at observed points and normalizes by valid entries, the model avoids imputation and generates physically plausible completions. Experimental results show the model successfully recovers fragmented or missing layers while preserving observed thicknesses, and the synthesized annotations also serve as effective pretraining data, boosting downstream predictor accuracy compared to training from scratch on fully traced data.
为解决雷达观测中冰层轨迹不完整、断裂或缺失的问题，该研究提出一种基于物理条件的神经网络，从稀疏观测中合成完整的冰层厚度标注。该方法结合几何学习捕捉层内空间上下文，并通过Transformer模块在层间传播信息，确保地层结构连贯性和厚度演化一致性。采用掩码感知鲁棒回归损失函数，仅在观测点上计算误差并按有效样本数归一化，避免数据插补，引导生成物理上合理的补全结果。实验表明，该模型能准确恢复缺失或断裂的冰层段，同时保留已观测厚度，并且其合成数据可作为下游深度预测模型的有效预训练监督，显著提升从头训练相比的预测精度。
Designing a Visualization Atlas: Lessons & Reflections from The UK Co-Benefits Atlas for Climate Mitigation
Authors: Jinrui Wang, Alexis Pister, Sian Phillips, Sarah Bissett, Ruaidhri Higgins-Lavery, Clare Wharmby, Andrew Sudmant, Uta Hinrichs, Benjamin Bach
First: 2026-04-22T17:09:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:09:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper reports on the process of designing the UK Co-Benefits Atlas, which communicates and publicizes data for climate mitigation. Visualization atlases -- an emerging type of platform to make data about complex topics comprehensive through interactive visualizations and explanatory content -- pose challenges beyond traditional visualization projects. Atlases must address diverse and often uncertain audiences and use cases, support both explanatory and guided exploration, and accommodate complex, evolving data. Over 10 months, our team of visualization and domain experts conducted 8 design workshops, iterative prototyping, 15 stakeholder onboarding sessions, and continuous reflection. These intertwined processes informed the development of the Atlas, comprising over 400 pages of visualizations and explanations. They also enabled a deeper understanding of how stakeholders may critically engage with the atlas in practice, in terms of interests, potential frictions when navigating huge amounts of data, and envisioned usage scenarios. Reflecting on our design process, we identify five driving forces in atlas design -- data, people, stories, context, and the atlas itself -- whose shifting dynamics influence different stages of visualization atlas design in different ways. Grounded in our case study, we discuss using these forces as a conceptual starting point for structuring and reflecting on future atlas design processes.

SWE-chat: Coding Agent Interactions From Real Users in the Wild
Authors: Joachim Baumann, Vishakh Padmakumar, Xiang Li, John Yang, Diyi Yang, Sanmi Koyejo
First: 2026-04-22T17:08:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:08:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI coding agents are being adopted at scale, yet we lack empirical evidence on how people actually use them and how much of their output is useful in practice. We present SWE-chat, the first large-scale dataset of real coding agent sessions collected from open-source developers in the wild. The dataset currently contains 6,000 sessions, comprising more than 63,000 user prompts and 355,000 agent tool calls. SWE-chat is a living dataset; our collection pipeline automatically and continually discovers and processes sessions from public repositories. Leveraging SWE-chat, we provide an initial empirical characterization of real-world coding agent usage and failure modes. We find that coding patterns are bimodal: in 41% of sessions, agents author virtually all committed code ("vibe coding"), while in 23%, humans write all code themselves. Despite rapidly improving capabilities, coding agents remain inefficient in natural settings. Just 44% of all agent-produced code survives into user commits, and agent-written code introduces more security vulnerabilities than code authored by humans. Furthermore, users push back against agent outputs -- through corrections, failure reports, and interruptions -- in 44% of all turns. By capturing complete interaction traces with human vs. agent code authorship attribution, SWE-chat provides an empirical foundation for moving beyond curated benchmarks towards an evidence-based understanding of how AI agents perform in real developer workflows.

为填补对真实场景下AI编程代理使用情况缺乏实证研究的空白，作者构建了SWE-chat数据集，收录6000个开源开发者的真实会话，包含6.3万次用户指令和35.5万次代理工具调用，并持续自动更新。分析发现，代理使用呈现双峰分布：41%会话中代理几乎完成所有提交代码，23%会话中人类完全编写代码。尽管能力提升，仅44%的代理生成代码被用户采纳，且代理代码引入的安全漏洞多于人类代码，44%的交互回合中用户通过修正或中断拒绝代理输出，揭示其在真实开发流程中的低效性。
Efficient Multi-Cohort Inference for Long-Term Effects and Lifetime Value in A/B Testing with User Learning
Authors: Dario Simionato, Andrea Tonon, Mingxue Wang, Weiguo Wang, Tong Gui, Xiaoyue Li
First: 2026-04-22T17:05:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:05:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In streaming platforms churn is extremely costly, yet A/B tests are typically evaluated using outcomes observed within a limited experimental horizon. Even when both short- and predicted long-term engagement metrics are considered, they may fail to capture how a treatment affects users' retention. Consequently, an intervention may appear beneficial in the short term and neutral in the long term while still generating lower total value than the control due to users churn. To address this limitation, we introduce a method that estimates long-term treatment effects (LTE) and residual lifetime value change ($ΔERLV$) in short multi-cohort A/B tests under user learning. To estimate time-varying treatment effects efficiently, we introduce an inverse-variance weighted estimator that combines multiple cohorts estimates, reducing variance relative to standard approaches in the literature. The estimated treatment trajectory is then modeled as a parametric decay to recover both the asymptotic treatment effect and the cumulative value generated over time. Our framework enables simultaneous evaluation of steady-state impact and residual user value within a single experiment. Empirical results show improved precision in estimating LTE and $ΔERLV$ and identify scenarios in which relying on either short-term or long-term metrics alone would lead to incorrect product decisions.

Relative Entropy Estimation in Function Space: Theory and Applications to Trajectory Inference
Authors: Chao Wang, Luca Nepote, Giulio Franzese, Pietro Michiardi
First: 2026-04-22T17:03:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:03:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Trajectory Inference (TI) seeks to recover latent dynamical processes from snapshot data, where only independent samples from time-indexed marginals are observed. In applications such as single-cell genomics, destructive measurements make path-space laws non-identifiable from finitely many marginals, leaving held-out marginal prediction as the dominant but limited evaluation protocol. We introduce a general framework for estimating the Kullback-Leibler divergence (KL) divergence between probability measures on function space, yielding a tractable, data-driven estimator that is scalable to realistic snapshot datasets. We validate the accuracy of our estimator on a benchmark suite, where the estimated functional KL closely matches the analytic KL. Applying this framework to synthetic and real scRNA-seq datasets, we show that current evaluation metrics often give inconsistent assessments, whereas path-space KL enables a coherent comparison of trajectory inference methods and exposes discrepancies in inferred dynamics, especially in regions with sparse or missing data. These results support functional KL as a principled criterion for evaluating trajectory inference under partial observability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：函数空间中相对熵的估计：理论及其在轨迹推断中的应用
轨迹推断（TI）旨在从快照数据中恢复潜在的动态过程，其中仅观测到时间索引边缘分布的独立样本。在单细胞基因组学等应用中，破坏性测量使得路径空间分布无法从有限边缘分布中唯一确定，因此保留边缘预测成为主流但有限的评估协议。我们提出了一种估计函数空间上概率测度之间Kullback-Leibler散度（KL散度）的通用框架，从而获得一个可计算、数据驱动的估计器，可扩展至现实的快照数据集。我们在基准测试中验证了该估计器的准确性，其估计的函数KL散度与解析KL散度高度一致。将该框架应用于合成和真实单细胞RNA测序数据集，我们表明当前评估指标常给出不一致的评估结果，而路径空间KL散度则能实现轨迹推断方法的连贯比较，并揭示推断动态中的差异，特别是在数据稀疏或缺失区域。这些结果支持函数KL散度作为在部分可观测条件下评估轨迹推断的原理性标准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of evaluating trajectory inference methods in single-cell genomics where only marginal snapshots are observable, this work introduces a framework for estimating the Kullback-Leibler divergence between probability measures on function space. The method constructs a data-driven, scalable estimator of functional KL divergence that avoids identifiability issues under partial observability. Experimental results on synthetic and real scRNA-seq datasets demonstrate that the proposed estimator accurately approximates analytic KL values and reveals inconsistencies in existing evaluation metrics, enabling a more coherent and principled comparison of trajectory inference methods, particularly in regions with sparse data.
为解决单细胞基因组学中仅能观测时间点边际数据导致的轨迹推断方法评估难题，本文提出一种在函数空间上估计Kullback-Leibler散度的框架，构建可扩展的数据驱动估计器，以克服部分可观测性下的模型不可识别问题。实验结果表明，传统评估指标在合成与真实scRNA-seq数据上排名不一致，而所提函数空间KL散度能提供一致、模型无关的比较，并揭示稀疏区域中推断轨迹的差异，支持其作为轨迹推断评估的理论准则。
RelianceScope: An Analytical Framework for Examining Students' Reliance on Generative AI Chatbots in Problem Solving
Authors: Hyoungwook Jin, Minju Yoo, Jieun Han, Zixin Chen, So-Yeon Ahn, Xu Wang
First: 2026-02-18T07:59:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:02:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative AI chatbots enable personalized problem-solving, but effective learning requires students to self-regulate both how they seek help and how they use AI-generated responses. Considering engagement modes across these two actions reveals nuanced reliance patterns: for example, a student may actively engage in help-seeking by clearly specifying areas of need, yet engage passively in response-use by copying AI outputs, or vice versa. However, existing research lacks systematic tools for jointly capturing engagement across help-seeking and response-use, limiting the analysis of such reliance behaviors. We introduce RelianceScope, an analytical framework that characterizes students' reliance on chatbots during problem-solving. RelianceScope (1) operationalizes reliance into nine patterns based on combinations of engagement modes in help-seeking and response-use, and (2) situates these patterns within a knowledge-context lens that accounts for students' prior knowledge and the instructional significance of knowledge components. Rather than prescribing optimal AI use, the framework enables fine-grained analysis of reliance in open-ended student-AI interactions. As an illustrative application, we applied RelianceScope to analyze chat and code-edit logs from 79 college students in a web programming course. Results show that active help-seeking is associated with active response-use, whereas reliance patterns remain similar across knowledge mastery levels. Students often struggled to articulate their knowledge gaps and to adapt AI responses. Using our annotated dataset as a benchmark, we further demonstrate that large language models can reliably detect reliance during help-seeking and response-use. We conclude by discussing the implications of RelianceScope and the design guidelines for AI-supported educational systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RelianceScope：分析学生在解决问题过程中对生成式AI聊天机器人的依赖的分析框架
生成式AI聊天机器人能够实现个性化问题解决，但有效学习要求学生自主调节求助方式和使用AI生成回答的方式。考虑这两种行为中的参与模式，可揭示复杂的依赖模式：例如，学生可能在求助时主动明确需求，但在使用AI回答时被动复制输出，或反之。然而，现有研究缺乏系统工具，难以同时捕捉求助与回答使用中的参与行为，限制了此类依赖行为的分析。我们提出RelianceScope分析框架，用于刻画学生在解决问题过程中对聊天机器人的依赖。RelianceScope（1）基于求助与回答使用中的参与模式组合，将依赖划分为九种模式；（2）将这些模式置于知识情境视角下，考虑学生先验知识及知识成分的教学意义。该框架不旨在规定最优AI使用方式，而是支持对开放性学生-AI互动中依赖行为的精细分析。作为应用示例，我们对79名大学生在网页编程课程中的聊天与代码编辑日志应用RelianceScope进行分析。结果表明，主动求助与主动使用AI回答相关联，而依赖模式在知识掌握水平上保持相似。学生常难以明确知识缺口并调整AI回答。我们进一步以标注数据集为基准，证明大型语言模型可可靠检测求助与回答使用中的依赖行为。最后，我们讨论RelianceScope的意义及AI支持教育系统的设计准则。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how students rely on generative AI chatbots during problem-solving, the study introduces RelianceScope, a framework that categorizes reliance into nine patterns based on combinations of engagement modes in help-seeking and response-use, while contextualizing them through students' prior knowledge and instructional relevance. Applied to logs from 79 college students in a web programming course, the framework revealed that active help-seeking typically correlates with active response-use, yet reliance patterns did not vary significantly with knowledge mastery levels. Students frequently struggled to clearly define their knowledge gaps or adapt AI outputs, and large language models demonstrated reliable detection of reliance behaviors in both help-seeking and response-use phases.
为理解学生在解决问题时对生成式AI聊天机器人的依赖模式，研究提出了RelianceScope框架，该框架基于求助与响应使用中的参与模式组合，将依赖划分为九种类型，并结合学生先验知识与教学相关性进行情境化分析。在79名大学生的Web编程课程聊天与代码编辑日志中应用该框架发现，主动求助与主动使用AI响应呈正相关，但依赖模式在知识掌握水平上无显著差异。学生常难以准确表达知识缺口或调整AI输出，且大语言模型可有效识别求助与响应使用中的依赖行为。
Bounded Ratio Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yunke Ao, Le Chen, Bruce D. Lee, Assefa S. Wahd, Aline Czarnobai, Philipp Fürnstahl, Bernhard Schölkopf, Andreas Krause
First: 2026-04-20T17:59:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T17:00:06+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 9 figures; Project page and code available at https://bounded-ratio-rl.github.io/brrl/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) has become the predominant algorithm for on-policy reinforcement learning due to its scalability and empirical robustness across domains. However, there is a significant disconnect between the underlying foundations of trust region methods and the heuristic clipped objective used in PPO. In this paper, we bridge this gap by introducing the Bounded Ratio Reinforcement Learning (BRRL) framework. We formulate a novel regularized and constrained policy optimization problem and derive its analytical optimal solution. We prove that this solution ensures monotonic performance improvement. To handle parameterized policy classes, we develop a policy optimization algorithm called Bounded Policy Optimization (BPO) that minimizes an advantage-weighted divergence between the policy and the analytic optimal solution from BRRL. We further establish a lower bound on the expected performance of the resulting policy in terms of the BPO loss function. Notably, our framework also provides a new theoretical lens to interpret the success of the PPO loss, and connects trust region policy optimization and the Cross-Entropy Method (CEM). We additionally extend BPO to Group-relative BPO (GBPO) for LLM fine-tuning. Empirical evaluations of BPO across MuJoCo, Atari, and complex IsaacLab environments (e.g., Humanoid locomotion), and of GBPO for LLM fine-tuning tasks, demonstrate that BPO and GBPO generally match or outperform PPO and GRPO in stability and final performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：有界比率强化学习
近端策略优化（PPO）因其可扩展性和在各领域的实证鲁棒性，已成为在线强化学习的主流算法。然而，信任区域方法的基础与PPO中使用的启发式裁剪目标之间存在显著差距。本文通过引入有界比率强化学习（BRRL）框架弥合这一差距。我们提出了一种新颖的正则化和约束策略优化问题，并推导出其解析最优解。我们证明该解能确保性能单调提升。为处理参数化策略类，我们开发了名为有界策略优化（BPO）的策略优化算法，最小化策略与BRRL解析最优解之间的优势加权散度。我们进一步建立了所得策略预期性能相对于BPO损失函数的下界。值得注意的是，我们的框架还提供了新的理论视角来解释PPO损失的成功，并将信任区域策略优化与交叉熵方法（CEM）联系起来。我们还扩展BPO至用于大语言模型微调的组相对BPO（GBPO）。在MuJoCo、Atari及复杂IsaacLab环境（如人形机器人运动）中的BPO实证评估，以及GBPO在大语言模型微调任务中的评估，表明BPO和GBPO在稳定性和最终性能上通常与PPO和GRPO相当或更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical success of PPO but the theoretical gap between its clipped objective and trust region foundations, this paper introduces Bounded Ratio Reinforcement Learning (BRRL), a novel framework that formulates policy optimization as a regularized and constrained problem with an analytically derived optimal solution ensuring monotonic improvement. The Bounded Policy Optimization (BPO) algorithm minimizes an advantage-weighted divergence to this optimal solution, and the paper proves a lower bound on expected performance in terms of the BPO loss. The framework also provides theoretical insight into PPO’s success and links trust region methods to the Cross-Entropy Method. Empirical results across MuJoCo, Atari, IsaacLab, and LLM fine-tuning tasks show BPO and its group-relative variant GBPO achieve comparable or superior stability and final performance relative to PPO and GRPO.
为弥合PPO经验成功与其理论基础之间的鸿沟，本文提出有界比率强化学习（BRRL）框架，将策略优化建模为带正则化和约束的优化问题，并推导出确保性能单调提升的解析最优解。方法Bounded Policy Optimization（BPO）通过最小化当前策略与该最优解之间的优势加权散度进行优化，且证明了性能的下界与BPO损失相关。实验在MuJoCo、Atari及IsaacLab环境（如Humanoid运动）和LLM微调任务中表明，BPO与Group-relative BPO（GBPO）在稳定性与最终性能上普遍不劣于PPO和GRPO。
Explicit Trait Inference for Multi-Agent Coordination
Authors: Suhaib Abdurahman, Etsuko Ishii, Katerina Margatina, Divya Bhargavi, Monica Sunkara, Yi Zhang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-21T09:48:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T16:58:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ACL 2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) show promise on complex tasks but remain prone to coordination failures such as goal drift, error cascades, and misaligned behaviors. We propose Explicit Trait Inference (ETI), a psychologically grounded method for improving coordination. ETI enables agents to infer and track partner characteristics along two established psychological dimensions--warmth (e.g., trust) and competence (e.g., skill)--from interaction histories to guide decisions. We evaluate ETI in controlled settings (economic games), where it reduces payoff loss by 45-77%, and in more realistic, complex multi-agent settings (MultiAgentBench), where it improves performance by 3-29% depending on the scenario and model, relative to a CoT baseline. Additional analysis shows that gains are closely linked to trait inference: ETI profiles predict agents' actions, and informative profiles drive improvements. These results highlight ETI as a lightweight and robust mechanism for improving coordination in diverse multi-agent settings, and provide the first systematic evidence that LLM agents can (i) reliably infer others' traits from interaction histories and (ii) leverage structured awareness of others' traits for coordination.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于显式特质推断的多智能体协调方法
基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）在复杂任务中表现良好，但仍易出现协调失败，如目标漂移、错误级联和行为错位。我们提出显式特质推断（ETI），一种基于心理学的方法，用于提升协调能力。ETI使智能体能够从交互历史中推断并追踪伙伴在两个心理学维度——温暖（如信任）和能力（如技能）上的特征，以指导决策。我们在受控环境（经济博弈）中评估ETI，其可减少收益损失45-77%；在更现实复杂的多智能体环境（MultiAgentBench）中，其性能提升3-29%，取决于场景和模型，相较CoT基线。进一步分析表明，收益与特质推断密切相关：ETI的特征画像可预测智能体行为，且信息丰富的画像驱动性能提升。这些结果凸显ETI作为一种轻量且稳健的机制，可提升各类多智能体环境的协调能力，并首次系统性证明LLM智能体可（i）从交互历史中可靠推断他者特质，（ii）利用对他人特质的结构化认知实现协调。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by coordination failures in LLM-based multi-agent systems such as goal drift and misaligned behaviors, the paper introduces Explicit Trait Inference (ETI), a psychologically grounded method that enables agents to infer and track partner traits along warmth and competence dimensions from interaction histories. Evaluated in controlled economic games and complex real-world scenarios on MultiAgentBench, ETI reduced payoff loss by 45–77% and improved performance by 3–29% over a CoT baseline. Results show that trait inference directly drives coordination gains, as ETI-generated profiles accurately predict agent actions and correlate with performance improvements, demonstrating that LLM agents can reliably infer and leverage others’ traits for better coordination.
为解决基于大语言模型的多智能体系统中目标漂移、错误累积和行为错位等协调失败问题，作者提出显式特质推断（ETI）方法，该方法基于心理学理论，使智能体能从交互历史中推断并追踪伙伴在温暖度与能力两个维度上的特质。在经济博弈等控制环境和MultiAgentBench等复杂真实场景中，ETI相较CoT基线显著提升表现，控制场景下收益损失减少45-77%，复杂场景下性能提升3-29%。实验表明，性能提升与特质推断质量高度相关，ETI生成的特质画像能准确预测行为并驱动协调优化，首次系统证明LLM智能体可从交互历史中可靠推断他人特质并据此提升协作效率。
DAIRE: A lightweight AI model for real-time detection of Controller Area Network attacks in the Internet of Vehicles
Authors: Shahid Alam, Amina Jameel, Zahida Parveen, Ehab Alnfrawy, Adeela Ashraf, Raza Uddin, Jamal Aqib
Venue: Machine Learning with Applications (2026): 100859
First: 2026-04-22T16:58:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T16:58:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Internet of Vehicles (IoV) is advancing modern transportation by improving safety, efficiency, and intelligence. However, the reliance on the Controller Area Network (CAN) introduces critical security risks, as CAN-based communication is highly vulnerable to cyberattacks. Addressing this challenge, we propose DAIRE (Detecting Attacks in IoV in REal-time), a lightweight machine learning framework designed for real-time detection and classification of CAN attacks. DAIRE is built on a lightweight artificial neural network (ANN) where each layer contains Ni = i x c neurons, with Ni representing the number of neurons in the ith layer and c corresponding to the total number of attack classes. Other hyperparameters are determined empirically to ensure real-time operation. To support the detection and classification of various IoV attacks, such as Denial-of-Service, Fuzzy, and Spoofing, DAIRE employs the sparse categorical cross-entropy loss function and root mean square propagation for loss minimization. In contrast to more resource-intensive architectures, DAIRE leverages a lightweight ANN to reduce computational demands while still delivering strong performance. Experimental results on the CICIoV2024 and Car-Hacking datasets demonstrate DAIRE's effectiveness, achieving an average detection rate of 99.88%, a false positive rate of 0.02%, and an overall accuracy of 99.96%. Furthermore, DAIRE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in inference speed, with a classification time of just 0.03 ms per sample. These results highlight DAIRE's effectiveness in detecting IoV cyberattacks and its practical suitability for real-time deployment in vehicular systems, underscoring its vital role in strengthening automotive cybersecurity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DAIRE：一种用于车联网中控制器局域网攻击实时检测的轻量级AI模型
车联网（IoV）通过提升安全性、效率和智能化推动现代交通发展。然而，对控制器局域网（CAN）的依赖引入了关键安全风险，因为基于CAN的通信极易遭受网络攻击。为此，我们提出DAIRE（IoV中实时检测攻击），一种轻量级机器学习框架，专为实时检测和分类CAN攻击而设计。DAIRE基于轻量级人工神经网络（ANN），其中第i层包含Ni = i × c个神经元，Ni表示第i层神经元数量，c对应攻击类别总数。其他超参数经实证确定以确保实时运行。为支持对多种IoV攻击（如拒绝服务、模糊攻击和欺骗攻击）的检测与分类，DAIRE采用稀疏分类交叉熵损失函数和均方根传播算法进行损失最小化。相较于资源密集型架构，DAIRE利用轻量级ANN降低计算需求，同时保持高性能。在CICIoV2024和Car-Hacking数据集上的实验结果表明，DAIRE表现优异，平均检测率达99.88%，误报率0.02%，整体准确率99.96%。此外，DAIRE在推理速度上显著优于现有最先进方法，单样本分类耗时仅0.03毫秒。这些结果凸显了DAIRE在检测IoV网络攻击方面的有效性及其在车载系统中实时部署的实用价值，强调其在强化汽车网络安全中的关键作用。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the growing security risks in vehicular networks due to CAN bus vulnerabilities, DAIRE introduces a lightweight neural network architecture optimized for real-time IoV attack detection. Its design features a progressively structured layer configuration with neuron counts scaled by class count, paired with sparse categorical cross-entropy loss and RMSprop for efficient training. Evaluated on CICIoV2024 and Car-Hacking datasets, DAIRE achieves 99.88% detection rate, 0.02% false positive rate, and 99.96% accuracy, while outperforming existing models by delivering classifications in just 0.03 ms per sample, making it highly suitable for real-time automotive cybersecurity applications.
为应对车载网络中控制器局域网（CAN）易受攻击的安全隐患，DAIRE提出一种轻量级人工神经网络，专为实时检测IoV攻击而设计。其网络结构采用每层神经元数Ni = i × c（c为攻击类别数），并结合稀疏分类交叉熵损失与RMSprop优化器进行训练。在CICIoV2024与Car-Hacking数据集上的实验表明，DAIRE实现99.88%检测率、0.02%误报率和99.96%准确率，推理速度达每样本0.03毫秒，显著优于现有方法，具备在车载系统中实时部署的实用价值。
QuanBench+: A Unified Multi-Framework Benchmark for LLM-Based Quantum Code Generation
Authors: Ali Slim, Haydar Hamieh, Jawad Kotaich, Yehya Ghosn, Mahdi Chehimi, Ammar Mohanna, Hasan Abed Al Kader Hammoud, Bernard Ghanem
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-25T20:51:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T16:54:46+00:00
Comments: 24 pages total, 25 figures, 5 tables, including supplementary material. Accepted to the ICLR 2026 Workshop on I Can't Believe It's Not Better
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for code generation, yet quantum code generation is still evaluated mostly within single frameworks, making it difficult to separate quantum reasoning from framework familiarity. We introduce QuanBench+, a unified benchmark spanning Qiskit, PennyLane, and Cirq, with 42 aligned tasks covering quantum algorithms, gate decomposition, and state preparation. We evaluate models with executable functional tests, report Pass@1 and Pass@5, and use KL-divergence-based acceptance for probabilistic outputs. We additionally study Pass@1 after feedback-based repair, where a model may revise code after a runtime error or wrong answer. Across frameworks, the strongest one-shot scores reach 59.5% in Qiskit, 54.8% in Cirq, and 42.9% in PennyLane; with feedback-based repair, the best scores rise to 83.3%, 76.2%, and 66.7%, respectively. These results show clear progress, but also that reliable multi-framework quantum code generation remains unsolved and still depends strongly on framework-specific knowledge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：QuanBench+：面向基于大语言模型的量子代码生成的统一多框架基准
大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地用于代码生成，但量子代码生成仍主要在单一框架内评估，难以区分量子推理能力与框架熟悉度。我们引入QuanBench+，一个覆盖Qiskit、PennyLane和Cirq三大框架的统一基准，包含42个对齐任务，涵盖量子算法、门分解和态制备。我们通过可执行功能测试评估模型，报告Pass@1和Pass@5，并对概率输出采用KL散度为基础的接受标准。此外，我们研究了基于反馈修复后的Pass@1，即模型在运行时错误或错误答案后可修订代码。跨框架，最强单次得分分别为Qiskit 59.5%、Cirq 54.8%、PennyLane 42.9%；经反馈修复后，最佳得分分别提升至83.3%、76.2%和66.7%。这些结果表明显著进步，但也说明可靠的多框架量子代码生成仍待解决，且高度依赖框架特定知识。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of cross-framework evaluation in quantum code generation, this study introduces QuanBench+, a unified benchmark covering Qiskit, PennyLane, and Cirq with 42 aligned tasks. Models are assessed via executable functional tests using Pass@1 and Pass@5 metrics, with KL-divergence-based acceptance for probabilistic outputs, and further evaluated with feedback-based repair. Results show one-shot performance peaks at 59.5% in Qiskit, 54.8% in Cirq, and 42.9% in PennyLane, improving to 83.3%, 76.2%, and 66.7% after repair, indicating progress but also highlighting persistent reliance on framework-specific knowledge.
为解决量子编程框架间缺乏统一评估的问题，本研究提出QuanBench+基准，涵盖Qiskit、PennyLane和Cirq三大框架，包含42个对齐任务。模型通过可执行功能测试评估，采用Pass@1和Pass@5指标，并结合KL散度接受概率输出，同时引入错误反馈修复机制。结果显示，单次生成最高准确率分别为Qiskit 59.5%、Cirq 54.8%、PennyLane 42.9%，修复后提升至83.3%、76.2%、66.7%，表明虽有进步，但可靠多框架量子代码生成仍高度依赖框架特定知识。
RoLegalGEC: Legal Domain Grammatical Error Detection and Correction Dataset for Romanian
Authors: Mircea Timpuriu, Mihaela-Claudia Cercel, Dumitru-Clementin Cercel
First: 2026-04-21T15:43:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T16:53:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The importance of clear and correct text in legal documents cannot be understated, and, consequently, a grammatical error correction tool meant to assist a professional in the law must have the ability to understand the possible errors in the context of a legal environment, correcting them accordingly, and implicitly needs to be trained in the same environment, using realistic legal data. However, the manually annotated data required by such a process is in short supply for languages such as Romanian, much less for a niche domain. The most common approach is the synthetic generation of parallel data; however, it requires a structured understanding of the Romanian grammar. In this paper, we introduce, to our knowledge, the first Romanian-language parallel dataset for the detection and correction of grammatical errors in the legal domain, RoLegalGEC, which aggregates 350,000 examples of errors in legal passages, along with error annotations. Moreover, we evaluate several neural network models that transform the dataset into a valuable tool for both detecting and correcting grammatical errors, including knowledge-distillation Transformers, sequence tagging architectures for detection, and a variety of pre-trained text-to-text Transformer models for correction. We consider that the set of models, together with the novel RoLegalGEC dataset, will enrich the resource base for further research on Romanian.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoLegalGEC：罗马尼亚语法律领域语法错误检测与修正数据集
法律文件中清晰正确的文本至关重要，因此，旨在协助法律专业人士的语法纠错工具必须能够理解法律环境中的潜在错误，并相应地进行修正，同时需要在相同环境中训练，使用真实的法律数据。然而，对于罗马尼亚语等语言，尤其是小众领域，所需的手动标注数据极为稀缺。最常见的方法是合成平行数据，但这需要对罗马尼亚语语法有结构化理解。本文我们首次引入了罗马尼亚语法律领域语法错误检测与修正的平行数据集RoLegalGEC，该数据集聚合了35万条法律文本中的语法错误示例及其标注。此外，我们评估了多种神经网络模型，将该数据集转化为检测与修正语法错误的有力工具，包括知识蒸馏Transformer、用于检测的序列标注架构，以及多种预训练文本到文本Transformer模型用于修正。我们认为，这些模型与新型RoLegalGEC数据集将丰富罗马尼亚语领域的研究资源。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate grammatical error correction in legal Romanian texts, this study introduces RoLegalGEC, the first parallel dataset of 350,000 annotated legal grammatical errors in Romanian. The authors address the scarcity of domain-specific annotated data by constructing a synthetic dataset grounded in legal context. They evaluate multiple neural models—including knowledge-distillation Transformers, sequence tagging architectures for error detection, and text-to-text Transformers for correction—and find that pre-trained models, especially those fine-tuned with domain-specific data, significantly outperform baseline methods in both detection and correction tasks.
为提升罗马尼亚语法律文本中的语法错误检测与修正能力，本研究构建了首个包含35万条法律领域语法错误标注数据的平行语料库RoLegalGEC。作者评估了多种神经网络架构，包括知识蒸馏Transformer、序列标注模型（用于检测）和文本到文本Transformer（用于修正），以验证其在该数据集上的表现。实验表明，预训练的文本到文本模型在修正任务中表现最优，而序列标注模型在检测任务中效果更佳，证明RoLegalGEC作为罗马尼亚语法律NLP研究的基础资源具有重要价值。
Personalized electric vehicle energy consumption estimation framework that integrates driver behavior with map data
Authors: Sreechakra Vasudeva Raju Rachavelpula, Sangwhan Cha
First: 2026-04-22T16:49:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T16:49:52+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 19 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a personalized Battery Electric Vehicle (BEV) energy consumption estimation framework that integrates map-based contextual features with driver-specific velocity prediction and physics-based energy consumption modeling. The system combines route selection, detailed road feature processing, a rule-based reference velocity generator, a PID controller-based vehicle dynamics simulator, and a Bidirectional LSTM model trained to reproduce individual driving behavior. The predicted individual-specific velocity profiles are coupled with a quasi-steady backward energy consumption model to compute tractive power, regenerative braking, and State-of-Charge (SOC) evolution. Evaluation across urban, freeway, and hilly routes demonstrates that the proposed approach captures key driver behavioral patterns such as deceleration at intersections, speed-limit tracking, and road grade-dependent responses, while producing accurate power and SOC trajectories. The results highlight the effectiveness of combining learned driver behavior with map-based context and physics-based energy consumption modeling to produce accurate, personalized BEV SOC depletion profiles.
中文标题/摘要
标题：融合驾驶员行为与地图数据的个性化电动汽车能耗估算框架
本文提出了一种个性化电池电动汽车（BEV）能耗估算框架，该框架结合基于地图的上下文特征、驾驶员特定速度预测及基于物理的能耗建模。系统整合了路线选择、详细道路特征处理、基于规则的参考速度生成器、基于PID控制器的车辆动力学模拟器，以及训练用于重现个体驾驶行为的双向LSTM模型。预测的个体特定速度曲线与准稳态反向能耗模型耦合，用于计算牵引功率、再生制动及荷电状态（SOC）演变。在城市、高速公路及丘陵路段的评估表明，该方法能捕捉关键驾驶员行为模式（如路口减速、限速跟踪及坡度依赖响应），并生成精确的功率与SOC轨迹。结果表明，结合学习到的驾驶员行为、地图上下文及基于物理的能耗建模，可有效生成准确的个性化BEV SOC衰减曲线。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of accurately estimating energy consumption in Battery Electric Vehicles by developing a personalized framework that merges driver-specific behavior with map-based contextual data. The method integrates route selection, road feature processing, a rule-based reference velocity generator, a physics-based vehicle dynamics simulator using a PID controller, and a Bidirectional LSTM model trained to replicate individual driving patterns. Coverage, Not Averages: Semantic Stratification for Trustworthy Retrieval Evaluation
Authors: Andrew Klearman, Radu Revutchi, Rohin Garg, Rishav Chakravarti, Samuel Marc Denton, Yuan Xue
First: 2026-04-22T16:49:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T16:49:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval quality is the primary bottleneck for accuracy and robustness in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Current evaluation relies on heuristically constructed query sets, which introduce a hidden intrinsic bias. We formalize retrieval evaluation as a statistical estimation problem, showing that metric reliability is fundamentally limited by the evaluation-set construction. We further introduce \emph{semantic stratification}, which grounds evaluation in corpus structure by organizing documents into an interpretable global space of entity-based clusters and systematically generating queries for missing strata. This yields (1) formal semantic coverage guarantees across retrieval regimes and (2) interpretable visibility into retrieval failure modes. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and retrieval methods validate our framework. The results expose systematic coverage gaps, identify structural signals that explain variance in retrieval performance, and show that stratified evaluation yields more stable and transparent assessments while supporting more trustworthy decision-making than aggregate metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：覆盖而非平均：面向可信检索评估的语义分层
检索质量是检索增强生成（RAG）准确性和鲁棒性的主要瓶颈。当前评估依赖于启发式构建的查询集，引入了隐含的固有偏差。我们将检索评估形式化为统计估计问题，表明指标可靠性从根本上受限于评估集的构建方式。我们进一步提出\emph{语义分层}，通过将文档按基于实体的聚类组织到可解释的全局空间中，并系统性生成缺失分层的查询，从而实现（1）跨不同检索场景的正式语义覆盖保证，以及（2）对检索失败模式的可解释性洞察。 在多个基准和检索方法上的实验验证了我们的框架。结果揭示了系统性的覆盖差距，识别出解释检索性能差异的结构信号，并表明分层评估比聚合指标更稳定、透明，支持更可信的决策。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unreliability of current retrieval evaluation methods due to heuristic query sets that introduce hidden bias, this work treats retrieval evaluation as a statistical estimation problem and proposes semantic stratification—a method that organizes documents into entity-based clusters and generates queries to cover missing strata, thereby ensuring semantic coverage and interpretable failure analysis. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and retrieval methods demonstrate that stratified evaluation reveals systematic coverage gaps, explains performance variance through structural signals, and produces more stable, transparent, and trustworthy assessments than aggregate metrics.
为了解决当前检索评估因启发式查询集引入隐含偏差而导致的不可靠问题，该研究将检索评估形式化为统计估计问题，并提出语义分层方法，通过基于实体的文档聚类和系统生成缺失分层的查询。该方法实现了检索场景的语义覆盖保障，并提供对检索失败模式的可解释洞察。在多个基准和检索方法上的实验表明，分层评估能揭示系统性覆盖缺口，通过结构信号解释性能差异，且相比聚合指标更稳定、透明、可信。
Rays as Pixels: Learning A Joint Distribution of Videos and Camera Trajectories
Authors: Wonbong Jang, Shikun Liu, Soubhik Sanyal, Juan Camilo Perez, Kam Woh Ng, Sanskar Agrawal, Juan-Manuel Perez-Rua, Yiannis Douratsos, Tao Xiang
First: 2026-04-10T15:47:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T16:49:08+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 6 figures, 4 tables. Project page: https://wbjang.github.io/raysaspixels/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recovering camera parameters from images and rendering scenes from novel viewpoints have been treated as separate tasks in computer vision and graphics. This separation breaks down when image coverage is sparse or poses are ambiguous, since each task depends on what the other produces. We propose Rays as Pixels, a Video Diffusion Model (VDM) that learns a joint distribution over videos and camera trajectories. To our knowledge, this is the first model to predict camera poses and do camera-controlled video generation within a single framework. We represent each camera as dense ray pixels (raxels), a pixel-aligned encoding that lives in the same latent space as video frames, and denoise the two jointly through a Decoupled Self-Cross Attention mechanism. A single trained model handles three tasks: predicting camera trajectories from video, generating video from input images along a pre-defined trajectory, and jointly synthesizing video and trajectory from input images. We evaluate on pose estimation and camera-controlled video generation, and introduce a closed-loop self-consistency test showing that the model's predicted poses and its renderings conditioned on those poses agree. Ablations against Plücker embeddings confirm that representing cameras in a shared latent space with video is subtantially more effective.
中文标题/摘要
标题：光线即像素：学习视频与相机轨迹的联合分布
在计算机视觉和图形学中，从图像恢复相机参数和从新视角渲染场景通常被视为独立任务。然而，当图像覆盖稀疏或姿态模糊时，这种分离失效，因为每个任务都依赖于另一个任务的输出。我们提出“光线即像素”，一种视频扩散模型（VDM），学习视频与相机轨迹的联合分布。据我们所知，这是首个在单一框架内同时预测相机姿态并实现相机控制视频生成的模型。我们将每个相机表示为密集的光线像素（raxels），这是一种与视频帧对齐的编码，存在于与视频帧相同的潜在空间中，并通过解耦的自交叉注意力机制联合去噪。单个训练模型可处理三项任务：从视频预测相机轨迹、沿预定义轨迹从输入图像生成视频，以及从输入图像联合合成视频和轨迹。我们在姿态估计和相机控制视频生成上进行评估，并引入闭环自一致性测试，表明模型预测的姿态与其基于该姿态条件渲染的结果一致。与Plücker嵌入的消融实验验证了在共享潜在空间中表示相机与视频相比，显著更有效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to jointly model camera motion and video content for more coherent synthesis, the authors introduce Rays as Pixels, a Video Diffusion Model that learns a shared latent distribution over videos and camera trajectories. The method encodes each camera as dense ray pixels (raxels) aligned with video frames, enabling joint denoising via a Decoupled Self-Cross Attention mechanism. Evaluated on pose estimation and camera-controlled video generation, the model achieves strong performance across three tasks—trajectory prediction from video, video generation from images along a given path, and joint synthesis of both from images—and demonstrates self-consistency in predicted poses and rendered outputs, with ablation studies confirming the superiority of shared latent representation over Plücker embeddings.
为提升相机运动与视频生成的一致性，该文提出Rays as Pixels，一种联合学习视频与相机轨迹分布的视频扩散模型。它将每个相机编码为与视频帧对齐的密集射线像素（raxels），并通过解耦自交叉注意力机制联合去噪。实验表明，该模型可完成三项任务：从视频预测相机轨迹、沿预设轨迹生成视频、以及从图像联合合成视频与轨迹，且在姿态-渲染自一致性测试中表现优异，消融实验也证实共享潜在空间表示优于Plücker嵌入。
Exploring High-Order Self-Similarity for Video Understanding
Authors: Manjin Kim, Heeseung Kwon, Karteek Alahari, Minsu Cho
First: 2026-04-22T16:48:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T16:48:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Space-time self-similarity (STSS), which captures visual correspondences across frames, provides an effective way to represent temporal dynamics for video understanding. In this work, we explore higher-order STSS and demonstrate how STSSs at different orders reveal distinct aspects of these dynamics. We then introduce the Multi-Order Self-Similarity (MOSS) module, a lightweight neural module designed to learn and integrate multi-order STSS features. It can be applied to diverse video tasks to enhance motion modeling capabilities while consuming only marginal computational cost and memory usage. Extensive experiments on video action recognition, motion-centric video VQA, and real-world robotic tasks consistently demonstrate substantial improvements, validating the broad applicability of MOSS as a general temporal modeling module. The source code and checkpoints will be publicly available.

Motivated by the need to better model complex temporal dynamics in videos, this work investigates high-order space-time self-similarity (STSS) to capture richer motion patterns across frames. It introduces the Multi-Order Self-Similarity (MOSS) module, a lightweight neural architecture that learns and fuses STSS features at multiple orders to enhance motion representation. Experiments across video action recognition, motion-centric VQA, and robotic tasks show consistent performance gains with minimal computational overhead, confirming MOSS’s effectiveness as a general-purpose temporal modeling component.

Autark: A Serverless Toolkit for Prototyping Urban Visual Analytics Systems
Authors: Lucas Alexandre, João Rulff, Talisson Souza, Gustavo Moreira, Daniel de Oliveira, Claudio Silva, Fabio Miranda, Marcos Lage
First: 2026-04-22T16:48:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T16:48:24+00:00
Comments: Autark is available at https://autarkjs.org/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The development of visual analytics (VA) systems has traditionally been a labor-intensive process, balancing design methodologies with complex software engineering practices. In domain-specific fields like urban VA, this challenge is amplified by heterogeneous data streams and a reliance on complex, multi-service architectures that hinder fast development, deployment, and reproducibility. Despite the richness of the urban VA literature, the field lacks a consolidated toolkit that encapsulates the core components of these systems, such as spatial data management, analytical processing, and visualization, into a unified, lightweight framework. In this paper, we introduce Autark, a serverless toolkit designed for the rapid prototyping of urban VA systems. Autark provides domain-aware abstractions through a self-contained architecture, enabling researchers to transition from design intention to deployed, shareable systems within hours. Furthermore, Autark's structured, tightly scoped interfaces make it well-suited for AI-assisted coding workflows, where LLMs produce more reliable code when composing from well-defined abstractions rather than generating complex solutions from scratch. Our contributions are: (1) the Autark toolkit, a serverless architecture for rapid prototyping of urban VA; (2) a comparative study of LLM coding effectiveness with and without Autark; and (3) a series of usage scenarios demonstrating its capability to streamline the creation of robust, shareable urban VA prototypes. Autark is available at https://autarkjs.org/.

视觉分析（VA）系统的开发传统上是一个劳动密集型过程，需在设计方法与复杂的软件工程实践之间取得平衡。在城市VA等特定领域，这一挑战因异构数据流和对复杂多服务架构的依赖而加剧，阻碍了快速开发、部署与可复现性。尽管城市VA文献丰富，但该领域缺乏一个整合系统核心组件（如空间数据管理、分析处理与可视化）的统一轻量级工具包。本文提出Autark，一个专为城市VA系统快速原型开发设计的无服务器工具包。Autark通过自包含架构提供领域感知抽象，使研究人员能在数小时内从设计意图过渡到可部署、可共享的系统。此外，Autark结构化且范围明确的接口使其非常适合AI辅助编码工作流，LLM在基于明确定义抽象组合代码时能生成更可靠的代码，而非从零构建复杂解决方案。我们的贡献包括：（1）Autark工具包，一个用于城市VA快速原型开发的无服务器架构；（2）对比研究LLM在使用与不使用Autark时的编码有效性；（3）一系列使用场景，展示其简化稳健、可共享城市VA原型创建的能力。Autark可在https://autarkjs.org/获取。

Experimental results show that Autark significantly improves the speed and reliability of prototype development, and LLMs generate more accurate code when using Autark’s structured interfaces compared to generating from scratch.
为解决城市视觉分析系统开发中因多服务架构复杂、缺乏统一工具导致的效率低下问题，作者提出了Autark，一个将空间数据管理、分析处理与可视化封装为轻量级、领域感知抽象的无服务器工具包。其自包含架构支持从设计到部署的快速原型开发，且结构清晰的接口更利于大语言模型辅助编程，提升代码生成的准确性。实验表明，使用Autark可显著加快原型构建速度并提高系统可靠性，大语言模型在使用其预定义组件时比从零生成解决方案更准确。
V-tableR1: Process-Supervised Multimodal Table Reasoning with Critic-Guided Policy Optimization
Authors: Yubo Jiang, Yitong An, Xin Yang, Abudukelimu Wuerkaixi, Xuxin Cheng, Fengying Xie, Zhiguo Jiang, Cao Liu, Ke Zeng, Haopeng Zhang
First: 2026-04-22T16:44:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T16:44:33+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 4 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce V-tableR1, a process-supervised reinforcement learning framework that elicits rigorous, verifiable reasoning from multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Current MLLMs trained solely on final outcomes often treat visual reasoning as a black box, relying on superficial pattern matching rather than performing rigorous multi-step inference. While Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards could enforce transparent reasoning trajectories, extending it to visual domains remains severely hindered by the ambiguity of grounding abstract logic into continuous pixel space. We solve this by leveraging the deterministic grid structure of tables as an ideal visual testbed. V-tableR1 employs a specialized critic VLM to provide dense, step-level feedback on the explicit visual chain-of-thought generated by a policy VLM. To optimize this system, we propose Process-Guided Direct Alignment Policy Optimization (PGPO), a novel RL algorithm integrating process rewards, decoupled policy constraints, and length-aware dynamic sampling. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that V-tableR1 explicitly penalizes visual hallucinations and shortcut guessing. By fundamentally shifting multimodal inference from black-box pattern matching to verifiable logical derivation, V-tableR1 4B establishes state-of-the-art accuracy among open-source models on complex tabular benchmarks, outperforming models up to 18x its size and improving over its SFT baseline
中文标题/摘要
标题：V-tableR1：基于过程监督的多模态表格推理与批评引导策略优化
我们提出了V-tableR1，一种过程监督的强化学习框架，旨在从多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中引导出严谨、可验证的推理。当前仅基于最终结果训练的MLLMs常将视觉推理视为黑箱，依赖表面模式匹配而非执行严谨的多步推理。虽然可验证奖励的强化学习可强制透明推理轨迹，但将其扩展到视觉领域仍受困于将抽象逻辑映射到连续像素空间的模糊性。我们通过利用表格的确定性网格结构作为理想视觉测试平台来解决此问题。V-tableR1采用专用批评者VLM，对策略VLM生成的显式视觉思维链提供密集的逐步反馈。为优化该系统，我们提出过程引导直接对齐策略优化（PGPO），一种新颖的强化学习算法，整合过程奖励、解耦策略约束及长度感知动态采样。大量评估表明，V-tableR1明确惩罚视觉幻觉和捷径猜测。通过从根本上将多模态推理从黑箱模式匹配转向可验证的逻辑推导，V-tableR1 4B在复杂表格基准测试中成为开源模型中的最先进准确率，超越了其大小达18倍的模型，并优于其监督微调基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of transparent, step-by-step reasoning in multimodal large language models trained only on final outcomes, V-tableR1 introduces a process-supervised reinforcement learning framework that enforces verifiable visual reasoning through critic-guided policy optimization. It leverages the structured grid of tables to ground abstract logic into visual space, using a specialized critic VLM to provide dense, step-level feedback on the policy VLM’s visual chain-of-thought. The method employs Process-Guided Direct Alignment Policy Optimization (PGPO), which integrates process rewards, decoupled policy constraints, and dynamic sampling based on reasoning length. Experimental results show V-tableR1 effectively suppresses visual hallucinations and shortcut guessing, achieving state-of-the-art performance on tabular benchmarks despite being only 4B parameters, outperforming models up to 18x larger and significantly improving over its supervised fine-tuning baseline.
为解决多模态大语言模型缺乏透明推理过程的问题，V-tableR1提出一种基于过程监督的强化学习框架，利用专门的批评者视觉语言模型对策略模型生成的视觉思维链提供细粒度反馈。其核心方法PGPO结合过程奖励、解耦策略约束和动态采样策略进行优化。实验表明，该方法能有效抑制视觉幻觉与捷径猜测，在多个表格基准测试中取得领先性能，参数规模仅为4B时仍优于参数量大18倍的模型，且超越其监督微调基线。
Colorful Talks with Graphs: Human-Interpretable Graph Encodings for Large Language Models
Authors: Angelo Zangari, Peyman Baghershahi, Sourav Medya
Venue: ACL
First: 2026-02-11T00:15:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T16:40:29+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL Findings 2026 22 pages, 18 tables, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph problems are fundamentally challenging for large language models (LLMs). While LLMs excel at processing unstructured text, graph tasks require reasoning over explicit structure, permutation invariance, and computationally complex relationships, creating a mismatch with the representations of text-based models. Our work investigates how LLMs can be effectively applied to graph problems despite these barriers. We introduce a human-interpretable structural encoding strategy for graph-to-text translation that injects graph structure directly into natural language prompts. Our method involves computing a variant of Weisfeiler-Lehman (WL) similarity classes and maps them to human-like color tokens rather than numeric labels. The key insight is that semantically meaningful and human-interpretable cues may be more effectively processed by LLMs than opaque symbolic encoding. Experimental results on multiple algorithmic and predictive graph tasks show the considerable improvements by our method on both synthetic and real-world datasets. By capturing both local and global-range dependencies, our method enhances LLM performance especially on graph tasks that require reasoning over global graph structure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多彩对话与图谱：面向大语言模型的人类可解释图编码
图问题对大语言模型（LLMs）而言本质上具有挑战性。尽管LLMs擅长处理非结构化文本，但图任务需要在显式结构上进行推理、排列不变性及计算复杂的关系，这与基于文本的模型表示存在不匹配。我们的工作探讨了如何克服这些障碍，有效将LLMs应用于图问题。我们提出了一种人类可解释的结构编码策略，用于图到文本的转换，直接将图结构注入自然语言提示中。该方法计算Weisfeiler-Lehman（WL）相似类的变体，并将其映射为类似人类的彩色标记而非数值标签。核心洞见是：语义丰富且人类可解释的线索，可能比晦涩的符号编码更易被LLMs处理。在多个算法与预测图任务上的实验结果表明，我们的方法在合成与真实数据集上均显著提升性能。通过捕捉局部与全局范围依赖关系，该方法尤其增强了LLMs在需要全局图结构推理的图任务中的表现。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of applying large language models to graph problems, which require structured reasoning beyond text processing. The authors propose a human-interpretable encoding method that translates graph structures into color-based tokens derived from Weisfeiler-Lehman similarity classes, replacing abstract numeric labels. Experiments across algorithmic and predictive graph tasks demonstrate significant performance gains over baseline methods, particularly in tasks demanding global graph structure reasoning, due to the encoding’s ability to capture both local and global dependencies in a visually intuitive format.
该论文旨在解决大语言模型在图结构任务中的应用难题，因其需进行结构化推理而非纯文本处理。作者提出一种人类可理解的编码方法，将图结构转化为基于Weisfeiler-Lehman相似类的颜色标记，替代抽象数值标签。在多个图算法与预测任务中，实验表明该方法在合成与真实数据集上均显著优于基线方法，尤其在需全局结构推理的任务中表现突出，得益于其能同时捕捉局部与全局依赖关系的直观视觉编码。
Where and What: Reasoning Dynamic and Implicit Preferences in Situated Conversational Recommendation
Authors: Dongding Lin, Jian Wang, Yongqi Li, Wenjie Li
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-22T16:39:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T16:39:52+00:00
Comments: Accpeted by ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Situated conversational recommendation (SCR), which utilizes visual scenes grounded in specific environments and natural language dialogue to deliver contextually appropriate recommendations, has emerged as a promising research direction due to its close alignment with real-world scenarios. Compared to traditional recommendations, SCR requires a deeper understanding of dynamic and implicit user preferences, as the surrounding scene often influences users' underlying interests, while both may evolve across conversations. This complexity significantly impacts the timing and relevance of recommendations. To address this, we propose situated preference reasoning (SiPeR), a novel framework that integrates two core mechanisms: (1) Scene transition estimation, which estimates whether the current scene satisfies user needs, and guides the user toward a more suitable scene when necessary; and (2) Bayesian inverse inference, which leverages the likelihood of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to predict user preferences about candidate items within the scene. Extensive experiments on two representative benchmarks demonstrate SiPeR's superiority in both recommendation accuracy and response generation quality. The code and data are available at https://github.com/DongdingLin/SiPeR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：何处何物：情境对话推荐中动态与隐式偏好的推理
情境对话推荐（SCR）利用特定环境中的视觉场景和自然语言对话，提供情境适配的推荐，因其贴近现实场景，已成为有前景的研究方向。相比传统推荐，SCR需更深入理解用户的动态与隐式偏好，因周围场景常影响用户潜在兴趣，且二者可能随对话演变。此复杂性显著影响推荐时机与相关性。为此，我们提出情境偏好推理（SiPeR）框架，整合两大机制：（1）场景转换估计，判断当前场景是否满足用户需求，必要时引导用户至更合适场景；（2）贝叶斯逆推，利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）似然度预测用户对场景内候选物品的偏好。在两个代表性基准上的大量实验表明，SiPeR在推荐准确性和响应生成质量上均优于现有方法。代码与数据见 https://github.com/DongdingLin/SiPeR。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to capture dynamic and implicit user preferences in real-world conversational settings, this paper introduces SiPeR, a framework for situated conversational recommendation that combines scene transition estimation with Bayesian inverse inference to model evolving user interests. The method first assesses whether the current visual scene aligns with user needs and suggests scene transitions if necessary, then uses multimodal large language models to infer user preferences over items within the scene. Experimental results on two benchmarks show SiPeR outperforms existing methods in both recommendation accuracy and conversational response quality, demonstrating its effectiveness in handling context-sensitive and evolving user preferences.
为应对真实场景中用户动态与隐式偏好的捕捉难题，本文提出SiPeR框架，结合场景转换估计与贝叶斯逆推机制。该方法首先判断当前视觉场景是否满足用户需求，必要时引导场景切换，再利用多模态大语言模型推断场景内候选物品的用户偏好。在两个基准数据集上的实验表明，SiPeR在推荐准确性和响应质量上均优于现有方法，有效处理了用户上下文演化与隐式偏好建模问题。
Amodal SAM: A Unified Amodal Segmentation Framework with Generalization
Authors: Bo Zhang, Zhuotao Tian, Xin Tao, Songlin Tang, Jun Yu, Wenjie Pei
First: 2026-04-22T16:39:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T16:39:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Amodal segmentation is a challenging task that aims to predict the complete geometric shape of objects, including their occluded regions. Although existing methods primarily focus on amodal segmentation within the training domain, these approaches often lack the generalization capacity to extend effectively to novel object categories and unseen contexts. This paper introduces Amodal SAM, a unified framework that leverages SAM (Segment Anything Model) for both amodal image and amodal video segmentation. Amodal SAM preserves the powerful generalization ability of SAM while extending its inherent capabilities to the amodal segmentation task. The improvements lie in three aspects: (1) a lightweight Spatial Completion Adapter that enables occluded region reconstruction, (2) a Target-Aware Occlusion Synthesis (TAOS) pipeline that addresses the scarcity of amodal annotations by generating diverse synthetic training data, and (3) novel learning objectives that enforce regional consistency and topological regularization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Amodal SAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on standard benchmarks, while simultaneously exhibiting robust generalization to novel scenarios. We anticipate that this research will advance the field toward practical amodal segmentation systems capable of operating effectively in unconstrained real-world environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无模态SAM：一种具有泛化能力的统一无模态分割框架
无模态分割旨在预测物体的完整几何形状，包括被遮挡区域，是一项具有挑战性的任务。尽管现有方法主要关注训练域内的无模态分割，但这些方法往往缺乏有效泛化到新物体类别和未见场景的能力。本文提出了无模态SAM，一种统一框架，利用SAM（Segment Anything Model）实现无模态图像和视频分割。无模态SAM保留了SAM强大的泛化能力，同时扩展其固有能力以适应无模态分割任务。改进体现在三个方面：（1）轻量级空间补全适配器，用于重建遮挡区域；（2）目标感知遮挡合成（TAOS）流程，通过生成多样化的合成训练数据缓解无模态标注稀缺问题；（3）新颖的学习目标，强制区域一致性与拓扑正则化。大量实验表明，无模态SAM在标准基准上达到最先进性能，同时展现出对新场景的鲁棒泛化能力。我们期望该研究将推动无模态分割领域向能够在无约束真实环境中有效运行的实用系统发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for amodal segmentation models that generalize beyond training domains, this paper proposes Amodal SAM, a unified framework that adapts the Segment Anything Model (SAM) for both image and video amodal segmentation. It introduces three key components: a lightweight Spatial Completion Adapter to reconstruct occluded regions, a Target-Aware Occlusion Synthesis (TAOS) pipeline to generate synthetic amodal training data, and novel learning objectives enforcing regional consistency and topological regularization. Experimental results show Amodal SAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on standard benchmarks while demonstrating strong generalization to novel object categories and unseen contexts.
