Generative Flow Networks for Model Adaptation in Digital Twins of Natural Systems
Authors: Pascal Archambault, Houari Sahraoui, Eugene Syriani
First: 2026-04-22T15:48:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:48:48+00:00
Comments: Under Review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Digital twins of natural systems must remain aligned with physical systems that evolve over time, are only partially observed, and are typically modeled by mechanistic simulators whose parameters cannot be measured directly. In such settings, model adaptation is naturally posed as a simulation-based inference problem. However, sparse and indirect observations often fail to identify a unique and optimal calibration, leaving several simulator parameterizations compatible with the available evidence. This article presents a GFlowNet-based approach to model adaptation for digital twins of natural systems. We formulate adaptation as a generative modeling problem over complete simulator configurations, so that plausible parameterizations can be sampled with probability proportional to a reward derived from agreement between simulated and observed behavior. Using a controlled environment agriculture case study based on a mechanistic tomato model, we show that the learned policy recovers dominant regions of the adaptation landscape, retrieves strong calibration hypotheses, and preserves multiple plausible configurations under uncertainty.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于自然系统数字孪生体的生成流网络模型自适应方法
自然系统的数字孪生体必须持续与随时间演化的物理系统保持一致，这些系统通常仅部分可观测，且其模型由机制模拟器构成，参数无法直接测量。在此类场景下，模型自适应自然地转化为基于仿真的推断问题。然而，稀疏且间接的观测往往无法唯一确定最优校准，导致多个模拟器参数化方案与现有证据兼容。本文提出一种基于GFlowNet的自然系统数字孪生体模型自适应方法。我们将自适应问题形式化为对完整模拟器配置的生成建模问题，从而可按模拟行为与观测行为一致性所导出的奖励值比例采样出合理的参数化方案。通过基于机制番茄模型的受控农业案例研究，我们表明所学习的策略能恢复自适应景观中的主导区域，检索出强校准假设，并在不确定性下保留多个合理配置。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of maintaining alignment between evolving natural systems and their digital twins under sparse observations and unmeasurable simulator parameters, this work proposes a Generative Flow Network (GFlowNet) approach to model adaptation. The method frames adaptation as a generative modeling task over complete simulator configurations, sampling plausible parameterizations with probability weighted by their agreement with observed data. In a controlled agricultural case study using a mechanistic tomato model, the approach successfully identifies dominant regions of the adaptation landscape, retrieves strong calibration hypotheses, and preserves multiple plausible configurations under uncertainty.
为解决自然系统数字孪生体在观测稀疏、参数不可直接测量情况下难以精准校准的问题，本文提出基于生成流网络（GFlowNet）的模型自适应方法，将自适应建模转化为对模拟器配置的生成建模任务。该方法依据模拟与观测行为的一致性赋予参数配置采样概率，从而在不确定性下保留多个合理解。在基于机械番茄模型的农业控制实验中，该方法成功识别了自适应空间的主要区域，提取出强校准假设，并在数据不足时维持多个可行参数配置。
Stability and Generalization in Looped Transformers
Authors: Asher Labovich
First: 2026-04-16T17:35:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:48:32+00:00
Comments: 11 main pages, 27 total
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Looped transformers promise test-time compute scaling by spending more iterations on harder problems, but it remains unclear which architectural choices let them extrapolate to harder problems at test time rather than memorize training-specific solutions. We introduce a fixed-point based framework for analyzing looped architectures along three axes of stability -- reachability, input-dependence, and geometry -- and use it to characterize when fixed-point iteration yields meaningful predictions. Theoretically, we prove that looped networks without recall have countable fixed points and cannot achieve strong input-dependence at any spectral regime, while recall combined with outer normalization reliably produces a regime in which fixed points are simultaneously reachable, locally smooth in the input, and supported by stable backpropagation. Empirically, we train single-layer looped transformers on chess, sudoku, and prefix-sums and find that downstream performance tracks the framework's predictions across tasks and architectural configurations. We additionally introduce internal recall, a novel recall placement variant, and show that it becomes competitive with -- and on sudoku, substantially better than -- standard recall placement once outer normalization is applied.
中文标题/摘要
标题：循环变压器中的稳定性与泛化能力
循环变压器通过在较难问题上投入更多迭代次数来实现测试时计算量的扩展，但尚不清楚哪些架构选择能使其在测试时对更难的问题进行外推而非记忆训练特定解。我们引入一个基于定点的框架，沿三个稳定性维度——可达性、输入依赖性和几何结构——分析循环架构，并用其刻画定点迭代何时能产生有意义的预测。理论上，我们证明无记忆的循环网络在任何谱域内仅有可数个定点，且无法实现强输入依赖性；而结合外层归一化的记忆机制则能可靠地产生一个定点同时可达、输入局部平滑且支持稳定反向传播的谱域。实证上，我们在国际象棋、数独和前缀和任务上训练单层循环变压器，发现下游性能在不同任务和架构配置下均与框架预测一致。我们还引入了一种新颖的记忆放置变体——内部记忆，并证明在应用外层归一化后，其性能可与标准记忆放置相媲美，而在数独任务上甚至显著优于后者。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how looped transformers generalize to harder test-time problems without memorizing training data, this work introduces a fixed-point analysis framework that evaluates stability across three axes: reachability, input-dependence, and geometry. Theoretically, it proves that looped networks without recall have countable fixed points and cannot achieve strong input-dependence, while recall combined with outer normalization enables stable, reachable, and smooth fixed points supporting effective backpropagation. Empirically, single-layer looped transformers trained on chess, sudoku, and prefix-sums show downstream performance aligned with theoretical predictions, and a novel internal recall variant, when paired with outer normalization, achieves performance competitive with or superior to standard recall, especially on sudoku.
为理解循环Transformer如何在测试时泛化到更难问题而非记忆训练数据，本文提出基于定点分析的框架，从可达性、输入依赖性和几何结构三个维度评估稳定性。理论上证明，无回溯的循环网络仅有可数定点且无法实现强输入依赖，而结合外层归一化的回溯机制可稳定生成可达、平滑且支持反向传播的定点。实验表明，训练于国际象棋、数独和前缀和任务的单层循环Transformer性能与理论预测一致，且新提出的内部回溯机制在配合外层归一化后，在数独任务上表现优于标准回溯方案。
SAMix: Calibrated and Accurate Continual Learning via Sphere-Adaptive Mixup and Neural Collapse
Authors: Trung-Anh Dang, Vincent Nguyen, Ngoc-Son Vu, Christel Vrain
First: 2025-10-17T15:36:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:48:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While most continual learning methods focus on mitigating forgetting and improving accuracy, they often overlook the critical aspect of network calibration, despite its importance. Neural collapse, a phenomenon where last-layer features collapse to their class means, has demonstrated advantages in continual learning by reducing feature-classifier misalignment. Few works aim to improve the calibration of continual models for more reliable predictions. Our work goes a step further by proposing a novel method that not only enhances calibration but also improves performance by reducing overconfidence, mitigating forgetting, and increasing accuracy. We introduce Sphere-Adaptive Mixup (SAMix), an adaptive mixup strategy tailored for neural collapse-based methods. SAMix adapts the mixing process to the geometric properties of feature spaces under neural collapse, ensuring more robust regularization and alignment. Experiments show that SAMix significantly boosts performance, surpassing SOTA methods in continual learning while also improving model calibration. SAMix enhances both across-task accuracy and the broader reliability of predictions, making it a promising advancement for robust continual learning systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAMix：通过球面自适应混合与神经坍缩实现校准准确的持续学习
尽管大多数持续学习方法侧重于减轻遗忘并提升准确率，但往往忽视了网络校准这一关键方面，尽管其重要性不容忽视。神经坍缩现象——即最后一层特征坍缩至各类别均值——在持续学习中通过减少特征与分类器的错位，已展现出优势。目前鲜有研究致力于提升持续学习模型的校准以获得更可靠的预测。本文进一步提出一种新方法，不仅增强校准，还通过降低过自信、减轻遗忘并提升准确率来提高性能。我们引入球面自适应混合（SAMix），这是一种专为基于神经坍缩方法设计的自适应混合策略。SAMix根据神经坍缩下特征空间的几何特性调整混合过程，确保更稳健的正则化与对齐。实验表明，SAMix显著提升性能，在持续学习中超越当前最先进方法，同时改善模型校准。SAMix不仅提升跨任务准确率，也增强预测的广泛可靠性，是构建稳健持续学习系统的重要进展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underappreciated role of model calibration in continual learning, this work proposes Sphere-Adaptive Mixup (SAMix), an adaptive mixup strategy designed to enhance calibration while improving accuracy and reducing forgetting. SAMix leverages the geometric structure of feature spaces under neural collapse to guide mixup operations, promoting better feature-classifier alignment and reducing overconfidence. Experimental results demonstrate that SAMix outperforms state-of-the-art methods in continual learning, achieving higher across-task accuracy and improved calibration metrics, thereby enhancing prediction reliability.
鉴于现有持续学习方法常忽视模型校准的重要性，本文提出Sphere-Adaptive Mixup（SAMix），一种基于神经坍缩几何特性的自适应混合策略，旨在同时提升校准精度与性能。SAMix通过调整混合过程以适应特征空间的球形结构，增强正则化并减少过自信，实验表明其在持续学习任务中显著提升跨任务准确率与预测可靠性，超越当前最优方法。
The Role and Relationship of Initialization and Densification in 3D Gaussian Splatting
Authors: Ivan Desiatov, Torsten Sattler
First: 2026-03-21T08:37:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:48:16+00:00
Comments: Sources are available at https://github.com/deivse/ivd_splat . Changes in this version: fixed wrong graphs being used in Fig. 6 (b), Fig. 10 (a,c,d) due to compilation issue; results with EDGS* are now using splat scale increase when reducing init. size (previously reported results without scale increase, but conclusions remain unchanged)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become the method of choice for photo-realistic 3D reconstruction of scenes, due to being able to efficiently and accurately recover the scene appearance and geometry from images. 3DGS represents the scene through a set of 3D Gaussians, parameterized by their position, spatial extent, and view-dependent color. Starting from an initial point cloud, 3DGS refines the Gaussians' parameters as to reconstruct a set of training images as accurately as possible. Typically, a sparse Structure-from-Motion point cloud is used as initialization. In order to obtain dense Gaussian clouds, 3DGS methods thus rely on a densification stage. In this paper, we systematically study the relation between densification and initialization. Proposing a new benchmark, we study combinations of different types of initializations (dense laser scans, dense (multi-view) stereo point clouds, dense monocular depth estimates, sparse SfM point clouds) and different densification schemes. We show that current densification approaches are not able to take full advantage of dense initialization as they are often unable to (significantly) improve over sparse SfM-based initialization. We will make our benchmark publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：初始化与稠密化在3D高斯点绘中的作用与关系
3D高斯点绘（3DGS）已成为从图像高效准确恢复场景外观与几何结构的首选方法，其通过一组3D高斯分布表示场景，参数包括位置、空间范围及视角相关颜色。从初始点云出发，3DGS优化高斯参数以尽可能精确重建训练图像。通常使用稀疏SfM点云作为初始化。为获得稠密高斯云，3DGS方法依赖于稠密化阶段。本文系统研究稠密化与初始化的关系，提出新基准，研究不同初始化类型（密集激光扫描、密集（多视角）立体点云、密集单目深度估计、稀疏SfM点云）与不同稠密化方案的组合。我们表明当前稠密化方法无法充分利用密集初始化，因其常无法（显著）超越稀疏SfM初始化。我们将公开该基准。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how initialization quality and densification strategies interact in 3D Gaussian Splatting, a method for photo-realistic 3D scene reconstruction. Motivated by the observation that current densification techniques fail to leverage dense initializations effectively, the authors introduce a new benchmark comparing various initialization types—including dense laser scans, stereo point clouds, monocular depth estimates, and sparse SfM point clouds—with multiple densification approaches. Their experiments reveal that densification methods typically do not significantly outperform sparse SfM initialization, even when given dense input, suggesting a fundamental mismatch between current densification algorithms and high-quality initial data.
本文研究了3D高斯点绘中初始化质量与稠密化策略之间的关系，旨在解决当前稠密化方法难以有效利用密集初始化数据的问题。作者构建了一个新基准，对比了多种初始化方式（如密集激光扫描、立体匹配点云、单目深度估计和稀疏SfM点云）与不同稠密化方法的效果。实验表明，现有稠密化方法通常无法显著超越稀疏SfM初始化的效果，即使输入数据本身非常密集，说明当前稠密化方法与密集初始化数据之间存在根本性不匹配。
QuanForge: A Mutation Testing Framework for Quantum Neural Networks
Authors: Minqi Shao, Shangzhou Xia, Jianjun Zhao
First: 2026-04-22T15:47:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:47:44+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 4 figures, accepted at FSE 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the growing synergy between deep learning and quantum computing, Quantum Neural Networks (QNNs) have emerged as a promising paradigm by leveraging quantum parallelism and entanglement. However, testing QNNs remains underexplored due to their complex quantum dynamics and limited interpretability. Developing a mutation testing technique for QNNs is promising while requires addressing stochastic factors, including the inherent randomness of mutation operators and quantum measurements. To tackle these challenges, we propose QuanForge, a mutation testing framework specifically designed for QNNs. We first introduce statistical mutation killing to provide a more reliable criterion. QuanForge incorporates nine post-training mutation operators at both gate and parameter levels, capable of simulating various potential errors in quantum circuits. Finally, a mutant generation algorithm is formalized that systematically produces effective mutants, thereby enabling a robust and reliable mutation analysis. Through extensive experiments on benchmark datasets and QNN architectures, we show that QuanForge can effectively distinguish different test suites and localize vulnerable circuit regions, providing insights for data enhancement and structural assessment of QNNs. We also analyze the generation capabilities of different operators and evaluate performance under simulated noisy conditions to assess the practical feasibility of QuanForge for future quantum devices.
中文标题/摘要
标题：QuanForge：面向量子神经网络的突变测试框架
随着深度学习与量子计算的协同发展，量子神经网络（QNNs）作为一种利用量子并行性和纠缠效应的新兴范式，展现出巨大潜力。然而，由于其复杂的量子动力学和有限的可解释性，QNNs的测试仍处于探索阶段。开发针对QNNs的突变测试技术具有前景，但需应对随机因素，包括突变算子的固有随机性及量子测量的不确定性。为此，我们提出QuanForge，专为QNNs设计的突变测试框架。首先引入统计突变杀灭准则以提升可靠性；QuanForge在门级与参数级集成九种后训练突变算子，可模拟量子电路中的多种潜在错误；最后，我们形式化了一种突变体生成算法，系统性地生成有效突变体，从而实现稳健可靠的突变分析。通过在基准数据集与QNN架构上的大量实验，我们证明QuanForge能有效区分不同测试套件并定位脆弱电路区域，为QNNs的数据增强与结构评估提供洞见。同时，我们分析不同算子的生成能力，并在模拟噪声环境下评估其性能，以评估QuanForge在未来的量子设备中的实际可行性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve the reliability of Quantum Neural Networks (QNNs) amid their complex quantum dynamics and limited interpretability, the authors propose QuanForge, a mutation testing framework tailored for QNNs. The method introduces statistical mutation killing to handle stochasticity from quantum measurements and mutation operators, and incorporates nine mutation operators at gate and parameter levels to simulate realistic circuit errors. A formal mutant generation algorithm ensures systematic and effective mutant creation. Experimental results across benchmark datasets and architectures demonstrate QuanForge’s ability to differentiate test suite effectiveness and pinpoint vulnerable circuit regions, while also validating operator performance under simulated noise, confirming its practical relevance for future quantum hardware.
为提升量子神经网络（QNN）的可靠性和可解释性，本文提出QuanForge框架，专用于QNN的突变测试。该框架通过引入统计突变杀灭准则应对随机性挑战，并在门和参数层面集成九种突变操作符以模拟量子电路中的实际错误。框架还包含一个系统化生成有效突变体的算法。在多个基准数据集和QNN架构上的实验表明，QuanForge能有效区分测试套件质量、定位脆弱电路区域，并在模拟噪声环境下验证其鲁棒性，支持其在未来的量子硬件中的实际应用。
SSL-R1: Self-Supervised Visual Reinforcement Post-Training for Multimodal Large Language Models
Authors: Jiahao Xie, Alessio Tonioni, Nathalie Rauschmayr, Federico Tombari, Bernt Schiele
First: 2026-04-22T15:46:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:46:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has demonstrated the great potential of enhancing the reasoning abilities in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, the reliance on language-centric priors and expensive manual annotations prevents MLLMs' intrinsic visual understanding and scalable reward designs. In this work, we introduce SSL-R1, a generic self-supervised RL framework that derives verifiable rewards directly from images. To this end, we revisit self-supervised learning (SSL) in visual domains and reformulate widely-used SSL tasks into a set of verifiable visual puzzles for RL post-training, requiring neither human nor external model supervision. Training MLLMs on these tasks substantially improves their performance on multimodal understanding and reasoning benchmarks, highlighting the potential of leveraging vision-centric self-supervised tasks for MLLM post-training. We think this work will provide useful experience in devising effective self-supervised verifiable rewards to enable RL at scale. Project page: https://github.com/Jiahao000/SSL-R1.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SSL-R1：面向多模态大语言模型的自监督视觉强化学习后训练
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已展现出增强多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）推理能力的巨大潜力。然而，其对语言中心先验的依赖及昂贵的人工标注，阻碍了MLLMs内在视觉理解与可扩展奖励设计。本文提出SSL-R1，一种通用的自监督强化学习框架，直接从图像中推导可验证奖励。为此，我们重新审视视觉领域的自监督学习（SSL），将常用SSL任务重构为一组可验证的视觉谜题，用于RL后训练，无需人工或外部模型监督。在这些任务上训练MLLMs显著提升了其在多模态理解和推理基准上的表现，凸显了利用视觉中心自监督任务进行MLLM后训练的潜力。我们认为，本工作将为设计有效的自监督可验证奖励以实现大规模RL提供有益经验。项目页面：https://github.com/Jiahao000/SSL-R1。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of language-centric rewards and costly annotations in enhancing multimodal large language models' visual reasoning, this work proposes SSL-R1, a self-supervised reinforcement learning framework that generates verifiable rewards directly from images. It reformulates common visual self-supervised learning tasks into RL-compatible puzzles, eliminating the need for human or external model supervision. Experimental results show that training MLLMs on these visual puzzles significantly boosts their performance on multimodal understanding and reasoning benchmarks, demonstrating the effectiveness of vision-centric self-supervised tasks for scalable post-training reinforcement learning.
为克服语言中心奖励依赖及人工标注成本高对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）能力提升的限制，本文提出SSL-R1框架，通过从视觉数据直接生成可验证奖励，实现自监督强化学习。该方法将常用视觉自监督任务重构为强化学习兼容的视觉谜题，无需人工或外部模型监督。实验表明，基于此类视觉中心任务训练的MLLMs在多模态理解与推理基准上表现显著提升，验证了视觉自监督任务在大规模强化学习后训练中的有效性。
Auto-ART: Structured Literature Synthesis and Automated Adversarial Robustness Testing
Authors: Abhijit Talluri
Venue: NeurIPS 2026
First: 2026-04-22T15:46:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:46:11+00:00
Comments: NeurIPS 2026 Evaluations and Datasets Track Submission
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Adversarial robustness evaluation underpins every claim of trustworthy ML deployment, yet the field suffers from fragmented protocols and undetected gradient masking. We make two contributions. (1) Structured synthesis. We analyze nine peer-reviewed corpus sources (2020--2026) through seven complementary protocols, producing the first end-to-end structured analysis of the field's consensus and unresolved challenges. (2) Auto-ART framework. We introduce Auto-ART, an open-source framework that operationalizes identified gaps: 50+ attacks, 28 defense modules, the Robustness Diagnostic Index (RDI), and gradient-masking detection. It supports multi-norm evaluation (l1/l2/linf/semantic/spatial) and compliance mapping to NIST AI RMF, OWASP LLM Top 10, and the EU AI Act. Empirical validation on RobustBench demonstrates that Auto-ART's pre-screening identifies gradient masking in 92% of flagged cases, and RDI rankings correlate highly with full AutoAttack. Multi-norm evaluation exposes a 23.5 pp gap between average and worst-case robustness on state-of-the-art models. No prior work combines such structured meta-scientific analysis with an executable evaluation framework bridging literature gaps into engineering.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Auto-ART：结构化文献综述与自动化对抗鲁棒性测试
对抗鲁棒性评估是可信机器学习部署的基石，但该领域存在协议碎片化和未被发现的梯度掩码问题。我们做出两项贡献：(1) 结构化综述。我们通过七种互补协议分析2020至2026年间九个同行评审文献库，首次实现该领域共识与未解挑战的端到端结构化分析。(2) Auto-ART框架。我们提出Auto-ART开源框架，将识别出的差距转化为实际操作：包含50多种攻击、28种防御模块、鲁棒性诊断指数（RDI）及梯度掩码检测功能。支持多范式评估（l1/l2/linf/语义/空间）并兼容NIST AI RMF、OWASP LLM Top 10及欧盟AI法案合规映射。在RobustBench上的实证验证表明，Auto-ART预筛选可识别92%的梯度掩码案例，RDI排名与完整AutoAttack高度相关。多范式评估揭示当前最先进模型平均与最坏情况鲁棒性之间存在23.5个百分点差距。此前尚无工作将此类结构化元科学分析与可执行评估框架结合，以弥合文献差距并实现工程落地。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of standardized adversarial robustness evaluation and frequent gradient masking in ML systems, this work introduces Auto-ART: a framework that synthesizes structured literature analysis with automated testing. It systematically reviews nine peer-reviewed sources (2020–2026) using seven protocols to map consensus and open challenges, then implements 50+ attacks, 28 defenses, and a Robustness Diagnostic Index (RDI) for multi-norm evaluation. Experiments on RobustBench show Auto-ART detects gradient masking in 92% of flagged cases and reveals a 23.5 point performance gap between average and worst-case robustness across state-of-the-art models, demonstrating its effectiveness in bridging literature gaps with executable engineering tools.
为解决机器学习系统对抗鲁棒性评估缺乏标准及梯度掩码未被发现的问题，该研究提出Auto-ART框架，结合结构化文献综述与自动化测试。框架整合50多种攻击方法和28种防御模块，引入鲁棒性诊断指数（RDI）进行评估，并支持l1/l2/linf/语义/空间等多种范式评估，同时符合NIST、OWASP和欧盟AI法案标准。在RobustBench上的实验证明，Auto-ART预筛查能识别92%的梯度掩码案例，且揭示当前最先进模型在平均与最坏情况鲁棒性间存在23.5点差距，有效弥合了文献分析与工程实践之间的鸿沟。
Overcoming the Modality Gap in Context-Aided Forecasting
Authors: Vincent Zhihao Zheng, Étienne Marcotte, Arjun Ashok, Andrew Robert Williams, Lijun Sun, Alexandre Drouin, Valentina Zantedeschi
First: 2026-03-12T21:05:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:43:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Context-aided forecasting (CAF) holds promise for integrating domain knowledge and forward-looking information, enabling AI systems to surpass traditional statistical methods. However, recent empirical studies reveal a puzzling gap: multimodal models often fail to outperform their unimodal counterparts. We hypothesize that this underperformance stems from poor context quality in existing datasets, as verification is challenging. To address these limitations, we introduce a semi-synthetic data augmentation method that generates contexts both descriptive of temporal dynamics and verifiably complementary to numerical histories. This approach enables massive-scale dataset creation, resulting in CAF-7M, a corpus of 7 million context-augmented time series windows, including a rigorously verified test set. We demonstrate that semi-synthetic pre-training transfers effectively to real-world evaluation, and show clear evidence of context utilization. Our results suggest that dataset quality, rather than architectural limitations, has been the primary bottleneck in context-aided forecasting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：克服上下文辅助预测中的模态差距
上下文辅助预测（CAF）有望整合领域知识和前瞻性信息，使AI系统超越传统统计方法。然而，近期实证研究表明，多模态模型往往无法胜过单模态模型。我们推测，这一表现不佳源于现有数据集中上下文质量较差，且验证困难。为解决此问题，我们提出一种半合成数据增强方法，生成既描述时间动态又可验证地补充数值历史的上下文。该方法可大规模创建数据集，形成CAF-7M语料库，包含700万个上下文增强的时间序列窗口及严格验证的测试集。我们证明半合成预训练可有效迁移到真实评估，并提供上下文利用的明确证据。结果表明，数据集质量而非架构限制，一直是上下文辅助预测的主要瓶颈。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underperformance of multimodal models in context-aided forecasting despite their theoretical advantages, the authors hypothesize that poor context quality in existing datasets is the root cause. They propose a semi-synthetic data augmentation method to generate high-quality, verifiably complementary contexts paired with numerical time series, enabling the creation of CAF-7M—a 7 million window corpus with a rigorously verified test set. Experiments show that semi-synthetic pre-training effectively transfers to real-world tasks and demonstrates clear context utilization, indicating that dataset quality—not model architecture—is the primary bottleneck in this domain.
鉴于多模态模型在上下文辅助预测中表现不佳的现象，作者推测现有数据集中上下文质量低下是根本原因。他们提出一种半合成数据增强方法，生成兼具时序动态描述性和可验证互补性的上下文，从而构建了包含700万窗口的CAF-7M数据集，并配备严格验证的测试集。实验表明，半合成预训练能有效迁移到真实场景，显著优于单模态基线，证明上下文利用有效，数据集质量而非模型架构才是当前上下文辅助预测的主要瓶颈。
MixLLM: LLM Quantization with Global Mixed-precision between Output-features and Highly-efficient System Design
Authors: Zhen Zheng, Xiaonan Song, Chuanjie Liu
First: 2024-12-19T07:15:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:43:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted at MLSys 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Quantization has become one of the most effective methodologies to compress LLMs into smaller size. However, the existing quantization solutions still show limitations of either non-negligible accuracy drop or low system efficiency. In this paper, we propose MixLLM that explores the optimization space of mixed-precision quantization between output features, based on the insight that different features matter differently in the model. MixLLM identifies the important output features in the global view rather than within each single layer, effectively assigning larger bit-width to output features that need it the most to achieve high accuracy and low memory usage. We present the sweet spot of quantization configuration of algorithm-system co-design with high accuracy and system efficiency. To address the system challenge, we design the two-step dequantization to make use of the Tensor Core easily and fast data type conversion to reduce dequantization overhead, and present the software pipeline to overlap the memory access, dequantization and the MatMul to the best. Extensive experiments show that with only 10\% more bits, the perplexity increase can be reduced from about 0.5 in SOTA to within 0.2 for Llama 3.1 70B, while MMLU-Pro loss can be reduced from 1.92 to 0.99 over the SOTA of three popular models. Besides its superior accuracy, MixLLM also achieves state-of-the-art system efficiency. Code is released at https://github.com/microsoft/MixLLM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MixLLM：基于输出特征全局混合精度的LLM量化与高效系统设计
量化已成为压缩大语言模型（LLM）尺寸最有效的手段之一。然而，现有量化方案仍存在精度损失不可忽略或系统效率低下的问题。本文提出MixLLM，基于“模型中不同特征重要性不同”的洞察，探索输出特征间的混合精度优化空间。MixLLM从全局视角识别重要输出特征，而非逐层分析，为最需要的特征分配更大位宽，以实现高精度与低内存占用。我们提出算法与系统协同设计的量化配置最优解，兼顾高精度与高效率。为应对系统挑战，设计两阶段反量化机制，充分利用Tensor Core并加速数据类型转换以降低反量化开销，同时构建软件流水线，最大程度重叠内存访问、反量化与矩阵乘法操作。大量实验表明，仅增加10%位宽，Llama 3.1 70B模型的困惑度增幅可从当前最优方案约0.5降至0.2以内，MMLU-Pro损失可从三款流行模型的最优值1.92降至0.99。除精度优势外，MixLLM亦实现系统效率的最先进水平。代码已开源：https://github.com/microsoft/MixLLM。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between accuracy and efficiency in existing LLM quantization methods, MixLLM introduces a global mixed-precision strategy that assigns varying bit-widths to output features based on their importance across the entire model, rather than per-layer. It employs a two-step dequantization scheme and an optimized software pipeline to minimize overhead and maximize Tensor Core utilization, enabling efficient computation. Experiments demonstrate that with only 10% more bits, MixLLM reduces perplexity increase from ~0.5 to ~0.2 on Llama 3.1 70B and cuts MMLU-Pro loss from 1.92 to 0.99 compared to state-of-the-art methods, while also achieving top system efficiency.
为解决现有LLM量化方法在精度与效率间难以平衡的问题，MixLLM提出一种全局混合精度策略，根据输出特征在整个模型中的重要性动态分配比特宽度，而非按层分配。该方法识别关键特征并为其分配更高精度以保障精度，同时降低内存占用。为提升系统效率，MixLLM设计了两阶段去量化方案以适配Tensor Core，并通过软件流水线重叠内存访问、去量化与矩阵乘法操作以减少开销。实验表明，在仅增加10%比特数的情况下，MixLLM将Llama 3.1 70B的困惑度增长从约0.5降至0.2，MMLU-Pro损失从1.92降至0.99，优于当前最优方法，同时实现系统效率领先。
Quantum Adaptive Self-Attention for Quantum Transformer Models
Authors: Chi-Sheng Chen, En-Jui Kuo
First: 2025-04-05T02:52:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:43:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Integrating quantum computing into deep learning architectures is a promising but poorly understood endeavor: when does a quantum layer actually help, and how much quantum is enough? We address both questions through Quantum Adaptive Self-Attention (QASA), a hybrid Transformer that replaces the value projection in a \emph{single} encoder layer with a parameterized quantum circuit (PQC), while keeping all other layers classical. This \emph{minimal quantum integration} strategy uses only 36 trainable quantum parameters -- fewer than any competing quantum model -- yet achieves the best MSE on 4 of 9 synthetic benchmarks and a 6.0\% MAE reduction on the real-world ETTh1 dataset. An ablation study reveals that quantum layer \emph{position} matters more than \emph{count}: adding more quantum layers degrades performance, while a single layer at the optimal position consistently outperforms multi-layer quantum configurations. Comparison with two recent quantum time-series baselines -- QLSTM and QnnFormer -- confirms that QASA matches or exceeds models with $2$--$4\times$ more quantum parameters, significantly outperforming QLSTM on the seasonal trend task ($p{=}0.009$, Cohen's $d{>}6$). Crucially, the benefit is \emph{task-conditional}: QASA excels on chaotic, noisy, and trend-dominated signals, while classical Transformers remain superior for clean periodic waveforms -- providing a practical taxonomy for when quantum enhancement is warranted. These findings establish an \emph{architectural parsimony} principle for hybrid quantum-classical design: maximal quantum benefit is achieved not by maximizing quantum resources, but by strategically placing minimal quantum computation where it matters most.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于量子Transformer模型的量子自适应注意力
将量子计算融入深度学习架构是一个有前景但尚未充分理解的领域：何时量子层真正有益？需要多少量子资源？我们通过量子自适应注意力（QASA）解决这两个问题：QASA是一种混合Transformer，仅在单个编码器层中用参数化量子电路（PQC）替代值投影，其余层保持经典。这种“最小量子集成”策略仅使用36个可训练量子参数——少于任何竞争性量子模型——却在9个合成基准中的4个上取得最佳均方误差，并在真实ETTh1数据集上实现6.0%的MAE降低。消融研究表明，量子层的“位置”比“数量”更重要：增加量子层会降低性能，而单层置于最优位置始终优于多层量子配置。与两个近期量子时间序列基线模型QLSTM和QnnFormer对比确认，QASA在量子参数量为2–4倍的情况下表现匹配或超越，且在季节性趋势任务上显著优于QLSTM（p=0.009，Cohen's d>6）。关键的是，其优势是“任务依赖”的：QASA在混沌、噪声和趋势主导信号上表现卓越，而经典Transformer在干净周期波形上仍更优——这为何时需要量子增强提供了实用分类法。这些发现确立了混合量子-经典设计的“架构简约”原则：最大量子效益并非来自最大化量子资源，而是通过将最小量子计算战略性地部署在最关键位置实现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand when and how quantum layers enhance deep learning, this work introduces Quantum Adaptive Self-Attention (QASA), a hybrid Transformer that replaces only the value projection in one encoder layer with a parameterized quantum circuit, using just 36 trainable parameters. Experimental results show QASA achieves the best mean squared error on four of nine synthetic benchmarks and reduces MAE by 6.0% on the real-world ETTh1 dataset. Ablation studies reveal that quantum layer position is more critical than quantity, with a single optimally placed layer outperforming multi-layer quantum variants. QASA matches or surpasses two recent quantum baselines with 2–4× more parameters, significantly outperforming QLSTM on seasonal trends (p=0.009, Cohen’s d>6), and demonstrates task-specific benefits: excelling on chaotic, noisy, or trend-rich signals while classical Transformers remain superior for clean periodic data, establishing that minimal, strategically placed quantum computation yields maximal benefit.
为明确量子层在深度学习中的适用场景与效果边界，该研究提出量子自适应注意力机制（QASA），仅将单个编码器层的价值投影替换为参数化量子电路，仅需36个可训练量子参数。实验表明，QASA在九个合成基准中的四个上取得最优均方误差，并在真实ETTh1数据集上降低MAE 6.0%。消融实验显示，量子层位置比层数更重要，单层最优位置配置优于多层量子结构。QASA在参数量仅为竞品2-4倍的情况下，性能持平或超越QLSTM和QnnFormer，尤其在季节性趋势任务上显著优于QLSTM（p=0.009，Cohen’s d>6）。关键发现是任务依赖性：QASA在混沌、噪声或趋势主导信号上表现优异，而经典Transformer在纯净周期信号上更优，为量子增强提供了实用判断依据。
R-CoV: Region-Aware Chain-of-Verification for Alleviating Object Hallucinations in LVLMs
Authors: Jiahao Xie, Alessio Tonioni, Nathalie Rauschmayr, Federico Tombari, Bernt Schiele
First: 2026-04-22T15:41:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:41:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in various multimodal understanding and reasoning tasks. However, they still struggle with object hallucinations, i.e., the claim of nonexistent objects in the visual input. To address this challenge, we propose Region-aware Chain-of-Verification (R-CoV), a visual chain-of-verification method to alleviate object hallucinations in LVLMs in a post-hoc manner. Motivated by how humans comprehend intricate visual information -- often focusing on specific image regions or details within a given sample -- we elicit such region-level processing from LVLMs themselves and use it as a chaining cue to detect and alleviate their own object hallucinations. Specifically, our R-CoV consists of six steps: initial response generation, entity extraction, coordinate generation, region description, verification execution, and final response generation. As a simple yet effective method, R-CoV can be seamlessly integrated into various LVLMs in a training-free manner and without relying on external detection models. Extensive experiments on several widely used hallucination benchmarks across multiple LVLMs demonstrate that R-CoV can significantly alleviate object hallucinations in LVLMs. Project page: https://github.com/Jiahao000/R-CoV.
中文标题/摘要
标题：R-CoV：面向区域感知的验证链，用于缓解LVLM中的物体幻觉
大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）在多种多模态理解和推理任务中表现出色，但仍存在物体幻觉问题，即声称视觉输入中不存在的物体。为解决此问题，我们提出区域感知验证链（R-CoV），一种事后验证方法，用于缓解LVLM中的物体幻觉。受人类理解复杂视觉信息时常聚焦于特定图像区域或细节的启发，我们从LVLM自身中激发区域级处理，并将其作为链式提示，以检测和缓解其自身的物体幻觉。具体而言，R-CoV包含六个步骤：初始响应生成、实体提取、坐标生成、区域描述、验证执行和最终响应生成。作为一种简单而有效的方法，R-CoV可无缝集成至各类LVLM中，无需训练且不依赖外部检测模型。在多个LVLM上广泛使用的幻觉基准测试中，大量实验证明R-CoV能显著缓解物体幻觉。项目页面：https://github.com/Jiahao000/R-CoV。
Summary / 总结
To combat object hallucinations in large vision-language models (LVLMs), the authors propose R-CoV, a region-aware chain-of-verification method inspired by human visual processing. R-CoV operates post-hoc and requires no training or external detectors, using six sequential steps: generating an initial response, extracting entities, generating coordinates, describing regions, verifying claims, and producing a final response. Experiments across multiple LVLMs and hallucination benchmarks show R-CoV effectively reduces hallucinations without compromising performance, demonstrating its simplicity and robustness.
受人类通过聚焦图像局部区域验证物体存在的启发，作者提出R-CoV方法，通过区域感知的链式验证机制缓解LVLM中的物体幻觉问题，且无需重新训练。该方法包含六个步骤：生成初始响应、实体提取、坐标生成、区域描述、验证执行和最终输出修正。在多个LVLM和幻觉基准测试中，R-CoV显著降低幻觉率，同时结构轻量、无需外部检测器或训练即可部署。
LEAD: Breaking the No-Recovery Bottleneck in Long-Horizon Reasoning
Authors: Denys Pushkin, Emmanuel Abbe
First: 2026-03-06T20:42:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:40:03+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 5 figures, 2 tables. Updated version to reflect the manuscript under review at COLM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-horizon execution in Large Language Models (LLMs) remains unstable even when high-level strategies are provided. Evaluating on controlled algorithmic puzzles, we demonstrate that while decomposition is essential for stability, extreme decomposition creates a "no-recovery bottleneck". We show that this bottleneck becomes critical due to highly non-uniform error distribution, where consistent errors on a few "hard" steps become irreversible. To address this, we propose Lookahead-Enhanced Atomic Decomposition (LEAD). By incorporating short-horizon future validation and aggregating overlapping rollouts, LEAD provides enough isolation to maintain stability while retaining enough local context to correct errors. This enables the o4-mini model to solve Checkers Jumping up to complexity $n=13$, whereas extreme decomposition fails beyond $n=11$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LEAD：突破长时程推理中的无恢复瓶颈
即使提供高层策略，大型语言模型（LLMs）的长时程执行仍不稳定。在受控的算法谜题上，我们证明分解对稳定性至关重要，但过度分解会产生“无恢复瓶颈”。该瓶颈因错误分布高度非均匀而变得关键，少数“困难”步骤上的持续错误会变得不可逆。为此，我们提出前瞻增强原子分解（LEAD），通过结合短时程未来验证和聚合重叠轨迹，LEAD在保持稳定性的同时保留足够的局部上下文以纠正错误，使o4-mini模型可解决跳棋问题至复杂度n=13，而过度分解在n=11后即失效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of long-horizon reasoning in LLMs despite high-level strategies, the paper identifies a "no-recovery bottleneck" caused by extreme decomposition, where errors on a few hard steps become irreversible due to non-uniform error distribution. To resolve this, LEAD (Lookahead-Enhanced Atomic Decomposition) is proposed, combining short-horizon future validation with overlapping rollout aggregation to isolate errors while preserving local context for correction. Experiments show that LEAD enables the o4-mini model to solve Checkers Jumping up to complexity n=13, whereas extreme decomposition fails beyond n=11.
该论文指出，尽管提供高层策略，LLM在长时程执行中仍不稳定，极端分解会导致因关键步骤错误累积而无法恢复的瓶颈。LEAD方法通过结合短时程前瞻验证与重叠 rollout 聚合，在隔离错误的同时保留局部上下文以修正错误。实验表明，LEAD 使 o4-mini 能解决至复杂度 n=13 的跳棋问题，而极端分解在 n=11 后即失效。
A Kinematic Framework for Evaluating Pinch Configurations in Robotic Hand Design without Object or Contact Models
Authors: HyoJae Kang, Joonho Lee, Hyunmok Jung, Dong Il Park
First: 2026-04-22T15:38:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:38:40+00:00
Comments: This manuscript has been submitted for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating the pinch capability of a robotic hand is important for understanding its functional dexterity. However, many existing grasp evaluation methods rely on object geometry or contact force models, which limits their applicability during the early stages of robotic hand design. This study proposes a kinematic evaluation method for analyzing pinch configurations of robotic hands based on interactions between fingertip workspaces. First, the reachable workspace of each fingertip is computed from the joint configurations of the fingers. Then, feasible pinch configurations are detected by evaluating the relationships between fingertip pairs. Since the proposed method does not require information about object geometry or contact force models, the pinch capability of a robotic hand can be evaluated solely based on its kinematic structure. In addition, analyses are performed on four different kinematic structures of the hand to investigate their impact on the pinch configurations. The proposed evaluation framework can serve as a useful tool for comparing different robotic hand designs and analyzing pinch capability during the design stage.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需物体或接触模型的机器人手抓握构型评估的运动学框架
评估机器人手的抓握能力对于理解其功能灵巧性至关重要。然而，许多现有抓握评估方法依赖于物体几何或接触力模型，这限制了其在机器人手设计早期阶段的应用。本文提出了一种基于指尖工作空间相互作用的运动学评估方法，用于分析机器人手的抓握构型。首先，根据手指关节构型计算每个指尖的可达工作空间；然后，通过评估指尖对之间的关系检测可行抓握构型。由于该方法无需物体几何或接触力模型信息，机器人手的抓握能力可仅基于其运动学结构进行评估。此外，本文对四种不同运动学结构的手进行了分析，以研究其对抓握构型的影响。该评估框架可作为比较不同机器人手设计并分析抓握能力的有用工具。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge of evaluating robotic hand pinch capability early in design, without requiring object geometry or contact force models, which are often unavailable at that stage. The proposed method computes fingertip reachable workspaces from joint configurations and identifies feasible pinch configurations by analyzing spatial relationships between fingertip pairs. Experimental analysis across four distinct kinematic hand structures demonstrated that the framework effectively quantifies pinch capability based solely on kinematic structure, enabling direct comparison of designs without physical objects or force models.
为在机器人手设计早期阶段无需依赖物体几何或接触力模型即可评估其夹持能力，本研究提出一种基于指尖工作空间交互关系的运动学评估框架。该方法通过计算各指尖在关节配置下的可达工作空间，并分析指尖对之间的空间关系以识别可行夹持构型。对四种不同运动学结构的手部进行的实验分析表明，该框架可仅依据运动学结构量化夹持能力，从而支持不同设计方案的直接比较，无需物体几何或力模型。
FingerEye: Continuous and Unified Vision-Tactile Sensing for Dexterous Manipulation
Authors: Zhixuan Xu, Yichen Li, Xuanye Wu, Tianyu Qiu, Lin Shao
First: 2026-04-22T15:37:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:37:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Dexterous robotic manipulation requires comprehensive perception across all phases of interaction: pre-contact, contact initiation, and post-contact. Such continuous feedback allows a robot to adapt its actions throughout interaction. However, many existing tactile sensors, such as GelSight and its variants, only provide feedback after contact is established, limiting a robot's ability to precisely initiate contact. We introduce FingerEye, a compact and cost-effective sensor that provides continuous vision-tactile feedback throughout the interaction process. FingerEye integrates binocular RGB cameras to provide close-range visual perception with implicit stereo depth. Upon contact, external forces and torques deform a compliant ring structure; these deformations are captured via marker-based pose estimation and serve as a proxy for contact wrench sensing. This design enables a perception stream that smoothly transitions from pre-contact visual cues to post-contact tactile feedback. Building on this sensing capability, we develop a vision-tactile imitation learning policy that fuses signals from multiple FingerEye sensors to learn dexterous manipulation behaviors from limited real-world data. We further develop a digital twin of our sensor and robot platform to improve policy generalization. By combining real demonstrations with visually augmented simulated observations for representation learning, the learned policies become more robust to object appearance variations. Together, these design aspects enable dexterous manipulation across diverse object properties and interaction regimes, including coin standing, chip picking, letter retrieving, and syringe manipulation. The hardware design, code, appendix, and videos are available on our project website: https://nus-lins-lab.github.io/FingerEyeWeb/
中文标题/摘要
标题：FingerEye：用于灵巧操作的连续统一视觉-触觉感知
灵巧的机器人操作需要在交互全过程（接触前、接触起始和接触后）实现全面感知。这种连续反馈使机器人能在交互过程中调整其动作。然而，许多现有触觉传感器（如GelSight及其变体）仅在接触建立后提供反馈，限制了机器人精确启动接触的能力。我们提出了FingerEye，一种紧凑且低成本的传感器，可在整个交互过程中提供连续的视觉-触觉反馈。FingerEye集成双目RGB相机，提供近距离视觉感知及隐式立体深度。接触时，外力和扭矩会变形柔性环结构；这些变形通过基于标记的姿态估计捕获，作为接触力矩传感的代理。该设计使感知流从接触前视觉线索平滑过渡到接触后触觉反馈。基于此感知能力，我们开发了一种视觉-触觉模仿学习策略，融合多个FingerEye传感器信号，从有限真实数据中学习灵巧操作行为。我们进一步构建了传感器与机器人平台的数字孪生体，以提升策略泛化能力。通过结合真实演示与视觉增强的模拟观测进行表征学习，所学策略对物体外观变化更具鲁棒性。这些设计共同支持机器人在多样物体属性和交互模式下实现灵巧操作，包括硬币直立、芯片拾取、字母提取和注射器操作。硬件设计、代码、附录和视频可在项目网站获取：https://nus-lins-lab.github.io/FingerEyeWeb/
Summary / 总结
To enable dexterous robotic manipulation, the paper addresses the limitation of existing tactile sensors that only provide feedback after contact, hindering precise contact initiation. FingerEye introduces a compact, cost-effective sensor combining binocular RGB cameras for visual depth and a deformable ring structure that captures contact wrench via marker-based pose estimation, enabling continuous vision-tactile feedback across pre-contact, contact, and post-contact phases. The system supports a vision-tactile imitation learning policy that fuses multi-sensor data to learn manipulation behaviors from limited real-world data, enhanced by a digital twin for improved generalization. Experimental results show robust performance across diverse tasks like coin standing, chip picking, letter retrieval, and syringe manipulation, demonstrating adaptability to varying object properties and interaction regimes.
为实现机器人在接触前、接触中及接触后各阶段的灵巧操作，作者提出FingerEye传感器，它通过双目RGB相机提供视觉深度感知，并利用可变形环结构配合标记点姿态估计，实现接触力矩的连续感知。该设计使视觉与触觉感知无缝衔接。基于多传感器数据，团队训练了融合视觉与触觉信号的模仿学习策略，并通过数字孪生平台结合真实演示与视觉增强仿真数据提升策略泛化能力。实验表明，该系统在硬币立起、芯片拾取、字母抓取及注射器操作等多样化任务中表现稳健，适应不同物体属性与交互模式。
Task-Dependent Evaluation of LLM Output Homogenization: A Taxonomy-Guided Framework
Authors: Shomik Jain, Jack Lanchantin, Maximilian Nickel, Candace Ross, Karen Ullrich, Ashia Wilson, Jamelle Watson-Daniels
First: 2025-09-25T14:58:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:37:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models often generate homogeneous outputs, but whether this is problematic depends on the specific task. For objective math tasks, responses may vary in terms of problem-solving strategy but should maintain the same verifiable answer. Whereas, for creative writing tasks, we often expect variation in key narrative components (e.g. plot, setting, etc.) beyond mere vocabulary diversity. Prior work on homogenization rarely conceptualizes diversity in a task-dependent way. We address this gap with four contributions: (1) a task taxonomy with distinct notions of functional diversity -- whether a user would perceive two responses as meaningfully different for a given task; (2) a small user study validating that the taxonomy aligns with human perception of functional diversity; (3) a task-dependent sampling technique that increases diversity only where homogenization is undesired; (4) evidence challenging the perceived diversity-quality trade-off, showing it may stem from mis-conceptualizing both diversity and quality in a task-agnostic way.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于任务依赖的大型语言模型输出同质化评估：一种基于分类框架的方法
大型语言模型常产生同质化输出，但其是否存在问题取决于具体任务。对于客观数学任务，不同响应可能在解题策略上存在差异，但应保持相同的可验证答案；而对于创意写作任务，我们通常期望关键叙事元素（如情节、背景等）的多样性，而不仅仅是词汇的多样性。此前关于同质化的研究很少从任务依赖角度概念化多样性。我们通过四项贡献填补这一空白：（1）一个任务分类体系，包含功能多样性的不同概念——即用户是否会在特定任务中感知两个响应为有意义的不同；（2）一项小型用户研究，验证该分类体系与人类对功能多样性的感知一致；（3）一种任务依赖的采样技术，仅在同质化不被期望时增加多样性；（4）证据挑战对多样性与质量权衡的普遍认知，表明该权衡可能源于对多样性与质量在任务无关方式下的错误概念化。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the issue that large language models often produce homogeneous outputs, but whether this is problematic varies by task: for math problems, diverse strategies are acceptable as long as answers are correct, while for creative writing, narrative variation is essential. The authors propose a task taxonomy that defines functional diversity based on user perception, validate it through a small user study, and introduce a task-dependent sampling method to selectively increase diversity where it matters. Experimental results show that homogenization is not inherently bad and that the perceived trade-off between diversity and quality may arise from task-agnostic definitions of both concepts.
该论文指出大语言模型输出常趋于同质化，但其是否为问题取决于具体任务：数学题允许策略多样但答案一致，而创意写作则需叙事元素的差异。作者提出一种任务分类法，从用户感知角度定义功能多样性，并通过小规模用户研究验证其有效性，进而设计任务导向的采样方法，仅在需多样性时增强输出差异。实验表明，同质化并非总是负面，所谓多样性与质量的权衡可能源于对二者缺乏任务语境的定义。
Storm Surge Modeling, Bias Correction, Graph Neural Networks, Graph Convolution Networks
Authors: Noujoud Nader, Stefanos Giaremis, Clint Dawson, Carola Kaiser, Karame Mohammadiporshokooh, Hartmut Kaiser
First: 2026-04-22T15:36:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:36:19+00:00
Comments: 51 pages, 9 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Storm surge forecasting remains a critical challenge in mitigating the impacts of tropical cyclones on coastal regions, particularly given recent trends of rapid intensification and increasing nearshore storm activity. Traditional high fidelity numerical models such as ADCIRC, while robust, are often hindered by inevitable uncertainties arising from various sources. To address these challenges, this study introduces StormNet, a spatio-temporal graph neural network (GNN) designed for bias correction of storm surge forecasts. StormNet integrates graph convolutional (GCN) and graph attention (GAT) mechanisms with long short-term memory (LSTM) components to capture complex spatial and temporal dependencies among water-level gauge stations. The model was trained using historical hurricane data from the U.S. Gulf Coast and evaluated on Hurricane Idalia (2023). Results demonstrate that StormNet can effectively reduce the root mean square error (RMSE) in water-level predictions by more than 70\% for 48-hour forecasts and above 50\% for 72-hour forecasts, as well as outperform a sequential LSTM baseline, particularly for longer prediction horizons. The model also exhibits low training time, enhancing its applicability in real-time operational forecasting systems. Overall, StormNet provides a computationally efficient and physically meaningful framework for improving storm surge prediction accuracy and reliability during extreme weather events.
中文标题/摘要
标题：风暴潮建模、偏差校正、图神经网络、图卷积网络
风暴潮预报在减轻热带气旋对沿海地区影响方面仍是一项关键挑战，尤其考虑到近期快速增强和近岸风暴活动增加的趋势。尽管传统高保真数值模型（如ADCIRC）稳健可靠，但其仍受多种来源固有不确定性的限制。为应对这些挑战，本研究提出了StormNet，一种用于风暴潮预报偏差校正的时空图神经网络（GNN）。StormNet结合图卷积（GCN）和图注意力（GAT）机制与长短期记忆（LSTM）组件，以捕捉水位观测站之间复杂的时空依赖关系。该模型使用美国墨西哥湾历史飓风数据进行训练，并在2023年飓风Idalia上进行评估。结果表明，StormNet可将48小时预报的水位预测均方根误差（RMSE）降低超过70%，72小时预报降低超过50%，且优于序列LSTM基线模型，尤其在更长预测周期中表现更优。该模型训练时间短，增强了其在实时业务预报系统中的适用性。总体而言，StormNet提供了一种计算高效且具有物理意义的框架，以提升极端天气事件期间风暴潮预测的准确性和可靠性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve storm surge forecasting amid increasing tropical cyclone intensity and coastal vulnerability, this study proposes StormNet, a spatio-temporal graph neural network combining graph convolution, graph attention, and LSTM modules to correct biases in numerical forecasts. Trained on historical U.S. Gulf Coast hurricane data and tested on Hurricane Idalia (2023), StormNet significantly reduces root mean square error by over 70% for 48-hour and over 50% for 72-hour predictions, outperforming a sequential LSTM baseline, especially at longer horizons. Its low training time supports real-time operational use, offering a computationally efficient and physically grounded approach to enhancing surge prediction accuracy during extreme events.
为应对热带气旋强度增强和沿海地区脆弱性上升带来的风暴潮预测挑战，本研究提出StormNet，一种结合图卷积与注意力机制的时空图神经网络，通过LSTM层捕捉水位观测站间的空间-时间依赖关系。该模型基于美国墨西哥湾历史飓风数据训练，并在2023年飓风Idalia上验证，48小时预报的均方根误差降低超70%，72小时预报降低超50%，显著优于序列LSTM基线，尤其在长时预测中表现更优。其训练速度快，适合实时业务化应用，提供了一种计算高效且物理意义明确的风暴潮预测改进框架。
Kinematic Optimization of Phalanx Length Ratios in Robotic Hands Using Potential Dexterity
Authors: HyoJae Kang, Joonho Lee, Jeongdo Ahn, Dong Il Park
First: 2026-04-22T15:34:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-22T15:34:33+00:00
Comments: This manuscript has been submitted for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In the design stage of robotic hands, it is not straightforward to quantitatively evaluate the effect of phalanx length ratios on dexterity without defining specific objects or manipulation tasks. Therefore, this study presents a framework for optimizing the phalanx length ratios of a five-finger robotic hand based on potential dexterity within a kinematic structure. The proposed method employs global manipulability, workspace volume, overlap workspace volume, and fingertip sensitivity as evaluation metrics, and identifies optimal design configurations using a weighted objective function under given constraints. The reachable workspace is discretized using a voxel-based representation, and joint motions are discretized at uniform intervals for evaluation. The optimization is performed over design sets for both the thumb and the other fingers, and design combinations that do not generate overlap workspace are excluded. The results show that each phalanx does not contribute equally to the overall dexterity, and the factors influencing each phalanx are identified. In addition, it is observed that the selection of weighting coefficients does not necessarily lead to the direct maximization of individual performance metrics, due to the non-uniform distribution of evaluation measures within the design space. The proposed framework provides a systematic approach to analyze the trade-offs among reachability, dexterity, and controllability, and can serve as a practical guideline for the kinematic design of multi-fingered robotic hands.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于潜在灵巧度的机器人手指节长度比运动学优化
在机器人手设计阶段，若未定义具体对象或操作任务，难以定量评估指节长度比对灵巧度的影响。本文提出一种基于潜在灵巧度的五指机器人手指节长度比优化框架。该方法采用全局可操作性、工作空间体积、重叠工作空间体积及指尖灵敏度作为评价指标，通过加权目标函数在给定约束下识别最优设计配置。采用体素化表示离散化可达工作空间，关节运动以均匀间隔离散化进行评估。优化分别针对拇指与其他手指的设计集合进行，排除产生重叠工作空间的设计组合。结果表明，各指节对整体灵巧度贡献不均，且各指节影响因素得以识别。此外，由于评价指标在设计空间内分布不均，权重系数的选择未必直接最大化各单项性能指标。该框架提供了一种系统方法，用于分析可达性、灵巧性与可控性之间的权衡，可作为多指机器人手运动学设计的实用指导。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to quantitatively assess how phalanx length ratios affect robotic hand dexterity without task-specific assumptions, this study proposes a kinematic optimization framework using potential dexterity. The method evaluates design configurations via global manipulability, workspace volume, overlap volume, and fingertip sensitivity, combined into a weighted objective function under physical constraints. Workspace is discretized voxel-wise and joint motions are sampled uniformly for evaluation, with non-overlapping workspace combinations excluded. Results reveal that phalanges contribute unequally to dexterity, and weighting coefficients do not directly maximize individual metrics due to uneven distribution across the design space, offering a systematic approach to balance reachability, dexterity, and controllability in multi-fingered robotic hand design.
为在不依赖特定任务前提下定量评估指节长度比对机械手灵巧性的影响，本研究提出基于潜在灵巧性的运动学优化框架。该方法通过全局可操作性、工作空间体积、重叠工作空间体积和指尖灵敏度四个指标构建加权目标函数，在约束条件下优化拇指及其他手指的指节长度组合。工作空间采用体素离散化，关节运动以均匀间隔采样评估，排除产生工作空间重叠的组合。结果表明，各指节对灵巧性的贡献不均，不同指节受不同因素影响，且权重系数无法直接最大化单个指标，因评价指标在设计空间内分布不均。该框架为平衡可达性、灵巧性与可控性提供了系统方法，可指导多指机械手的运动学设计。
